Real sentences from the [africa-corpus](https://github.com/AfriSpeech/africa-corpus-builder) dataset, segmented by africa-g2p into native-orthography phonemes (default) with the IPA shown for reference. 198 languages.

<details>
<summary><b>Acholi</b> (<code>ach</code> · SS)</summary>

> **Text:** ci ajwaka biciko ni gilwok jami ma two tye iye-ni, ci bimedo tweyo wiye pi nino abiro mukene.  
> **Phonemes:** `c i a j w a k a b i c i k o n i g i l w o k j a m i m a t w o t y e i y e-n i, c i b i m e d o t w e y o w i y e p i n i n o a b i r o m u k e n e.`  
> **IPA:** `ʧ i a ʤ w a k a b i ʧ i k o n i ɡ i l w o k ʤ a m i m a t w o t j e i j e-n i, ʧ i b i m e d o t w e j o w i j e p i n i n o a b i r o m u k e n e.`

> **Text:** Pi meno kadi wa gicikke mukwoŋo ca gimoko ki remo.  
> **Phonemes:** `p i m e n o k a d i w a g i c i k k e m u k w o ŋ o c a g i m o k o k i r e m o.`  
> **IPA:** `p i m e n o k a d i w a ɡ i ʧ i k k e m u k w o ŋ o ʧ a ɡ i m o k o k i r e m o.`

</details>

> **Text:** Tasi ɛm a, Nyam okm ereb agŋ ɛm.

> **IPA:** `t a s i ɛ m a, ɲ a m o k m e r e b a ɡ ŋ ɛ m.`

> **Text:** Lɛgŋ a cɛ, Jesu anŋ akŋ a ɛy ↄkm ɛtŋ im sig es mij saw eke ki yɛgm agŋ low.  
> **Phonemes:** `l ɛ g ŋ a c ɛ, j e s u a n ŋ a k ŋ a ɛ y ↄ k m ɛ t ŋ i m s i g e s m i j s a w e k e k i y ɛ g m a g ŋ l o w.`  
> **IPA:** `l ɛ ɡ ŋ a tʃ ɛ, dz e s u a n ŋ a k ŋ a ɛ j ↄ k m ɛ t ŋ i m s i ɡ e s m i dz s a w e k e k i j ɛ ɡ m a ɡ ŋ l o w.`

</details>

<summary><b>Afrikaans</b> (<code>afr</code> · BW)</summary>

> **Text:** en ek het gesien dat die hele land vol dorings is, dit is vol bossies, en die klipmuur het omgeval.  
> **Phonemes:** `e n e k h e t g e s ie n d a t d ie h e l e l a n d v o l d o r i ng s i s, d i t i s v o l b o s s ie s, e n d ie k l i p m uu r h e t o m g e v a l.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ ŋ ɛ k ɦ ɛ t χ ɛ s i ŋ d ɐ t d i ɦ ɛ l ɛ l ɐ ŋ d f ɔ l d ɔ r ə ŋ s ə s, d ə t ə s f ɔ l b ɔ s s i s, ɛ ŋ d i k l ə p m y r ɦ ɛ t ɔ m χ ɛ f ɐ l.`

> **Text:** Vuur moet gedurigdeur op die altaar aan die brand gehou word; dit mag nie doodgaan nie.  
> **Phonemes:** `v uu r m oe t g e d u r i g d eu r o p d ie a l t aa r aa n d ie b r a n d g e h ou w o r d; d i t m a g n ie d oo d g aa n n ie.`  
> **IPA:** `f y r m u t χ ɛ d œ r ə χ d eø r ɔ p d i ɐ l t ɑː r ɑː ŋ d i b r ɐ ŋ d χ ɛ ɦ œu w ɔ r d; d ə t m ɐ χ ŋ i d oə d χ ɑː ŋ ŋ i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Akan</b> (<code>aka</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɛno enti na apam a ɛdi ɛkan no wɔnam mogya so na wɔhyɛɛ no den no.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ n o e n t i n a a p a m a ɛ d i ɛ k a n n o w ɔ n a m m o gy a s o n a w ɔ hy ɛ ɛ n o d e n n o.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ n o e n tʰ ɪ n a a pʰ a m a ɛ d ɪ ɛ kʰ a n n o w ɔ n a m m o dʑ a s o n a w ɔ ç ɛ ɛ n o d e n n o.`

> **Text:** Ɛkorɔn sene ɔsoro, ɛdeɛn na wobɛtumi ayɛ? Emu dɔ sene damena ase tɔnn, ɛdeɛn na wobɛtumi ahunu?.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ k o r ɔ n s e n e ɔ s o r o, ɛ d eɛ n n a w o b ɛ t u m i a y ɛ? e m u d ɔ s e n e d a m e n a a s e t ɔ n n, ɛ d eɛ n n a w o b ɛ t u m i a h u n u?.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ kʰ o ɾ ɔ n s e n e ɔ s o ɾ o, ɛ d eɛ n n a w o b ɛ tʰ u m ɪ a j ɛ? e m u d ɔ s e n e d a m e n a a s e tʰ ɔ n n, ɛ d eɛ n n a w o b ɛ tʰ u m ɪ a h u n u?.`

</details>

> **Text:** Ma nia ipenj pi kier para, Man iyeny dubo para.

> **Text:** Ka kumeno dong kawono Farao yeny ngatu ma pidoie cu ma riek de, man ekethe wi ngom Misiri.  
> **Phonemes:** `k a k u m e n o d o ng k a w o n o f a r a o y e ny ng a t u m a p i d o i e c u m a r i e k d e, m a n e k e th e w i ng o m m i s i r i.`  
> **IPA:** `k a̘ k u̘ m e̘ n o̘ d o̘ ŋɡ k a̘ w o̘ n o̘ f a̘ r a̘ o̘ j e̘ ɲ ŋɡ a̘ t u̘ m a̘ p i̘ d o̘ i̘ e̘ c u̘ m a̘ r i̘ e̘ k d e̘, m a̘ n e̘ k e̘ t̟ e̘ w i̘ ŋɡ o̘ m m i̘ s i̘ r i̘.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Anyin</b> (<code>any</code> · CI)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɔ sanlɩn kɛ, ninnge ngacile ngacile'n mɔ ɔ lɛ kan yɩ́ nwʋn ɛjɔlɛ ɛ kele yɛ́'n, yɛ kulo kɛ ɛ tu yɩ́ bʋ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɔ s a n l ɩ n k ɛ, n i n ng e ng a c i l e ng a c i l e ' n m ɔ ɔ l ɛ k a n y ɩ́ n w ʋ n ɛ j ɔ l ɛ ɛ k e l e y ɛ́ ' n, y ɛ k u l o k ɛ ɛ t u y ɩ́ b ʋ.`  
> **IPA:** `ɔ s ɐ n l ɪ n k ɛ, n i n ŋ e ŋ ɐ ʧ i l e ŋ ɐ ʧ i l e Ɂ n m ɔ ɔ l ɛ k ɐ n j ɪ n w ʊ n ɛ ʤ ɔ l ɛ ɛ k e l e j ɛ Ɂ n, j ɛ k u l o k ɛ ɛ t u j ɪ b ʊ.`

> **Text:** Ɔ sanlɩn kɛ, Mân Baa'n walɩ kɛ ɔ 'ba kpʋnndɛ bɛ́ mɔ b'a minlin'n nán ɔ de bɛ́ ngʋan.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɔ s a n l ɩ n k ɛ, m â n b a a ' n w a l ɩ k ɛ ɔ 'b a kp ʋ n n d ɛ b ɛ́ m ɔ b ' a m i n l i n ' n n á n ɔ d e b ɛ́ ng ʋ a n.`  
> **IPA:** `ɔ s ɐ n l ɪ n k ɛ, m ɐ n b ɐ ɐ Ɂ n w ɐ l ɪ k ɛ ɔ 'b ɐ k͡p ʊ n n d ɛ b ɛ m ɔ b Ɂ ɐ m i n l i n Ɂ n n ɐ n ɔ d e b ɛ ŋ ʊ ɐ n.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Arabic (Chadian)</b> (<code>shu</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** وَ سِلْسِلَتَيْن هَنَا دَهَبْ صَافِي مَفْتُولِينْ مِثِلْ الْحَبِلْ. وَ عَلِّقْهُمْ فِي الْحَلَقَتَيْن.  
> **Phonemes:** `وَ سِلْسِلَتَيْن هَنَا دَهَبْ صَا فِي مَفْتُو لِي نْ مِثِلْ ا لْحَبِلْ. وَ عَلِّقْهُمْ فِي ا لْحَلَقَتَيْن.`  
> **IPA:** `wَ sِlْsِlَtَjْn hَnَ∅ dَhَbْ sَ∅ fِj mَfْtُw lِj nْ mِtِlْ ∅ lْhَbِlْ. wَ ∅َlِّɡْhُmْ fِj ∅ lْhَlَɡَtَjْn.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Arabic (Moroccan)</b> (<code>ary</code> · DZ)</summary>

> **Text:** يَسوع قالّوم: أنتُمَ من تحت، أمّا أنا من فوق. أنتُمَ من هَد الدنيا، أمّا أنا ماشي من هَد الدنيا.  
> **Phonemes:** `يَس و ع ق ا لّو م: أ ن تُمَ م ن ت ح ت، أ مّا أ ن ا م ن ف و ق. أ ن تُمَ م ن هَد ا ل د ن ي ا، أ مّا أ ن ا م ا ش ي م ن هَد ا ل د ن ي ا.`  
> **IPA:** `jَs w ʕ q ʔ lّw m: ʔ n tُmَ m n t ħ t، ʔ mّʔ ʔ n ʔ m n f w q. ʔ n tُmَ m n hَd ʔ l d n j ʔ، ʔ mّʔ ʔ n ʔ m ʔ ʃ j m n hَd ʔ l d n j ʔ.`

> **Text:** وْكَانْ كُلَّ سْبْتْ كَيْتّْنَاقْشْ مْعَ لِيهُودْ وْالْيُونَانِيِّينْ فْدَارْ الصّْلَاة وْكَيْحَاوْلْ يْقْنَعْهُمْ.  
> **Phonemes:** `وْكَا نْ كُلَّ سْبْتْ كَيْتّْنَا قْشْ مْعَ لِي هُو دْ وْا لْيُو نَا نِيِّي نْ فْدَا رْ ا ل صّْلَا ة وْكَيْحَا وْلْ يْقْنَعْهُمْ.`  
> **IPA:** `wْkَʔ nْ kُlَّ sْbْtْ kَjْtّْnَʔ qْʃْ mْʕَ lِj hُw dْ wْʔ lْjُw nَʔ nِjِّj nْ fْdَʔ rْ ʔ l sˁّْlَʔ ɛ wْkَjْħَʔ wْlْ jْqْnَʕْhُmْ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Aringa</b> (<code>luc</code> · SS)</summary>

> **Text:** Wó ãnãkpá ãzíla ãko ãmaní upalé táwụ̃nị̃ agâlé rĩ kí ãma awa kí ãma drĩdríŋĩ gá rá.  
> **Phonemes:** `w ó ã n ã kp á ã z í l a ã k o ã m a n í u p a l é t á w ụ̃ n ị̃ a g â l é r ĩ k í ã m a a w a k í ã m a dr ĩ dr í ŋ ĩ g á r á.`  
> **IPA:** `w ɔ a n a kp a a z ɪ l a a k ɔ a m a n ɪ ʊ p a l ɛ t a w ʊ n ɪ a ɡ a l ɛ r ɪ k ɪ a m a a w a k ɪ a m a ɖ ɪ ɖ ɪ ŋ ɪ ɡ a r a.`

> **Text:** Wó ãko pírí mádrị́ ụ̃rọ̃drị́ ru mání ándrá trũ adrujó rĩ kí álã kí ãko ãzí ụ̃rọ̃drị́ kóru la rú Kúrísĩtõ ꞌbã ị́jọ́ sĩ.  
> **Phonemes:** `w ó ã k o p í r í m á dr ị́ ụ̃ r ọ̃ dr ị́ r u m á n í á ndr á tr ũ a dr u j ó r ĩ k í á l ã k í ã k o ã z í ụ̃ r ọ̃ dr ị́ k ó r u l a r ú k ú r í s ĩ t õ ꞌ b ã ị́ j ọ́ s ĩ.`  
> **IPA:** `w ɔ a k ɔ p ɪ r ɪ m a ɖ ɪ ʊ r ɔ ɖ ɪ r ʊ m a n ɪ a ᶯɖ a ʈ ʊ a ɖ ʊ dz ɔ r ɪ k ɪ a l a k ɪ a k ɔ a z ɪ ʊ r ɔ ɖ ɪ k ɔ r ʊ l a r ʊ k ʊ r ɪ s ɪ t ɔ ꞌ b a ɪ dz ɔ s ɪ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ateso</b> (<code>teo</code> · KE)</summary>

> **Text:** naarai mam adiakar kiyait yes, naarai mere ikisila kiyaito yes, dimarai asianut.  
> **Phonemes:** `n a a r ai m a m a d i a k a r k i y ai t y e s, n a a r ai m e r e i k i s i l a k i y ai t o y e s, d i m a r ai a s i a n u t.`

> **Text:** Kitoik akit, ijo akuj, kener; Akwap da kopup akiro nuka aitukuka:.  
> **Phonemes:** `k i t oi k a k i t, i j o a k u j, k e n e r; a k w a p d a k o p u p a k i r o n u k a ai t u k u k a:.`  
> **IPA:** `k i t oi̯ k a k i t, i dʒ o a k u dʒ, k e n e r; a k w a p d a k o p u p a k i r o n u k a ai̯ t u k u k a:.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bamunka</b> (<code>bvm</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Mbʉ woke bə̀ŋhə tʉ ɔŋ mbɔ, Mɔ kie woləŋ fɛ. Jiso bə̀ŋhə mbɔ, A nɔ gìe i kʉ̀ʼnə ndə məghɔ̀ŋ mbɔ, Mɔ kie woləŋ fɛ.  
> **Phonemes:** `m b ʉ w o k e b ə̀ ŋ h ə t ʉ ɔ ŋ m b ɔ, m ɔ k i e w o l ə ŋ f ɛ. j i s o b ə̀ ŋ h ə m b ɔ, a n ɔ g ì e i k ʉ ' n ə n d ə m ə gh ɔ̀ ŋ m b ɔ, m ɔ k i e w o l ə ŋ f ɛ.`  
> **IPA:** `m b ʉ w o k e b ə ŋ ʔ ə t ʉ ɔ ŋ m b ɔ, m ɔ k i e w o l ə ŋ f ɛ. d͡ʒ i s o b ə ŋ ʔ ə m b ɔ, a n ɔ ɡ i e i k ʉ ' n ə n d ə m ə ɣ ɔ ŋ m b ɔ, m ɔ k i e w o l ə ŋ f ɛ.`

> **Text:** Kʉ̂ʉ bɛ haʼ yɔ̂ŋ-ghɔ̄, Jiso fyú hə mu njaa gɛ̀ ŋgwuu taanə-kəghəŋ-kə.  
> **Phonemes:** `k ʉ ʉ b ɛ h a ' y ɔ̂ ŋ-gh ɔ̄, j i s o f y ú h ə m u n j a a g ɛ̀ ŋ g w u u t a a n ə-k ə gh ə ŋ-k ə.`  
> **IPA:** `k ʉ ʉ b ɛ ʔ a ' j ɔ ŋ-ɣ ɔ, d͡ʒ i s o f j u ʔ ə m u n d͡ʒ a a ɡ ɛ ŋ ɡ w u u t a a n ə-k ə ɣ ə ŋ-k ə.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bandial</b> (<code>bqj</code> · SN)</summary>

> **Text:** Naagil : « Bu nakane ? » Nguogol : « Umu to gásumay. Fubambaŋ, bájurol Raĉel umua ájaeul me nekoreol. ».  
> **Phonemes:** `n a a g i l : « b u n a k a n e ? » ng u o g o l : « u m u t o g á s u m a y. f u b a mb a ŋ, b á j u r o l r a ĉ e l u m u a á j a e u l m e n e k o r e o l. ».`  
> **IPA:** `n a a ɡ ɪ l : « b ʊ n a k a n ɛ ? » ᵑɡ ʊ ɔ ɡ ɔ l : « ʊ m ʊ t ɔ ɡ a s ʊ m a j. f ʊ b a ᵐb a ŋ, b a ɟ ʊ ɾ ɔ l ɾ a c ɛ l ʊ m ʊ a a ɟ a ɛ ʊ l m ɛ n ɛ k ɔ ɾ ɛ ɔ l. ».`

> **Text:** Mbiban, aĉila faŋaol nákail ayab gayoŋ. Min ásilen bi nettam ñono futoh ni ñúuba bi etoh bo atiol.  
> **Phonemes:** `mb i b a n, a ĉ i l a f a ŋ a o l n á k a i l a y a b g a y o ŋ. m i n á s i l e n b i n e t t a m ñ o n o f u t o h n i ñ ú u b a b i e t o h b o a t i o l.`  
> **IPA:** `ᵐb ɪ b a n, a c ɪ l a f a ŋ a ɔ l n a k a ɪ l a j a b ɡ a j ɔ ŋ. m ɪ n a s ɪ l ɛ n b ɪ n ɛ t t a m n ɔ n ɔ f ʊ t ɔ h n ɪ n ʊ ʊ b a b ɪ ɛ t ɔ h b ɔ a t ɪ ɔ l.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bangi</b> (<code>bni</code> · CD)</summary>

> **Text:** nde bayike ba miboso bakozala mimbisa, mpe mimbisa miboso.  
> **Phonemes:** `nd e b a y i k e b a m i b o s o b a k o z a l a m i mb i s a, mp e m i mb i s a m i b o s o.`  
> **IPA:** `ⁿd e c a j i k e c a m i c o s o c a k o d͡z a l a m i ᵐb i s a, ᵐp e m i ᵐb i s a m i c o s o.`

> **Text:** mpe alobi ete, Onga mwanombe na? Bancakolaka. Lokoyisa nokula etuti o ndako e sango e yo.  
> **Phonemes:** `mp e a l o b i e t e, o ng a mw a n o mb e n a? b a nc a k o l a k a. l o k o y i s a n o k u l a e t u t i o nd a k o e s a ng o e y o.`  
> **IPA:** `ᵐp e a l o c i e t e, o ᵑɡ a mʷ a n o ᵐb e n a? c a ⁿt͡s a k o l a k a. l o k o j i s a n o k u l a e t u t i o ⁿd a k o e s a ᵑɡ o e j o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Baoulé</b> (<code>bci</code> · CI)</summary>

> **Text:** afin nán Krist sulafilɛ ngunmin yɛ Nyanmiɛn man amun lafi-ɔ, sanngɛ ɔ man amun wun nyrɛnnɛn i dunman nun ekun.  
> **Phonemes:** `a f in n á n k r i s t s u l a f i l ɛ n g un m in y ɛ ny an m i ɛn m an a m un l a f i-ɔ, s an n g ɛ ɔ m an a m un w un ny r ɛn n ɛn i d un m an n un e k un.`  
> **IPA:** `a f ĩ n a n k r i s t s u l a f i l ɛ n ɡ ũ m ĩ j ɛ ɲ ã m i ɛ̃ m ã a m ũ l a f i-ɔ, s ã n ɡ ɛ ɔ m ã a m ũ w ũ ɲ r ɛ̃ n ɛ̃ i d ũ m ã n ũ e k ũ.`

> **Text:** Bé wa sé é ke, ange wié'm bé yi bé wun nglô kléé bé, bé sé bé ke wa tyen.  
> **Phonemes:** `b é w a s é é k e, an g e w i é ' m b é y i b é w un n g l ô k l é é b é, b é s é b é k e w a t y e n.`  
> **IPA:** `b e w a s e e k e, ã ɡ e w i e ' m b e j i b e w ũ n ɡ l o k l e e b e, b e s e b e k e w a t j e n.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bari</b> (<code>bfa</code> · SS)</summary>

> **Text:** anyen se böŋö wukiŋdya rubaŋgajin lo tutulyöŋgu ŋun lo ki, anyen kwakwasakiŋdya nan mor ko ŋwajik kwe anyen si da bura.  
> **Phonemes:** `a ny e n s e b ö ŋ ö w u k i ŋ d y a r u b a ŋ g a j i n l o t u t u l y ö ŋ g u ŋ u n l o k i, a ny e n kw a kw a s a k i ŋ d y a n a n m o r k o ŋ w a j i k kw e a ny e n s i d a b u r a.`  
> **IPA:** `a ɲ e n s e b o ŋ o w u k i ŋ d j a r u b a ŋ ɡ a d͡ʒ i n l o t u t u l j o ŋ ɡ u ŋ u n l o k i, a ɲ e n kʷ a kʷ a s a k i ŋ d j a n a n m o r k o ŋ w a d͡ʒ i k kʷ e a ɲ e n s i d a b u r a.`

> **Text:** a si da inot mo kobbi kobbu lwölwöŋ tuparan kiden; a mudwe mo gwe gwoso ko bure.  
> **Phonemes:** `a s i d a i n o t m o k o b b i k o b b u l w ö l w ö ŋ t u p a r a n k i d e n; a m u d w e m o g w e g w o s o k o b u r e.`  
> **IPA:** `a s i d a i n o t m o k o b b i k o b b u l w o l w o ŋ t u p a r a n k i d e n; a m u d w e m o ɡ w e ɡ w o s o k o b u r e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bariba</b> (<code>bba</code> · BJ)</summary>

> **Text:** Mɑ u nim bɑnisi ɡuru winu sɔɔ. Kɑ mɛ, ɡuru wii ni, nu ku rɑ ɡɛ̃ɛre nim bunum sɔ̃.  
> **Phonemes:** `m ɑ u n i m b ɑ n i s i g u r u w i n u s ɔ ɔ. k ɑ m ɛ, g u r u w i i n i, n u k u r ɑ g ɛ̃ ɛ r e n i m b u n u m s ɔ̃.`  
> **IPA:** `m ɑ u n i m b ɑ n i s i ɡ u r u w i n u s ɔ ɔ. k ɑ m ɛ, ɡ u r u w i i n i, n u k u r ɑ ɡ ɛ ɛ r e n i m b u n u m s ɔ.`

> **Text:** i Isirelibɑ sɔ̃ɔwɔ i nɛɛ, ben ɡoo ù n kentu kpikɑ mɔ, u kuɑwɑ mi disiɡii.  
> **Phonemes:** `i i s i r e l i b ɑ s ɔ̃ ɔ w ɔ i n ɛ ɛ, b e n g o o ù n k e n t u kp i k ɑ m ɔ, u k u ɑ w ɑ m i d i s i g i i.`  
> **IPA:** `i i s i r e l i b ɑ s ɔ ɔ w ɔ i n ɛ ɛ, b e n ɡ o o u n k e n t u k͡p i k ɑ m ɔ, u k u ɑ w ɑ m i d i s i ɡ i i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bassa</b> (<code>bsq</code> · Liberia)</summary>

> **Text:** Sɔ̀ìn ɓóɛ gboɛɛ, nìí, sáa-zà-nyɔ̀ɔ̀ mɛ zàìn gbúɛ́ ɓě mɔɔ póo-póo ɓě ɓɛ́ nyɔɔ̀ ɓɛ́ìn dyéɛ kɛɛ sáa.  
> **Phonemes:** `s ɔ̀ ì n ɓ ó ɛ gb o ɛ ɛ, n ì í, s á a-z à-ny ɔ̀ ɔ̀ m ɛ z à ì n gb ú ɛ́ ɓ ě m ɔ ɔ p ó o-p ó o ɓ ě ɓ ɛ́ ny ɔ ɔ̀ ɓ ɛ́ ì n d y é ɛ k ɛ ɛ s á a.`  
> **IPA:** `s ɔ˧˩ i˧˩ n ɓ o˥ ɛ ɡ͡b o ɛ ɛ, n i˧˩ i˥, s a˥ a-z a˧˩-ɲ ɔ˧˩ ɔ˧˩ m ɛ z a˧˩ i˧˩ n ɡ͡b u˥ ɛ˥ ɓ e˩˥ m ɔ ɔ p o˥ o-p o˥ o ɓ e˩˥ ɓ ɛ˥ ɲ ɔ ɔ˧˩ ɓ ɛ˥ i˧˩ n d y e˥ ɛ k ɛ ɛ s a˥ a.`

> **Text:** Ḿ ɖá nyɛɔ̀ mɛ nì wãuń ɓó sáa-kɔ̃-zàìn-gbààɔ̀ kɔ̃ kánáá pū. Ɔ mɛ ɓɔ́ mɔ́ɔ fɔɔ cá.  
> **Phonemes:** `ḿ ɖ á ny ɛ ɔ̀ m ɛ n ì w ã u ń ɓ ó s á a-k ɔ̃-z à ì n-gb à à ɔ̀ k ɔ̃ k á n á á p ū. ɔ m ɛ ɓ ɔ́ m ɔ́ ɔ f ɔ ɔ c á.`  
> **IPA:** `m˥ ɖ a˥ ɲ ɛ ɔ˧˩ m ɛ n i˧˩ w a u n˥ ɓ o˥ s a˥ a-k ɔ-z a˧˩ i˧˩ n-ɡ͡b a˧˩ a˧˩ ɔ˧˩ k ɔ k a˥ n a˥ a˥ p u˧. ɔ m ɛ ɓ ɔ˥ m ɔ˥ ɔ f ɔ ɔ c a˥.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bassari</b> (<code>bsc</code> · GN)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɓën ex ɓër rëw̃ën këŋo Raŝel Yakob ɓën: ɓën ɗek, ɓela epëxw gë ɓënax.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɓ ë n e x ɓ ë r r ë w̃ ë n k ë ŋ o r a ŝ e l y a k o b ɓ ë n: ɓ ë n ɗ e k, ɓ e l a e p ë xw g ë ɓ ë n a x.`  
> **IPA:** `ɓ ɛ n ɛ x ɓ ɛ r r ɛ w̃ ɛ n k ɛ ŋ ɔ r a s ɛ l j a k ɔ b ɓ ɛ n: ɓ ɛ n ɗ ɛ k, ɓ ɛ l a ɛ p ɛ xʷ ɡ ɛ ɓ ɛ n a x.`

> **Text:** gayikako anang dëŋ nang kën ang nënga ko Axwën ak.  
> **Phonemes:** `g a y i k a k o a n a ng d ë ŋ n a ng k ë n a ng n ë ng a k o a xw ë n a k.`  
> **IPA:** `ɡ a j i k a k ɔ a n a ᵑɡ d ɛ ŋ n a ᵑɡ k ɛ n a ᵑɡ n ɛ ᵑɡ a k ɔ a xʷ ɛ n a k.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Beja</b> (<code>bej</code> · EG)</summary>

> **Text:** Baruuh hankaab areeya hoon akay hinin han areenay.  
> **Phonemes:** `b a r uu h h a n k aa b a r ee y a h oo n a k a y h i n i n h a n a r ee n a y.`  
> **IPA:** `b a r uː h h a n k aː b a r eː j a h oː n a k a j h i n i n h a n a r eː n a j.`

> **Text:** Baawloos been umajlisii aree bak giigiya.  
> **Phonemes:** `b aa w l oo s b ee n u m a j l i s ii a r ee b a k g ii g i y a.`  
> **IPA:** `b aː w l oː s b eː n u m a ɟ l i s iː a r eː b a k ɡ iː ɡ i j a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bekwarra</b> (<code>bky</code> · Nigeria)</summary>

> **Text:** abe e kuo ipi iyen dee iBokim. K'ipi ana abe e shang ukum gbuduu ka Onyua-Iye.  
> **Phonemes:** `a b e e k u o i p i i y e n d e e i b o k i m. k ' i p i a n a a b e e sh a ng u k u m gb u d u u k a o ny u a-i y e.`  
> **IPA:** `a b e e k u o i p i i j e n d e e i b o k i m. k ' i p i a n a a b e e ʃ a ŋ u k u m ɡ͡b u d u u k a o ɲ u a-i j e.`

> **Text:** Elisha a nwia ye k'aha, kung irishi ku he ufo imin abe n'unwatung unyiche ana ka che ushang ye fan Onyua-Iye.  
> **Phonemes:** `e l i sh a a n w i a y e k ' a h a, k u ng i r i sh i k u h e u f o i m i n a b e n ' u n w a t u ng u ny i ch e a n a k a ch e u sh a ng y e f a n o ny u a-i y e.`  
> **IPA:** `e l i ʃ a a n w i a j e k ' a h a, k u ŋ i r i ʃ i k u h e u f o i m i n a b e n ' u n w a t u ŋ u ɲ i tʃ e a n a k a tʃ e u ʃ a ŋ j e f a n o ɲ u a-i j e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bemba</b> (<code>bem</code> · BW)</summary>

> **Text:** Umuntu wamano akonka inshila iyitwala ku bumi, pakukana tentemukila ku calo ca bafwa.  
> **Phonemes:** `u m u n t u w a m a n o a k o n k a i n sh i l a i y i t w a l a k u b u m i, p a k u k a n a t e n t e m u k i l a k u c a l o c a b a f w a.`  
> **IPA:** `u m u n t u w a m a n ɔ a k ɔ n k a i n ʃ i l a i j i t w a l a k u b u m i, p a k u k a n a t e n t e m u k i l a k u tʃ a l ɔ tʃ a b a f w a.`

> **Text:** Lelo mukutuula amalambo, cilenga abantu ukwibukisha ifyabubifi fyabo cila mwaka.  
> **Phonemes:** `l e l o m u k u t uu l a a m a l a m b o, c i l e n g a a b a n t u u k w i b u k i sh a i f y a b u b i f i f y a b o c i l a m w a k a.`  
> **IPA:** `l e l ɔ m u k u t uː l a a m a l a m b ɔ, tʃ i l e n ɡ a a b a n t u u k w i b u k i ʃ a i f j a b u b i f i f j a b ɔ tʃ i l a m w a k a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Benga</b> (<code>bng</code> · GA)</summary>

> **Text:** Ka Mâ‐a tamuwa kěběngě kěběngě iyěně ja a ma ha lombo těeně.  
> **Phonemes:** `k a m â‐a t a m u w a k ě b ě ng ě k ě b ě ng ě i y ě n ě j a a m a h a l o mb o t ě e n ě.`  
> **IPA:** `k a m a‐a t a m u w a k e b e ŋ e k e b e ŋ e i j e n e ʒ a a m a h a l o ᵐb o t e e n e.`

> **Text:** Ka Jěhova a kala na Mosěs, na.  
> **Phonemes:** `k a j ě h o v a a k a l a n a m o s ě s, n a.`  
> **IPA:** `k a ʒ e h o v a a k a l a n a m o s e s, n a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Berom</b> (<code>bom</code> · NG)</summary>

> **Text:** ko e sè tyang na bemat beves wɛt, be gwɛ̌ a yɛ pɔlɔ lis wɛt, ba sé sede necam, yaga ba sap Dagwi wɛt.  
> **Phonemes:** `k o e s è t y a ng n a b e m a t b e v e s w ɛ t, b e g w ɛ̌ a y ɛ p ɔ l ɔ l i s w ɛ t, b a s é s e d e n e c a m, y a g a b a s a p d a g w i w ɛ t.`  
> **IPA:** `k o e s e t j a ŋ n a b e m a t b e v e s w ɛ t, b e ɡ w ɛ a j ɛ p ɔ l ɔ l i s w ɛ t, b a s e s e d e n e t͡ʃ a m, j a ɡ a b a s a p d a ɡ w i w ɛ t.`

> **Text:** Yin wok nemwishish mɛ wɛt. Pát in mwât mɔrɔ̂ mwat womo yi e ji BeIsrayɛl, yaga na yin bin sangal tyang.  
> **Phonemes:** `y i n w o k n e m w i sh i sh m ɛ w ɛ t. p á t i n m w â t m ɔ r ɔ̂ m w a t w o m o y i e j i b e i s r a y ɛ l, y a g a n a y i n b i n s a ng a l t y a ng.`  
> **IPA:** `j i n w o k n e m w i ʃ i ʃ m ɛ w ɛ t. p a t i n m w a t m ɔ r ɔ m w a t w o m o j i e d͡ʒ i b e i s r a j ɛ l, j a ɡ a n a j i n b i n s a ŋ a l t j a ŋ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bimoba</b> (<code>bim</code> · Ghana)</summary>

> **Text:** Ŋaan waa daa dɔɔ gɔɔnt na, namu saa pikinɔ nan fat-tɔɔ nae.  
> **Phonemes:** `ŋ a a n w a a d a a d ɔ ɔ g ɔ ɔ n t n a, n a m u s a a p i k i n ɔ n a n f a t-t ɔ ɔ n a e.`  
> **IPA:** `ŋ a a n w a a d a a d ɔ ɔ ɡ ɔ ɔ n t n a, n a m u s a a p i k i n ɔ n a n f a t-t ɔ ɔ n a e.`

> **Text:** nan Adama nan Rama nan Hasor.  
> **Phonemes:** `n a n a d a m a n a n r a m a n a n h a s o r.`  
> **IPA:** `n a n a d a m a n a n r a m a n a n h a s o r.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bisa</b> (<code>bib</code> · Burkina Faso)</summary>

> **Text:** Hinni bɩ dɔ ʊ bɩ, Yeezuu bɔ har ʊ, an aa nyɩnta higʊta bɩ lɛ ra.  
> **Phonemes:** `h i n n i b ɩ d ɔ ʊ b ɩ, y e e z u u b ɔ h a r ʊ, a n a a ny ɩ n t a h i g ʊ t a b ɩ l ɛ r a.`  
> **IPA:** `h i n n i b ɪ d ɔ ʊ b ɪ, j e e z u u b ɔ h a r ʊ, a n a a ɲ ɪ n t a h i ɡ ʊ t a b ɪ l ɛ r a.`

> **Text:** Dundo ɩ mɩŋŋa zi kan kɩ sɔ, bala, a bɩsɩ ka wɔɔ meerbaarɔ ma gʊta.  
> **Phonemes:** `d u n d o ɩ m ɩ ŋ ŋ a z i k a n k ɩ s ɔ, b a l a, a b ɩ s ɩ k a w ɔ ɔ m e e r b a a r ɔ m a g ʊ t a.`  
> **IPA:** `d u n d o ɪ m ɪ ŋ ŋ a z i k a n k ɪ s ɔ, b a l a, a b ɪ s ɪ k a w ɔ ɔ m e e r b a a r ɔ m a ɡ ʊ t a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bola</b> (<code>knf</code> · GW)</summary>

> **Text:** « Dan aşih pntaali pi nul, jibi babuk aşin bakşihuŋ ki baka.  
> **Phonemes:** `« d a n a ş i h p n t a a l i p i n u l, j i b i b a b u k a ş i n b a k ş i h u ŋ k i b a k a.`  
> **IPA:** `« (ⁿ)d ɑ n ɑ s i ɣ p n t ɑ ɑ l i p i n u l, j i (ᵐ)b i (ᵐ)b ɑ (ᵐ)b u k ɑ s i n (ᵐ)b ɑ k s i ɣ u ŋ k i (ᵐ)b ɑ k ɑ.`

> **Text:** Uţup mënţ, Naşibaţi aţëpna undiimaan ţi baţuparul ahoŋ wa ţi Ulibra wi nul.  
> **Phonemes:** `u ţ u p m ë n ţ, n a ş i b a ţ i a ţ ë p n a u n d i i m a a n ţ i b a ţ u p a r u l a h o ŋ w a ţ i u l i b r a w i n u l.`  
> **IPA:** `u t u p m ɛ n t, n ɑ s i (ᵐ)b ɑ t i ɑ t ɛ p n ɑ u n (ⁿ)d i i m ɑ ɑ n t i (ᵐ)b ɑ t u p ɑ r u l ɑ ɣ ɔ ŋ w ɑ t i u l i (ᵐ)b r ɑ w i n u l.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Borgu Fulfulde</b> (<code>fue</code> · BJ)</summary>

> **Text:** Taa ɓeydu keerol gesa maa ŋa tawunoɗaa baabiraaɓe maa e woodunoo.  
> **Phonemes:** `t aa ɓ e y d u k ee r o l g e s a m aa ŋ a t a w u n o ɗ aa b aa b i r aa ɓ e m aa e w oo d u n oo.`  
> **IPA:** `t aː ɓ e j d u k eː r o l ɡ e s a m aː ᵑɡ a t a w u n o ɗ aː b aː b i r aː ɓ e m aː e w oː d u n oː.`

> **Text:** O nyaaman nun fu, o horataako goɗɗum, kanjum acci jawdi makko ɓooyataa.  
> **Phonemes:** `o ny aa m a n n u n f u, o h o r a t aa k o g o ɗ ɗ u m, k a nj u m a c c i j a w d i m a k k o ɓ oo y a t aa.`  
> **IPA:** `o ɲ aː m a n n u n f u, o h o r a t aː k o ɡ o ɗ ɗ u m, k a ᶮʤ u m a ʧ ʧ i ʤ a w d i m a k k o ɓ oː j a t aː.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bukusu</b> (<code>bxk</code> · KE)</summary>

> **Text:** Omuloli omweyikinwa se aloma bubeeyi tawe, ne kakhali omuloli omubeeyi, aloma bubeeyi bwong'ene.  
> **Phonemes:** `o m u l o l i o m w e y i k i n w a s e a l o m a b u b e e y i t a w e, n e k a kh a l i o m u l o l i o m u b e e y i, a l o m a b u b e e y i b w o ng' e n e.`  
> **IPA:** `o m u l o l i o m w e j i k i n w a s e a l o m a b u b e e j i t a w e, n e k a x a l i o m u l o l i o m u b e e j i, a l o m a b u b e e j i b w o ŋ e n e.`

> **Text:** Nakhaba ne khali ne kamabi se nyala nakobosiana ne naye tawe, khoya sabe akholele siisa.  
> **Phonemes:** `n a kh a b a n e kh a l i n e k a m a b i s e ny a l a n a k o b o s i a n a n e n a y e t a w e, kh o y a s a b e a kh o l e l e s i i s a.`  
> **IPA:** `n a x a b a n e x a l i n e k a m a b i s e ɲ a l a n a k o b o s i a n a n e n a j e t a w e, x o j a s a b e a x o l e l e s i i s a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Bulu</b> (<code>bum</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** be nga fulan * meyoñ me ne te yeme Zambe be nga yé'é mimboone mi mam miap.  
> **Phonemes:** `b e n g a f u l a n * m e y o ñ m e n e t e y e m e z a m b e b e n g a y é ' é m i m b o o n e m i m a m m i a p.`  
> **IPA:** `[b] [ə] [n] [ɡ] [a] [f] [u] [l] [a] [n] * [m] [ə] [j] [ɔ] [n] [m] [ə] [n] [ə] [t] [ə] [j] [ə] [m] [ə] [z] [a] [m] [b] [ə] [b] [ə] [n] [ɡ] [a] [j] [ə] [ʔ] [ə] [m] [i] [m] [b] [ɔ] [ɔ] [n] [ə] [m] [i] [m] [a] [m] [m] [i] [a] [p].`

> **Text:** a ye kee ngume nya'a ôse e minkan atan, e vôm ba kut asup, a di'i wô nduan e nja'a yôp: a wo ye mane dik avis asube yôp.  
> **Phonemes:** `a y e k e e n g u m e ny a ' a ô s e e m i n k a n a t a n, e v ô m b a k u t a s u p, a d i ' i w ô n d u a n e n j a ' a y ô p: a w o y e m a n e d i k a v i s a s u b e y ô p.`  
> **IPA:** `[a] [j] [ə] [k] [ə] [ə] [n] [ɡ] [u] [m] [ə] [ɲ] [a] [ʔ] [a] [ɔ] [s] [ə] [ə] [m] [i] [n] [k] [a] [n] [a] [t] [a] [n], [ə] [v] [ɔ] [m] [b] [a] [k] [u] [t] [a] [s] [u] [p], [a] [d] [i] [ʔ] [i] [w] [ɔ] [n] [d] [u] [a] [n] [ə] [n] [d͡ʒ] [a] [ʔ] [a] [j] [ɔ] [p]: [a] [w] [ɔ] [j] [ə] [m] [a] [n] [ə] [d] [i] [k] [a] [v] [i] [s] [a] [s] [u] [b] [ə] [j] [ɔ] [p].`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Busa</b> (<code>bqp</code> · NG)</summary>

> **Text:** Ò pɔ́ è lán tɛ́nɛnɛnɔ bà à fã̀kɔ̃a à dìdi ń baadia.  
> **Phonemes:** `ò p ɔ́ è l á n t ɛ́ n ɛ n ɛ n ɔ b à à f ã̀ k ɔ̃ a à d ì d i ń b aa d i a.`  
> **IPA:** `o p ɔ e l a n t ɛ n ɛ n ɛ n ɔ b a a f a~ k ɔ~ a a d i d i n b aː d i a.`

> **Text:** À zĩ̀i blè Filisitininɔa ari Gaza kũ gu kũ ò likainɔ sɛna zaa gudãkpãkia ari wɛ̃tɛ bĩnidea.  
> **Phonemes:** `à z ĩ̀ i b l è f i l i s i t i n i n ɔ a a r i g a z a k ũ g u k ũ ò l i k a i n ɔ s ɛ n a z aa g u d ã kp ã k i a a r i w ɛ̃ t ɛ b ĩ n i d e a.`  
> **IPA:** `a z i~ i b l e f i l i s i t i n i n ɔ a a ɾ i ɡ a z a k u~ ɡ u k u~ o l i k a i n ɔ s ɛ n a z aː ɡ u d a~ kp a~ k i a a ɾ i w ɛ~ t ɛ b i~ n i d e a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Chakosi</b> (<code>cko</code> · BJ)</summary>

> **Text:** Tɔ, nzɛn Fɛmɛ Davidi mmɔɔ ni i tii yo fereeri Kristo n ka i Mibiɛ, ye Kristo n yo sɛ kaaki yo Fɛmɛ Davidi anuma a.  
> **Phonemes:** `t ɔ, n z ɛn f ɛ m ɛ d a v i d i m m ɔɔ n i i t i i y o f e r ee r i k r i s t o n k a i m i b i ɛ, y e k r i s t o n y o s ɛ k aa k i y o f ɛ m ɛ d a v i d i an u m a a.`  
> **IPA:** `t ɔ, n z ɛ̃ f ɛ m ɛ d a v i d i m m ɔː n i i t i i j o f e r eː r i k r i s t o n k a i m i b i ɛ, j e k r i s t o n j o s ɛ k aː k i j o f ɛ m ɛ d a v i d i ã u m a a.`

> **Text:** Nyini nfɛmɛ burum n yó bu nɔɔkun, na bu fá bu faŋga ni bu yiko kɛrɛ na bu fá ma boro neŋge­ndɔrɛ ni.  
> **Phonemes:** `ny in i n f ɛ m ɛ b u r u m n y ó b u n ɔɔ k un, n a b u f á b u f a ŋ g a n i b u y i k o k ɛ r ɛ n a b u f á m a b o r o n e ŋ g e­n d ɔ r ɛ n i.`  
> **IPA:** `ɲ ĩ i n f ɛ m ɛ b u r u m n j o b u n ɔː k ũ, n a b u f a b u f a ŋ ɡ a n i b u j i k o k ɛ r ɛ n a b u f a m a b o r o n e ŋ ɡ e­n d ɔ r ɛ n i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Chichewa</b> (<code>nya</code> · MW)</summary>

> **Text:** Ndipo anatcha malowo dzina lake Kibroti-Hatava; popeza pamenepo anaika anthu osusuka.  
> **Phonemes:** `n d i p o a n a tch a m a l o w o d z i n a l a k e k i b r o t i-h a t a v a; p o p e z a p a m e n e p o a n a i k a a n th u o s u s u k a.`  
> **IPA:** `n d i p o a n a ʧʰ a m a Ɩ o w o d z i n a Ɩ a k e k i b ɽ o t i-h a t a v a; p o p e z a p a m e n e p o a n a i k a a n tʰ u o s u s u k a.`

> **Text:** Koma munamwetsa Anaziri vinyo, ndi kulamulira aneneri ndi kuti, Musamanenera.  
> **Phonemes:** `k o m a m u n a m w e t s a a n a z i r i v i n y o, n d i k u l a m u l i r a a n e n e r i n d i k u t i, m u s a m a n e n e r a.`  
> **IPA:** `k o m a m u n a m w e t s a a n a z i ɽ i v i n y o, n d i k u Ɩ a m u Ɩ i ɽ a a n e n e ɽ i n d i k u t i, m u s a m a n e n e ɽ a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Chokwe</b> (<code>cjk</code> · AO)</summary>

> **Text:** mwe sasendote manjila kutala. Nyi musongo hiwatanda mu zuwo, mba wapwa mbumba ya mbongola mu zuwo, liapwa lia luchisa.  
> **Phonemes:** `m w e s a s e nd o t e m a n j i l a k u t a l a. ny i m u s o ng o h i w a t a nd a m u z u w o, mb a w a p w a mb u mb a y a mb o ng o l a m u z u w o, l i a p w a l i a l u c h i s a.`  
> **IPA:** `m w e s a s e ⁿd o t e m a n ɟ i l a k u t a l a. ɲ i m u s o ᵑɡ o h i w a t a ⁿd a m u z u w o, ᵐb a w a p w a ᵐb u ᵐb a j a ᵐb o ᵑɡ o l a m u z u w o, l i a p w a l i a l u c h i s a.`

> **Text:** chifuchi yichinyika, vula yanoka hashi, mumu yena Zambi ya mulundu wa Sinai , Zambi ya Aisraele, te uneza.  
> **Phonemes:** `c h i f u c h i y i c h i ny i k a, v u l a y a n o k a h a s h i, m u m u y e n a z a mb i y a m u l u nd u w a s i n a i , z a mb i y a a i s r a e l e, t e u n e z a.`  
> **IPA:** `c h i f u c h i j i c h i ɲ i k a, v u l a j a n o k a h a s h i, m u m u j e n a z a ᵐb i j a m u l u ⁿd u w a s i n a i , z a ᵐb i j a a i s r a e l e, t e u n e z a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Chumburung</b> (<code>ncu</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** Mfe̱ŋ ne̱ o te na Wuribware̱ baa Yeesuu Kristoo ado̱ŋ pɛɛɛ mò̱ kuwure‑o kaase̱.  
> **Phonemes:** `m f e̱ ŋ n e̱ o t e n a w u r i b w a r e̱ b a a y e e s u u k r i s t o o a d o̱ ŋ p ɛ ɛ ɛ m ò̱ k u w u r e‑o k a a s e̱.`  
> **IPA:** `m f e ŋ n e o t e n a w u r i b w a r e b a a j e e s u u k r i s t o o a d o ŋ p ɛ ɛ ɛ m o k u w u r e‑o k a a s e.`

> **Text:** Ane̱ maa taare̱ a ane̱ gyii ke̱tɔ ke̱maa mbe̱yɔmɔ. Ane̱ maa taare̱ a ane̱ tɔwe̱ ke̱tɔ ne̱ ke̱ e̱ ba‑ɔ pɛɛɛ.  
> **Phonemes:** `a n e̱ m a a t a a r e̱ a a n e̱ gy i i k e̱ t ɔ k e̱ m a a m b e̱ y ɔ m ɔ. a n e̱ m a a t a a r e̱ a a n e̱ t ɔ w e̱ k e̱ t ɔ n e̱ k e̱ e̱ b a‑ɔ p ɛ ɛ ɛ.`  
> **IPA:** `a n e m a a t a a r e a a n e d͡ʒ i i k e t ɔ k e m a a m b e j ɔ m ɔ. a n e m a a t a a r e a a n e t ɔ w e k e t ɔ n e k e e b a‑ɔ p ɛ ɛ ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Comorian</b> (<code>swb</code> · KM)</summary>

> **Text:** Mana uMwana wa Mwanadamu|Mwana wa Mwanadamu aja ahuyi wapotea.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a n a u mw a n a w a mw a n a d a m u|mw a n a w a mw a n a d a m u a j a a h u y i w a p o t e a.`  
> **IPA:** `m a n a u mw a n a w a mw a n a d a m u|mw a n a w a mw a n a d a m u a ʒ a a h u j i w a p o t e a.`

> **Text:** Mana Demasu anipuka ha uv̄endza wa lidunia la leo, andre Tesalonika. Kresensi andre Galatwiya, na Titusa waye Dalmatiya.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a n a d e m a s u a n i p u k a h a u v̄ e n dz a w a l i d u n i a l a l e o, a n dr e t e s a l o n i k a. k r e s e n s i a n dr e g a l a tw i y a, n a t i t u s a w a y e d a l m a t i y a.`  
> **IPA:** `m a n a d e m a s u a n i p u k a h a u v e n dz a w a l i d u n i a l a l e o, a n dr e t e s a l o n i k a. k r e s e n s i a n dr e ɡ a l a tw i j a, n a t i t u s a w a j e d a l m a t i j a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Daasanach</b> (<code>dsh</code> · ET)</summary>

> **Text:** Yíésu min geey, kieye, Sí, bélkú ká veʼ, hol ka eedhdhíe, yie.  
> **Phonemes:** `y í é s u m i n g e e y, k i e y e, s í, b é l k ú k á v e ', h o l k a e e d h d h í e, y i e.`  
> **IPA:** `j i e s u m i n ɡ e e j, k i e j e, s i, b e l k u k a v e ʔ, h o l k a e e d h d h i e, j i e.`

> **Text:** Yíésu kadich higileká máá giri aryáán hí yé gaa ˈdiiyyeká hí ruogone, giri mu taalle Yíésu hol egeetká.  
> **Phonemes:** `y í é s u k a d i ch h i g i l e k á m á á g i r i a r y á á n h í y é g a a ˈ d i i y y e k á h í r u o g o n e, g i r i m u t a a l l e y í é s u h o l e g e e t k á.`  
> **IPA:** `j i e s u k a d i c h i ɡ i l e k a m a a ɡ i r i a r j a a n h i j e ɡ a a ˈ d i i j j e k a h i r u o ɡ o n e, ɡ i r i m u t a a l l e j i e s u h o l e ɡ e e t k a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Dagbani</b> (<code>dag</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** ni zuɣulaannim mini tɔbbihi ni sariakaritiba ni anabinima ni baɣisi ni kpamba.  
> **Phonemes:** `n i z u ɣ u l aa n n i m m i n i t ɔ b b i h i n i s a r i a k a r i t i b a n i a n a b i n i m a n i b a ɣ i s i n i kp a m b a.`  
> **IPA:** `n i z u ɣ u l aː n n i m m i n i t ɔ b b i h i n i s a r i a k a r i t i b a n i a n a b i n i m a n i b a ɣ i s i n i k͡p a m b a.`

> **Text:** ka bi tɛhi ni napɔŋ ni tooi no ŋ-ŋma li, ka mɔɣu ni biŋkɔbiri gba ni tooi no li.  
> **Phonemes:** `k a b i t ɛ h i n i n a p ɔ ŋ n i t o o i n o ŋ-ŋm a l i, k a m ɔ ɣ u n i b i ŋ k ɔ b i r i gb a n i t o o i n o l i.`  
> **IPA:** `k a b i t ɛ h i n i n a p ɔ ŋ n i t oː oː i n oː ŋ-ŋ͡m a l i, k a m ɔ ɣ u n i b i ŋ k ɔ b i r i ɡ͡b a n i t oː oː i n oː l i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Dan</b> (<code>dnj</code> · CI)</summary>

> **Text:** «‑Gasitɛ 'dhɛ 'kö ya n Dëmɛ ꞊në yö ‑zë ‑kë n ‑dhɛ, 'yö ö wë bɔ n ‑ta, kë dhʋ̈ kö 'ö 'yiʋ̈‑ bho n ‑ta mɛ ꞊zinng 'gü .  
> **Phonemes:** `«‑g a s i t ɛ 'dh ɛ 'k ö y a n d ë m ɛ ꞊n ë y ö ‑z ë ‑k ë n ‑dh ɛ, 'y ö ö w ë b ɔ n ‑t a, k ë dh ʋ̈ k ö 'ö 'y i ʋ̈‑ bh o n ‑t a m ɛ ꞊z i n ng 'g ü .`  
> **IPA:** `«‑ɡ a s i t ɛ 'ɗ ɛ 'k o j a n d e m ɛ ꞊n e j o ‑z e ‑k e n ‑ɗ ɛ, 'j o o w e b ɔ n ‑t a, k e ɗ ʊ k o 'o 'j i ʊ‑ ɓ o n ‑t a m ɛ ꞊z i n ŋ 'ɡ u .`

> **Text:** Mɛ 'gbɛ ‑wo kë 'dhang bho‑ dhiʋ̈ dhiang ‑nu 'ö‑ zë dhʋ̈ bha‑ ‑wɔn 'gü.  
> **Phonemes:** `m ɛ 'gb ɛ ‑w o k ë 'dh a ng bh o‑ dh i ʋ̈ dh i a ng ‑n u 'ö‑ z ë dh ʋ̈ bh a‑ ‑w ɔ n 'g ü.`  
> **IPA:** `m ɛ 'ɡ͡b ɛ ‑w o k e 'ɗ a ŋ ɓ o‑ ɗ i ʊ ɗ i a ŋ ‑n u 'o‑ z e ɗ ʊ ɓ a‑ ‑w ɔ n 'ɡ u.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Dangaléat</b> (<code>daa</code> · TD)</summary>

> **Text:** Ƴiriy taar-at di, Iisa amiltu min ɗo ger-aka ho ŋa birtu bi ka barrer. Ɗo wer-ak, ŋa goytu, ŋa garkiyiy gee.  
> **Phonemes:** `ƴ i r i y t aa r-a t d i, ii s a a m i l t u m i n ɗ o g e r-a k a h o ŋ a b i r t u b i k a b a r r e r. ɗ o w e r-a k, ŋ a g o y t u, ŋ a g a r k i y i y g ee.`  
> **IPA:** `ʄ i r i j t aː r-a t d i, iː s a a m i l t u m i n ɗ o ɡ e r-a k a h o ŋ a b i r t u b i k a b a r r e r. ɗ o w e r-a k, ŋ a ɡ o j t u, ŋ a ɡ a r k i j i j ɡ eː.`

> **Text:** Ƴiriy rakki ta dumaasdi, Ruwwin ta Buŋdi asintu miin tak ho nu dora maan aaror ar gaaƴ ta gemor. Ta dora raɗa ar perne.  
> **Phonemes:** `ƴ i r i y r a k k i t a d u m aa s d i, r u w w i n t a b u ŋ d i a s i n t u m ii n t a k h o n u d o r a m aa n aa r o r a r g aa ƴ t a g e m o r. t a d o r a r a ɗ a a r p e r n e.`  
> **IPA:** `ʄ i r i j r a k k i t a d u m aː s d i, r u w w i n t a b u ŋ d i a s i n t u m iː n t a k h o n u d o r a m aː n aː r o r a r ɡ aː ʄ t a ɡ e m o r. t a d o r a r a ɗ a a r p e r n e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Dangme</b> (<code>ada</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** A de Yoshua ke, Wa ma sɔmɔ mo. E bi mɛ ke, Mɛnɔmɛ ji nyɛ? Jije nyɛ je.  
> **Phonemes:** `a d e y o s h u a k e, w a m a s ɔ m ɔ m o. e b i m ɛ k e, m ɛ n ɔ m ɛ j i ny ɛ? j i j e ny ɛ j e.`  
> **IPA:** `a̠ d e j o s h u a̠ k e, w a̠ m a̠ s ɔ̞ m ɔ̞ m o. e b i m æ k e, m æ n ɔ̞ m æ d͡ʒ i ɲ æ? d͡ʒ i d͡ʒ e ɲ æ d͡ʒ e.`

> **Text:** Nɛ a de a he ke: Anɛ wa tsui he dɔɛ la ngɛ wa mi benɛ e kɛ wɔ ngɛ munyu tue ngɛ blɔ ɔ nɔ nɛ e tsɔɔ wɔ ngmami ɔmɛ a mi ɔ.  
> **Phonemes:** `n ɛ a d e a h e k e: a n ɛ w a ts u i h e d ɔ ɛ l a ng ɛ w a m i b e n ɛ e k ɛ w ɔ ng ɛ m u ny u t u e ng ɛ b l ɔ ɔ n ɔ n ɛ e ts ɔ ɔ w ɔ ngm a m i ɔ m ɛ a m i ɔ.`  
> **IPA:** `n æ a̠ d e a̠ h e k e: a̠ n æ w a̠ t͡ʃ u i h e d ɔ̞ æ l a̠ ŋ æ w a̠ m i b e n æ e k æ w ɔ̞ ŋ æ m u ɲ u t u e ŋ æ b l ɔ̞ ɔ̞ n ɔ̞ n æ e t͡ʃ ɔ̞ ɔ̞ w ɔ̞ ŋ͡m a̠ m i ɔ̞ m æ a̠ m i ɔ̞.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Doayo</b> (<code>dow</code> · Cameroon)</summary>

> **Text:** gɛ Bɛt Mɔkil bɔ, hi mɛm bɔ hi ga̧a̧m bɔ, hin taarɛ wɔrɔ sɛw hi ɔks ya̧a̧ya̧ gbunu.  
> **Phonemes:** `g ɛ b ɛ t m ɔ k i l b ɔ, h i m ɛ m b ɔ h i g a̧ a̧ m b ɔ, h i n t a a r ɛ w ɔ r ɔ s ɛ w h i ɔ k s y a̧ a̧ y a̧ gb u n u.`  
> **IPA:** `ɡ ɛ b ɛ t m ɔ k i l b ɔ, h i m ɛ m b ɔ h i ɡ a a m b ɔ, h i n t a a r ɛ w ɔ r ɔ s ɛ w h i ɔ k s j a a j a ɡ͡b u n u.`

> **Text:** Yɔ saalzanɔ gbɔɔl zaa hi mɔks gi dɔŋdi, gii be kar ɗɔ̀ɔ̀t a lɛmb za du. Dood saal wɔ hi ɗɔ̀ɔ̀t a mɛmbalya̧ di be.  
> **Phonemes:** `y ɔ s a a l z a n ɔ gb ɔ ɔ l z a a h i m ɔ k s g i d ɔ ŋ d i, g i i b e k a r ɗ ɔ̀ ɔ̀ t a l ɛ m b z a d u. d o o d s a a l w ɔ h i ɗ ɔ̀ ɔ̀ t a m ɛ m b a l y a̧ d i b e.`  
> **IPA:** `j ɔ s a a l z a n ɔ ɡ͡b ɔ ɔ l z a a h i m ɔ k s ɡ i d ɔ ŋ d i, ɡ i i b e k a r ɗ ɔ˩ ɔ˩ t a l ɛ m b z a d u. d o o d s a a l w ɔ h i ɗ ɔ˩ ɔ˩ t a m ɛ m b a l j a d i b e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Duruma</b> (<code>dug</code> · Kenya)</summary>

> **Text:** na mutʼu adawae bee andakala ni ao atʼue mwenye.  
> **Phonemes:** `n a m u t' u a d a w a e b e e a nd a k a l a n i a o a t' u e mw e ny e.`  
> **IPA:** `n a m u tʰ u a d a w a ɛ b ɛ ɛ a n͡d a k a l a n i a ɔ a tʰ u ɛ ŋ͡m ɛ ɲ ɛ.`

> **Text:** mana, Mwenyezi Mulungu udzinihendera mabomu, na dzinare ni takatifu.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a n a, mw e ny e z i m u l u ng u u dz i n i h e nd e r a m a b o m u, n a dz i n a r e n i t a k a t i f u.`  
> **IPA:** `m a n a, ŋ͡m ɛ ɲ ɛ z i m u l u ŋ u u d͡z i n i h ɛ n͡d ɛ r a m a b ɔ m u, n a d͡z i n a r ɛ n i t a k a t i f u.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ebira</b> (<code>igb</code> · Nigeria)</summary>

> **Text:** Ọ́ọ́ zụ sị eyi tụra a ka ọ mẹ̀ ụkọrọ yị ọnị agụvị ka ọ nyị ụkata.  
> **Phonemes:** `ọ́ ọ́ z ụ s ị e y i t ụ r a a k a ọ m ẹ̀ ụ k ọ r ọ y ị ọ n ị a g ụ v ị k a ọ ny ị ụ k a t a.`  
> **IPA:** `o˥ o˥ z u s i e j i t u r a a k a o m e˩ u k o r o j i o n i a ɡ u v i k a o ɲ i u k a t a.`

> **Text:** Ọza ọnị eyi anị ọ yị hweyi vị ana kụ ụra ogu ama ọyẹrẹyị vị ọnị ịrẹyọyẹ vị ahaa yị.  
> **Phonemes:** `ọ z a ọ n ị e y i a n ị ọ y ị h w e y i v ị a n a k ụ ụ r a o g u a m a ọ y ẹ r ẹ y ị v ị ọ n ị ị r ẹ y ọ y ẹ v ị a h a a y ị.`  
> **IPA:** `o z a o n i e j i a n i o j i h w e j i v i a n a k u u r a o ɡ u a m a o j e r e j i v i o n i i r e j o j e v i a h a a j i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Efik</b> (<code>efi</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Ndien uyo oto ke ikpaenyọñ ọdọhọ ete, Emi edi Eyen Mi Ami mmekde; ekpañ utọñ enọ Enye.  
> **Phonemes:** `n d i e n u y o o t o k e i kp a e ny ọ ñ ọ d ọ h ọ e t e, e m i e d i e y e n m i a m i m m e k d e; e kp a ñ u t ọ ñ e n ọ e ny e.`  
> **IPA:** `n d i ɛ n u j o o t o k ɛ i k͡p a ɛ nʲ o n o d o h o ɛ t ɛ, ɛ m i ɛ d i ɛ j ɛ n m i a m i m m ɛ k d ɛ; ɛ k͡p a n u t o n ɛ n o ɛ nʲ ɛ.`

> **Text:** Ese ikpọ ubọk ñwed emi ami ndade ñwet ñwed emi ke idem mi nnọ esọk mbufo.  
> **Phonemes:** `e s e i kp ọ u b ọ k ñ w e d e m i a m i n d a d e ñ w e t ñ w e d e m i k e i d e m m i n n ọ e s ọ k mb u f o.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ s ɛ i k͡p o u b o k n w ɛ d ɛ m i a m i n d a d ɛ n w ɛ t n w ɛ d ɛ m i k ɛ i d ɛ m m i n n o ɛ s o k m͡b u f o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Esimbi</b> (<code>ags</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɛyəbhə nɛni ɛrutu mikpi mɛkələ, ɔhini monumbu ɛpili ɔndə kiburu ɛki nɔtsənə mabi ɛbi ini əsə otə vi ɔbhəŋgə.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ y ə bh ə n ɛ n i ɛ r u t u m i kp i m ɛ k ə l ə, ɔ h i n i m o n u mb u ɛ p i l i ɔ nd ə k i b u r u ɛ k i n ɔ t s ə n ə m a b i ɛ b i i n i ə s ə o t ə v i ɔ bh ə ŋg ə.`  
> **IPA:** `ɨ j ə β ə n ɨ n i ɨ r u t u m i k͡p i m ɨ k ə l ə, ɔ h i n i m o n u ᵐb u ɨ p i l i ɔ ⁿd ə k i b u r u ɨ k i n ɔ t s ə n ə m a b i ɨ b i i n i ə s ə o t ə v i ɔ β ə ᵑɡ ə.`

> **Text:** Ɛtiyi ondə kɛli olu imi əsə ɛvi kinu kebhərɛbhə kananə. Ɔhini ubu ondə nə imi otə mɔkpəmə ibi mɛvi ɔbhəŋgə.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ t i y i o nd ə k ɛ l i o l u i m i ə s ə ɛ v i k i n u k e bh ə r ɛ bh ə k a n a n ə. ɔ h i n i u b u o nd ə n ə i m i o t ə m ɔ kp ə m ə i b i m ɛ v i ɔ bh ə ŋg ə.`  
> **IPA:** `ɨ t i j i o ⁿd ə k ɨ l i o l u i m i ə s ə ɨ v i k i n u k e β ə r ɨ β ə k a n a n ə. ɔ h i n i u b u o ⁿd ə n ə i m i o t ə m ɔ k͡p ə m ə i b i m ɨ v i ɔ β ə ᵑɡ ə.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ewe</b> (<code>ewe</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** agbadɔ ŋu tsyotiwo kple kpɔ ŋu tsyotiwo kpakple woƒe atagbiawo;.  
> **Phonemes:** `a gb a d ɔ ŋ u ts y o t i w o kp l e kp ɔ ŋ u ts y o t i w o kp a kp l e w o ƒ e a t a gb i a w o;.`  
> **IPA:** `a ɡ͡b a d ɔ ŋ u ts y o t i w o k͡p l e k͡p ɔ ŋ u ts y o t i w o k͡p a k͡p l e w o ɸ e a t a ɡ͡b i a w o;.`

> **Text:** Yosua, Nun vi, ame si nye Mose ƒe kpeɖeŋutɔ tso ɖekakpuime ke la, gblɔ na Mose be: Nye Aƒetɔ Mose, gbe na wo.  
> **Phonemes:** `y o s u a, n u n v i, a m e si ny e m o s e ƒ e kp e ɖ e ŋ u t ɔ ts o ɖ e k a kp u i m e k e l a, gb l ɔ n a m o s e b e: ny e a ƒ e t ɔ m o s e, gb e n a w o.`  
> **IPA:** `y o s u a, n u n v i, a m e ʃi ɲ e m o s e ɸ e k͡p e ɖ e ŋ u t ɔ ts o ɖ e k a k͡p u i m e k e l a, ɡ͡b l ɔ n a m o s e b e: ɲ e a ɸ e t ɔ m o s e, ɡ͡b e n a w o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ewondo</b> (<code>ewo</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** atyan ai mòn woe ya angabyali a nda-bod David mfag ya minson.  
> **Phonemes:** `a t y a n a i m ò n w o e y a a ng a b y a l i a nd a-b o d d a v i d m f a g y a m i n s o n.`  
> **IPA:** `a t j a n a i m o n w o e j a a n.ɡ a b j a l i a n͡d a-b o d d a v i d m f a ɡ j a m i n s o n.`

> **Text:** Zakarias nyè ai englès na: Mayi yëm e mam ma ai dze? Amu ma ha, mënë nnom, ndò fë ngal wama antoa ya mimbu !.  
> **Phonemes:** `z a k a r i a s ny è a i e ng l è s n a: m a y i y ë m e m a m m a a i dz e? a m u m a h a, m ë n ë n n o m, nd ò f ë ng a l w a m a a n t o a y a m i mb u !.`  
> **IPA:** `z a k a ʀ i a s ɲ e a i e n.ɡ l e s n a: m a j i j e m e m a m m a a i ʣ e? a m u m a h a, m e n e n n o m, n͡d o f e n.ɡ a l w a m a a n t o a j a m i m͡b u !.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Fon</b> (<code>fon</code> · BJ)</summary>

> **Text:** Sɛ́mɛɖó, nǔ e mɛ ná wa din ɔ́, mɛ ná ba gbɛtɔ́ e bí ganjí, bó ɖó nǔnywɛ́ ɔ́ ɖokpó, bó ná sɔ́ ɖó Ejípu to ɔ́ nu.  
> **Phonemes:** `s ɛ́ m ɛ ɖ ó, n ǔ e m ɛ n á w a d i n ɔ́, m ɛ n á b a gb ɛ t ɔ́ e b í g a n j í, b ó ɖ ó n ǔ ny w ɛ́ ɔ́ ɖ o kp ó, b ó n á s ɔ́ ɖ ó e j í p u t o ɔ́ n u.`  
> **IPA:** `s ɛ m ɛ ɖ o, n u e m ɛ n a w a d i n ɔ, m ɛ n a b a ɡb ɛ t ɔ e b i ɡ a n dʒ i, b o ɖ o n u ɲ w ɛ ɔ ɖ o kp o, b o n a s ɔ ɖ o e dʒ i p u t o ɔ n u.`

> **Text:** Mɔyízi xa Mawusɛ́n ɔ́ nú Izlayɛ́li ví lɛ́ɛ.  
> **Phonemes:** `m ɔ y í z i x a m a w u s ɛ́ n ɔ́ n ú i z l a y ɛ́ l i v í l ɛ́ ɛ.`  
> **IPA:** `m ɔ j i z i x a m a w u s ɛ n ɔ n u i z l a j ɛ l i v i l ɛ ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Frafra</b> (<code>gur</code> · BF)</summary>

> **Text:** Yia, mam bo'ori Yinɛ fara Yezu puan ya za'a zuo, beni zuo, ya sakerɛ la yele to'e tiŋa wa zuo za'a mɛ.  
> **Phonemes:** `y i a, m a m b o ' o r i y in ɛ f a r a y e z u p u an y a z a ' a z u o, b en i z u o, y a s a k e r ɛ l a y e l e t o ' e t i ŋ a w a z u o z a ' a m ɛ.`  
> **IPA:** `[j] [iː] [a], [m] [a] [m] [b] [oː] [Ɂ] [oː] [ɾ] [iː] [j] [ĩ] [ɛ] [f] [a] [ɾ] [a] [j] [eː] [z] [u] [p] [u] [ã] [j] [a] [z] [a] [Ɂ] [a] [z] [u] [oː], [b] [ɛ̃] [iː] [z] [u] [oː], [j] [a] [s] [a] [k] [eː] [ɾ] [ɛ] [l] [a] [j] [eː] [l] [eː] [t] [oː] [Ɂ] [eː] [t] [iː] [ŋ] [a] [w] [a] [z] [u] [oː] [z] [a] [Ɂ] [a] [m] [ɛ].`

> **Text:** Ya sãn ẽŋɛ mam n yele ya se'em na, ya de la mam sɩrdõma.  
> **Phonemes:** `y a s ã n ẽ ŋ ɛ m a m n y e l e y a s e ' e m n a, y a d e l a m a m s ɩ r d õ m a.`  
> **IPA:** `[j] [a] [s] [a]̃ [n] [eː]̃ [ŋ] [ɛ] [m] [a] [m] [n] [j] [eː] [l] [eː] [j] [a] [s] [eː] [Ɂ] [eː] [m] [n] [a], [j] [a] [d] [eː] [l] [a] [m] [a] [m] [s] [ɪ] [ɾ] [d] [oː]̃ [m] [a].`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Fuliiru</b> (<code>flr</code> · Zaire)</summary>

> **Text:** Ee! Umubashu guli mu bagwatira ku kasiizira. Ulusinga, luli mu bananiira ngana-ngana.  
> **Phonemes:** `e e! u m u b a sh u g u l i m u b a g w a t i r a k u k a s i i z i r a. u l u s i ng a, l u l i m u b a n a n i i r a ng a n a-ng a n a.`  
> **IPA:** `e e! u m u b a ʃ u ɡ u l i m u b a ɡ w a t i r a k u k a s i i z i r a. u l u s i ŋɡ a, l u l i m u b a n a n i i r a ŋɡ a n a-ŋɡ a n a.`

> **Text:** Umundu woꞌmwete, ishungi zaage ziri mu múgaza. Si ngiisi úli mu kola naꞌkahimwe-himwe, ali mu yingira mu bukeni.  
> **Phonemes:** `u m u nd u w o ꞌ m w e t e, i sh u ng i z a a g e z i r i m u m ú g a z a. s i ng i i s i ú l i m u k o l a n a ꞌ k a h i m w e-h i m w e, a l i m u y i ng i r a m u b u k e n i.`  
> **IPA:** `u m u nd u w o ꞌ m w e t e, i ʃ u ŋɡ i z a a ɡ e z i r i m u m u ɡ a z a. s i ŋɡ i i s i u l i m u k o l a n a ꞌ k a h i m w e-h i m w e, a l i m u j i ŋɡ i r a m u b u k e n i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ga</b> (<code>gaa</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** kɛ gɔji grɔŋŋi lɛ fɛɛ kɛ gɔŋkpɔi ni kwɔlɔ lɛ fɛɛ.  
> **Phonemes:** `k ɛ g ɔ j i g r ɔ ŋ ŋ i l ɛ f ɛ ɛ k ɛ g ɔ ŋ kp ɔ i n i kw ɔ l ɔ l ɛ f ɛ ɛ.`  
> **IPA:** `k ε ɡ ɔ ʤ i ɡ r ɔ ŋ ŋ i l ε f ε ε k ε ɡ ɔ ŋ kp ɔ i n i kʷ ɔ l ɔ l ε f ε ε.`

> **Text:** Te̱sā mli ehĩo̱ ni dše̱i ewo̱ö̱, te̱sā le̱ nā šõšō̃šõ heni tamo̱ mō̃ le̱ no̱.  
> **Phonemes:** `t e̱ s ā m l i e h ĩ o̱ n i d š e̱ i e w o̱ ö̱, t e̱ s ā l e̱ n ā š õ š ō̃ š õ h e n i t a m o̱ m ō̃ l e̱ n o̱.`  
> **IPA:** `t e s a m l i e h ĩ o n i d s e i e w o o, t e s a l e n a s õ s õ s õ h e n i t a m o m õ l e n o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Gangam</b> (<code>gng</code> · Togo)</summary>

> **Text:** U li cère bɛ ń bɛnde kí ye Uwien cuube, kimɛ n li jo nʼBaa saan, nɛ na ji li lɛ nni.  
> **Phonemes:** `u l i c è r e b ɛ ń b ɛ n d e k í y e u w i e n c u u b e, k i m ɛ n l i j o n ' b a a s a a n, n ɛ n a j i l i l ɛ n n i.`  
> **IPA:** `u l i tʃ e˩ r e b ɛ n˥ b ɛ n d e k i˥ j e u w i e n tʃ u u b e, k i m ɛ n l i dʒ o n ' b a a s a a n, n ɛ n a dʒ i l i l ɛ n n i.`

> **Text:** Nɛn saan nɛ Mari tɔke' utond nnɔ ki ye: «Nì li tien mila kí tuo, kimɛ ma bɛn jɛ-a?».  
> **Phonemes:** `n ɛ n s a a n n ɛ m a r i t ɔ k e ' u t o n d n n ɔ k i y e: «n ì l i t i e n m i l a k í t u o, k i m ɛ m a b ɛ n j ɛ-a?».`  
> **IPA:** `n ɛ n s a a n n ɛ m a r i t ɔ k e ' u t o n d n n ɔ k i j e: «n i˩ l i t i e n m i l a k i˥ t u o, k i m ɛ m a b ɛ n dʒ ɛ-a?».`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Gawar</b> (<code>gou</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Ni azha, ayəw aka səku, aŋghə, ghalani kadək, ni a ŋtəranə jamargwal a cina hayak Ruma vəw?».  
> **Phonemes:** `n i a zh a, a y ə w a k a s ə k u, a ŋg h ə, gh a l a n i k a d ə k, n i a ŋ t ə r a n ə j a m a r gw a l a c i n a h a y a k r u m a v ə w?».`  
> **IPA:** `n ɪ ɑ ʒ ɑ, ɑ j ə w ɑ k ɑ s ə k ʊ, ɑ ᵑɡ x ə, ɣ ɑ l ɑ n ɪ k ɑ d ə k, n ɪ ɑ ŋ t ə r ɑ n ə d͡ʒ ɑ m ɑ r ɡʷ ɑ l ɑ t͡ʃ ɪ n ɑ x ɑ j ɑ k r ʊ m ɑ v ə w?».`

> **Text:** Ma vah watsa ya, Yesu a nda a bəza a gi, ka a ndza tə ka a ma mtil, aska a sərəkatana ma a həji aagi.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a v a h w a ts a y a, y e s u a nd a a b ə z a a g i, k a a ndz a t ə k a a m a m t i l, a s k a a s ə r ə k a t a n a m a a h ə j i a a g i.`  
> **IPA:** `m ɑ v ɑ x w ɑ t͡s ɑ j ɑ, j ɛ s ʊ ɑ ⁿd ɑ ɑ b ə z ɑ ɑ ɡ ɪ, k ɑ ɑ ⁿd͡z ɑ t ə k ɑ ɑ m ɑ m t ɪ l, ɑ s k ɑ ɑ s ə r ə k ɑ t ɑ n ɑ m ɑ ɑ x ə d͡ʒ ɪ ɑ ɑ ɡ ɪ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Gen</b> (<code>gej</code> · BJ)</summary>

> **Text:** Àmè kè ɖo ènunyaa, jina la kpla ènu kpe è; ènunyatɔ ɖona èto ènufiɔfiɔ àdodoewo.  
> **Phonemes:** `à m è k è ɖ o è n un y a a, j in a l a kp l a è n u kp e è; è n un y a t ɔ ɖ o n a è t o è n u f i ɔ f i ɔ à d o d o e w o.`  
> **IPA:** `a m e k e ɖ o e n ũ j a a, ɟ ĩ a l a k͡p l a e n u k͡p e e; e n ũ j a t ɔ ɖ o n a e t o e n u f i ɔ f i ɔ a d o d o e w o.`

> **Text:** Tɔhonɔ̀ miabe Mawu, àpo nyikɔ nyɔ glo lè xixea kpata mè. Àpo gogoa to xixea be gogo ŋuti.  
> **Phonemes:** `t ɔ h o n ɔ̀ m i a b e m a w u, à p o ny i k ɔ ny ɔ g l o l è x i x e a kp a t a m è. à p o g o g o a t o x i x e a b e g o g o ŋ u t i.`  
> **IPA:** `t ɔ h o n ɔ m i a b e m a w u, a p o ɲ i k ɔ ɲ ɔ ɡ l o l e x i x e a k͡p a t a m e. a p o ɡ o ɡ o a t o x i x e a b e ɡ o ɡ o ŋ u t i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Godié</b> (<code>god</code> · CI)</summary>

> **Text:** ‑Lɔɔ ylʋʋ 'kʋ, Zozii wlʋʋ‑ ‑zɔɔ 'nɩ ɔ ‑mʋ 'nyie yëku 'nɩ ɔ ‑kʋ ‑mɔɔ sä.  
> **Phonemes:** `‑l ɔ ɔ y l ʋ ʋ 'k ʋ, z o z i i w l ʋ ʋ‑ ‑z ɔ ɔ 'n ɩ ɔ ‑m ʋ 'ny i e y ë k u 'n ɩ ɔ ‑k ʋ ‑m ɔ ɔ s ä.`  
> **IPA:** `‑l ɔ ɔ j l ʊ ʊ 'k ʊ, z o z i i w l ʊ ʊ‑ ‑z ɔ ɔ 'n ɪ ɔ ‑m ʊ 'ɲ i e j e k u 'n ɪ ɔ ‑k ʊ ‑m ɔ ɔ s a.`

> **Text:** Zozii cii‑ bʋkʋ, ɔ lä sä n 'Bä ‑wää'ä lä nä, sɔɔ n ‑waa'anyɩ lä. Nä, a nämnö 'naa ‑wawʋʋ 'mnö!.  
> **Phonemes:** `z o z i i c i i‑ b ʋ k ʋ, ɔ l ä s ä n 'b ä ‑w ä ä ' ä l ä n ä, s ɔ ɔ n ‑w a a ' a ny ɩ l ä. n ä, a n ä m n ö 'n a a ‑w a w ʋ ʋ 'm n ö!.`  
> **IPA:** `z o z i i c i i‑ b ʊ k ʊ, ɔ l a s a n 'b a ‑w a a ' a l a n a, s ɔ ɔ n ‑w a a ' a ɲ ɪ l a. n a, a n a m n o 'n a a ‑w a w ʊ ʊ 'm n o!.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Gonja</b> (<code>gjn</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** Wu fo kenya kuwɔr nseŋ mɔlga ma, ŋkpal fo kasha nɛ k maŋ kɔ ekar na so.  
> **Phonemes:** `w u f o k e ny a k u w ɔ r n s e ŋ m ɔ l g a m a, ŋ kp a l f o k a sh a n ɛ k m a ŋ k ɔ e k a r n a s o.`

> **Text:** Pɛrez mo nanabiana nɛ b daŋ chena Jerusalɛm daa la basa lempo alfa ana nɛ adeshe nɛ aburwa nna.  
> **Phonemes:** `p ɛ r e z m o n a n a b i a n a n ɛ b d a ŋ ch e n a j e r u s a l ɛ m d aa l a b a s a l e m p o a l f a a n a n ɛ a d e sh e n ɛ a b u r w a n n a.`  
> **IPA:** `p ɛ r e z m o n a n a b i a n a n ɛ b d a ŋ t͡ʃ e n a d͡ʒ e r u s a l ɛ m d aː l a b a s a l e m p o a l f a a n a n ɛ a d e ʃ e n ɛ a b u r w a n n a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Gourmanchéma</b> (<code>gux</code> · BF)</summary>

> **Text:** «Diidi, n gandi Betisaleeli Uli bijua, Huli yaabili, Juda buolu yua.  
> **Phonemes:** `«d i i d i, n g a n d i b e t i s a l e e l i u l i b i j u a, h u l i y a a b i l i, j u d a b u o l u y u a.`  
> **IPA:** `«d i i d i, n ɡ a n d i b e t i s a l e e l i u l i b i ɟ u a, h u l i j a a b i l i, ɟ u d a b u o l u j u a.`

> **Text:** Yinba yaaba n ye foagima, Cengi mani min tieni yaala! Yinba yaaba n ye koanma nni mo N diidi n paalu!».  
> **Phonemes:** `y i n b a y a a b a n y e f o a g i m a, c e n g i m a n i m i n t i e n i y a a l a! y i n b a y a a b a n y e k o a n m a n n i m o n d i i d i n p a a l u!».`  
> **IPA:** `j i n b a j a a b a n j e f o a ɡ i m a, c e n ɡ i m a n i m i n t i e n i j a a l a! j i n b a j a a b a n j e k o a n m a n n i m o n d i i d i n p a a l u!».`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Guinea-Bissau Creole</b> (<code>pov</code> · GM)</summary>

> **Text:** Ña forsa seku kan! Ña lingua pega na jinjirba; bu ditandan na reia di mortu.  
> **Phonemes:** `ñ a f o r s a s e k u k a n! ñ a l i n g u a p e g a n a j i n j i r b a; b u d i t a n d a n n a r e i a d i m o r t u.`  
> **IPA:** `n a f o r s a s e k u k a n! n a l i n ɡ u a p e ɡ a n a ʝ i n ʝ i r b a; b u d i t a n d a n n a r e i a d i m o r t u.`

> **Text:** Ña ermons ku N ama, ka bo straña e forti probason ku bin riba di bos pa spurmenta bos, suma ki kusa strañu ku kontisi.  
> **Phonemes:** `ñ a e r m o n s k u n a m a, k a b o s t r a ñ a e f o r t i p r o b a s o n k u b i n r i b a d i b o s p a s p u r m e n t a b o s, s u m a k i k u s a s t r a ñ u k u k o n t i s i.`  
> **IPA:** `n a e r m o n s k u n a m a, k a b o s t r a n a e f o r t i p r o b a s o n k u b i n r i b a d i b o s p a s p u r m e n t a b o s, s u m a k i k u s a s t r a n u k u k o n t i s i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Gusii</b> (<code>guz</code> · KE)</summary>

> **Text:** Omonene, Inee, tingeecheti abwo bakogeechete? Tinkonyinyera abwo bagosaria asore?.  
> **Phonemes:** `o m o n e n e, i n ee, t i ng ee ch e t i a bw o b a k o g ee ch e t e? t i nk o ny i ny e r a a bw o b a g o s a r i a a s o r e?.`  
> **IPA:** `o m o n e n e, i n eː, t i ⁿɡ eː ʧ e t i a bʷ o β a k o ɣ eː ʧ e t e? t i ⁿk o ɲ i ɲ e ɾ a a bʷ o β a ɣ o s a ɾ i a a s o ɾ e?.`

> **Text:** Ere anyuome omoiseke ore omwekuungi.  
> **Phonemes:** `e r e a ny u o m e o m oi s e k e o r e o mw e k uu ng i.`  
> **IPA:** `e ɾ e a ɲ u o m e o m oi s e k e o ɾ e o mʷ e k uː ⁿɡ i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Gwere</b> (<code>gwr</code> · UG)</summary>

> **Text:** Neegomba ooti atyanu mbe eeyo egimuli njezye okutumula omu ngeri eyeetaagisya olwʼokubba nti atyanu munsobeire.  
> **Phonemes:** `n ee g o m b a oo t i a t y a n u m b e ee y o e g i m u l i n j e z y e o k u t u m u l a o m u n g e r i e y ee t aa g i s y a o l w ' o k u bb a n t i a t y a n u m u n s o b e i r e.`  
> **IPA:** `n eː ɡ o m β ɑ oː t i ɑ t j ɑ n u m β e eː j o e ɡ i m u l i n ɟ e z j e o k u t u m u l ɑ o m u n ɡ e r i e j eː t ɑː ɡ i s j ɑ o l w ' o k u b ɑ n t i ɑ t j ɑ n u m u n s o β e i r e.`

> **Text:** Omuntu oweyabbenganga omu kintu kyonakyona kwʼebyo, yabbutulanga mulwatu ekibbikibbi ekyakolere.  
> **Phonemes:** `o m u n t u o w e y a bb e n g a n g a o m u k i n t u k y o n a k y o n a k w ' e b y o, y a bb u t u l a n g a m u l w a t u e k i bb i k i bb i e k y a k o l e r e.`  
> **IPA:** `o m u n t u o w e j ɑ b e n ɡ ɑ n ɡ ɑ o m u k i n t u k j o n ɑ k j o n ɑ k w ' e β j o, j ɑ b u t u l ɑ n ɡ ɑ m u l w ɑ t u e k i b i k i b i e k j ɑ k o l e r e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Hadiyya</b> (<code>hdy</code> · ET)</summary>

> **Text:** Ihukkaarem ballina ballina lenoommuullakii duramoo gereebbisa xigammoommokii ixxi bikkinatte.  
> **Phonemes:** `i h u k k a a r e m b a l l i n a b a l l i n a l e n o o m m u u l l a k i i d u r a m o o g e r e e b b i s a x i g a m m o o m m o k i i i x x i b i k k i n a t t e.`  
> **IPA:** `i h u k k a a r e m b a l l i n a b a l l i n a l e n o o m m u u l l a k i i d u r a m o o ɡ e r e e b b i s a t’ i ɡ a m m o o m m o k i i i t’ t’ i b i k k i n a t t e.`

> **Text:** Ixxi hurbaata buxichchinne maqire baxxamoo bikkina biijaalli manchi maasse'amaakkoohan ihookko.  
> **Phonemes:** `i x x i h u r b a a t a b u x i ch ch i n n e m a q i r e b a x x a m o o b i k k i n a b i i j a a l l i m a n ch i m a a s s e ' a m a a k k o o h a n i h o o k k o.`  
> **IPA:** `i t’ t’ i h u r b a a t a b u t’ i t͡ʃ t͡ʃ i n n e m a k’ i r e b a t’ t’ a m o o b i k k i n a b i i d͡ʒ a a l l i m a n t͡ʃ i m a a s s e ' a m a a k k o o h a n i h o o k k o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Hanga</b> (<code>hag</code> · Ghana)</summary>

> **Text:** O ti bipogiba bayi la ni ba biisi pa yiya, Liya ni o biisi pa, ka Rachel ni Yisifu pa nyaaŋa.  
> **Phonemes:** `o t i b i p o g i b a b a y i l a n i b a b i i s i p a y i y a, l i y a n i o b i i s i p a, k a r a ch e l n i y i s i f u p a ny a a ŋ a.`  
> **IPA:** `ɔ t ɪ b ɪ p ɔ ɡ ɪ b a b a j ɪ l a n ɪ b a b ɪ ɪ s ɪ p a j ɪ j a, l ɪ j a n ɪ ɔ b ɪ ɪ s ɪ p a, k a d a t͡ʃ ɛ l n ɪ j ɪ s ɪ f ʊ p a ɲ a a ŋ a.`

> **Text:** O n si n nye ba, o yeli, Naawunni ziiziya n ŋa. Ka digili niŋi yuuri Mahanayim.  
> **Phonemes:** `o n s i n ny e b a, o y e l i, n a a w u n n i z i i z i y a n ŋ a. k a d i g i l i n i ŋ i y u u r i m a h a n a y i m.`  
> **IPA:** `ɔ n s ɪ n ɲ ɛ b a, ɔ j ɛ l ɪ, n a a w ʊ n n ɪ z ɪ ɪ z ɪ j a n ŋ a. k a d ɪ ɡ ɪ l ɪ n ɪ ŋ ɪ j ʊ ʊ d ɪ m a h a n a j ɪ m.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Hausa</b> (<code>hau-niger</code> · Niger)</summary>

> **Text:** Sai Bilatus ya tambayi Yesu ya ce, Kai ne sarkin Yahudawa? Yesu ya amsa ya ce, I, haka yake, kamar yadda ka faɗa.  
> **Phonemes:** `s a i b i l a t u s y a t a m b a y i y e s u y a c e, k a i n e s a r k i n y a h u d a w a? y e s u y a a m s a y a c e, i, h a k a y a k e, k a m a r y a d d a k a f a ɗ a.`  
> **IPA:** `s a i b i l a t u s j a t a m b a j i j e s u j a c e, k a i n e s a r k i n j a h u d a w a? j e s u j a a m s a j a c e, i, h a k a j a k e, k a m a r j a d d a k a hʷ a ɗ a.`

> **Text:** Mutum zai iya yi wa kansa alloli? Ai, waɗannan ba alloli ba ne.  
> **Phonemes:** `m u t u m z a i i y a y i w a k a n s a a l l o l i? a i, w a ɗ a n n a n b a a l l o l i b a n e.`  
> **IPA:** `m u t u m z a i i j a j i w a k a n s a a l l o l i? a i, w a ɗ a n n a n b a a l l o l i b a n e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Haya</b> (<code>hay</code> · TZ)</summary>

> **Text:** Oratahire omu sitoa z'eseluji, anga oraboine esitoa z'enjura y'orubare.  
> **Phonemes:** `o r a t a h i r e o m u s i t o a z ' e s e l u j i, a n g a o r a b o i n e e s i t o a z ' e n j u r a y ' o r u b a r e.`  
> **IPA:** `o ɾ a t a h i ɾ e o m u s i t o a z Ɂ e s e l u d͡ʒ i, a n ɡ a o ɾ a b o i n e e s i t o a z Ɂ e n d͡ʒ u ɾ a j Ɂ o ɾ u b a ɾ e.`

> **Text:** Omugorogoki ayenyinya ekili kibi, kandi eby'ababi bakora bibaanaga.  
> **Phonemes:** `o m u g o r o g o k i a y e ny i ny a e k i l i k i b i, k a n d i e b y ' a b a b i b a k o r a b i b aa n a g a.`  
> **IPA:** `o m u ɡ o ɾ o ɡ o k i a j e ɲ i ɲ a e k i l i k i b i, k a n d i e b j Ɂ a b a b i b a k o ɾ a b i b aː n a ɡ a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Igala</b> (<code>igl</code> · NG)</summary>

> **Text:** Awa muda akute̩ adua kpai e̩wñ o̩lO̩jo̩ eche.  
> **Phonemes:** `a w a m u d a a k u t e̩ a d u a kp a i e̩ w ñ o̩ l o̩ j o̩ e ch e.`  
> **IPA:** `a w a m u d a a k u t e a d u a k͡p a i e w n o l o d͡ʒ o e t͡ʃ e.`

> **Text:** I jʼaje̩ ñw mi ñmiñmi n, i muda je̩ ñw efu-mi ko̩ ñw e̩wñ urikoko.  
> **Phonemes:** `i j ' a j e̩ ñ w m i ñ m i ñ m i n, i m u d a j e̩ ñ w e f u-m i k o̩ ñ w e̩ w ñ u r i k o k o.`  
> **IPA:** `i d͡ʒ ' a d͡ʒ e n w m i n m i n m i n, i m u d a d͡ʒ e n w e f u-m i k o n w e w n u r i k o k o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Igbo</b> (<code>ibo</code> · NG)</summary>

> **Text:** Jehova we hu na o wezugara onwe-ya ihu, Chineke we si n'etiti osisi ntà ahu kpọ ya òkù, si, Moses, Moses. Ọ si, Lekwam.  
> **Phonemes:** `j e h o v a w e h u n a o w e z u g a r a o nw e-y a i h u, ch i n e k e w e s i n ' e t i t i o s i s i n t à a h u kp ọ y a ò k ù, s i, m o s e s, m o s e s. ọ s i, l e kw a m.`  
> **IPA:** `ʤ e ɦ o v a w e ɦ u n a o w e z u ɡ a ɹ a o ŋʷ e-j a i ɦ u, ʧ i n e k e w e s i n ' e t i t i o s i s i n t a a ɦ u ɓ̥ o j a o k u, s i, m o s e s, m o s e s. o s i, l e kʷ a m.`

> **Text:** Ma ha egeghị ntị, maọbụ kpọọ ya mkpa ha. Ha bụ ndị ntị ike, ndị na-adịghị aṅa ntị nʼịdọ aka na ntị, maọbụ nabata ya.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a h a e g e gh ị n t ị, m a ọ b ụ kp ọ ọ y a m kp a h a. h a b ụ n d ị n t ị i k e, n d ị n a-a d ị gh ị a ṅ a n t ị n ' ị d ọ a k a n a n t ị, m a ọ b ụ n a b a t a y a.`  
> **IPA:** `m a ɦ a e ɡ e ɣ i n t i, m a o b u ɓ̥ o o j a m ɓ̥ a ɦ a. ɦ a b u n d i n t i i k e, n d i n a-a d i ɣ i a n a n t i n ' i d o a k a n a n t i, m a o b u n a b a t a j a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Igede</b> (<code>ige</code> · Nigeria)</summary>

> **Text:** Ọwa-à ri Amkpaa Ohe Oluhye nyahị lẹ, ehile nya ọpyị ọkpakpa nyamwụ ri inya ala odehe wuu.  
> **Phonemes:** `ọ w a-à r i a m kp a a o h e o l u h y e ny a h ị l ẹ, e h i l e ny a ọ p y ị ọ kp a kp a ny a m w ụ r i i ny a a l a o d e h e w u u.`  
> **IPA:** `o w ə-ə˩ r i ə m k͡p ə ə o h e o l u h j e ɲ ə h i l e, e h i l e ɲ ə o p j i o k͡p ə k͡p ə ɲ ə m w u r i i ɲ ə ə l ə o d e h e w u u.`

> **Text:** Ọpyị ee, ọpyị, ọpyị, wo ẹla ká Amkpaa ya lẹẹ yẹkẹẹ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ọ p y ị e e, ọ p y ị, ọ p y ị, w o ẹ l a k á a m kp a a y a l ẹ ẹ y ẹ k ẹ ẹ.`  
> **IPA:** `o p j i e e, o p j i, o p j i, w o e l ə k ə˥ ə m k͡p ə ə j ə l e e j e k e e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ikwerre</b> (<code>ikw</code> · NG)</summary>

> **Text:** Ọkpa nka nụ be kpáge nka zá nhne ọ bụ, nhne zị mkpa bụ ọbita iwu Chiokike.  
> **Phonemes:** `ọ k p a n k a n ụ b e k p á g e n k a z á n h n e ọ b ụ, n h n e z ị m k p a b ụ ọ b i t a i w u ch i o k i k e.`  
> **IPA:** `o k p a n k a n u b e k p a ɡ e n k a z a n h n e o b u, n h n e z i m k p a b u o b i t a i w u t͡ʃ i o k i k e.`

> **Text:** Ọ bụru nụ m váge m kwunu be ọka, ikpe mmehie báamana be. Kịtna be jeeji kịni gọhoru ẹhni be hite nụ mmehie?.  
> **Phonemes:** `ọ b ụ r u n ụ m v á g e m kw u n u b e ọ k a, i k p e m m e h i e b á a m a n a b e. k ị t n a b e j e e j i k ị n i g ọ h o r u ẹ h n i b e h i t e n ụ m m e h i e?.`  
> **IPA:** `o b u ɾ u n u m v a ɡ e m kʷ u n u b e o k a, i k p e m m e h i e b a a m a n a b e. k i t n a b e d͡ʒ e e d͡ʒ i k i n i ɡ o h o ɾ u e h n i b e h i t e n u m m e h i e?.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Iraqw</b> (<code>irk</code> · TZ)</summary>

> **Text:** Yesu giri báy, «Án Neetlaangw ugwa ár, i gawaá doorí wa /e/eetín adór mankari.  
> **Phonemes:** `y e s u g i r i b á y, «á n n ee tl aa ngw u gw a á r, i g a w aá d oo r í w a /e/ee t í n a d ó r m a n k a r i.`  
> **IPA:** `y e s u ɡ i r i b a y, «a n n eː t͡ɫ’ aː ŋʷ u ɡʷ a a r, i ɡ a w aː d oː r i w a /e/eː t i n a d o r m a n k a r i.`

> **Text:** Baraá guruú doó Yosefuwo tlahhoó Efraimu, geeraharusmo a Kemueli garmoó Shiftani.  
> **Phonemes:** `b a r aá g u r uú d oó y o s e f u w o tl a hh oó e f r a i m u, g ee r a h a r u s m o a k e m u e l i g a r m oó sh i f t a n i.`  
> **IPA:** `b a r aː ɡ u r uː d oː y o s e f u w o t͡ɫ’ a ħ oː e f r a i m u, ɡ eː r a h a r u s m o a k e m u e l i ɡ a r m oː ʃ i f t a n i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Jibu</b> (<code>jib</code> · NG)</summary>

> **Text:** Yesu dinya mpìr Yahudawaká, ká bir sig pìkyinnbá á byarwá ni n rag, Wà na wib hing jírmí, ning í yann sukàká bumí àjai.  
> **Phonemes:** `y e s u d i ny a m p ì r y a h u d a w a k á, k á b i r s i g p ì ky i n n b á á by a r w á n i n r a g, w à n a w i b h i ng j í r m í, n i ng í y a n n s u k à k á b u m í à j a i.`  
> **IPA:** `j e s u d i ɲ a m p i r j a h u d a w a k a, k a b i r s i ɡ p i kʲ i n n b a a bʲ a r w a n i n r a ɡ, w a n a w i b h i ŋ ʒ i r m i, n i ŋ i j a n n s u k a k a b u m i a ʒ a i.`

> **Text:** Yesu dinya big n rag, Ayúnn-à sa hing wà bá swam yan mì Wùn Mpìr Jíkwìnn.  
> **Phonemes:** `y e s u d i ny a b i g n r a g, a y ú n n-à s a h i ng w à b á sw a m y a n m ì w ù n m p ì r j í kw ì n n.`  
> **IPA:** `j e s u d i ɲ a b i ɡ n r a ɡ, a j u n n-a s a h i ŋ w a b a sʷ a m j a n m i w u n m p i r ʒ i kʷ i n n.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Jur Modo</b> (<code>bex</code> · SS)</summary>

> **Text:** Bo ileki kititi lïjë, iya te, Ikalike bɔ mɔtɔ rɔ Labana kole ꞌba Nawura? Lïjë iya te, Yëë, dikali bo.  
> **Phonemes:** `b o i l e k i k i t i t i l ï j ë, i y a t e, i k a l i k e b ɔ m ɔ t ɔ r ɔ l a b a n a k o l e ꞌ b a n a w u r a? l ï j ë i y a t e, y ë ë, d i k a l i b o.`  
> **IPA:** `b ʊ ɪ l ɛ k ɪ k ɪ t ɪ t ɪ l ɪ ɟ ɛ, ɪ j a t ɛ, ɪ k a l ɪ k ɛ b ɔ m ɔ t ɔ r ɔ l a b a n a k ʊ l ɛ ꞌ b a n a w u r a? l ɪ ɟ ɛ ɪ j a t ɛ, j ɛ ɛ, d ɪ k a l ɪ b ʊ.`

> **Text:** Kina Bɔkoꞌba kako ŋgï bërï di mïtɔrɔ ti pɔli kɔrɔ ŋgila Musa yayi kïdëkï möyï koro abo mo tönë rɔ ŋere Bɔkoꞌba ne.  
> **Phonemes:** `k i n a b ɔ k o ꞌ b a k a k o ŋg ï b ë r ï d i m ï t ɔ r ɔ t i p ɔ l i k ɔ r ɔ ŋg i l a m u s a y a y i k ï d ë k ï m ö y ï k o r o a b o m o t ö n ë r ɔ ŋ e r e b ɔ k o ꞌ b a n e.`  
> **IPA:** `k ɪ n a b ɔ k ʊ ꞌ b a k a k ʊ ᵑɡ ɪ b ɛ r ɪ d ɪ m ɪ t ɔ r ɔ t ɪ p ɔ l ɪ k ɔ r ɔ ᵑɡ ɪ l a m u s a j a j ɪ k ɪ d ɛ k ɪ m ʊ j ɪ k ʊ r ʊ a b ʊ m ʊ t ʊ n ɛ r ɔ ŋ ɛ r ɛ b ɔ k ʊ ꞌ b a n ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kabiye</b> (<code>kbp</code> · BJ)</summary>

> **Text:** Toovenim lɛ, meheyiɣ-mɩ se: yee ɛna-ɩ yɔ, ɛ-taa hʋlʋmʋʋnɩ-ɩ nɛ pɩkɩlɩ kʋɖʋm fɛyɩ mɩnʋʋ weyi ɩkazaa yɔ.  
> **Phonemes:** `t oo v e n i m l ɛ, m e h e y iɣ-m ɩ s e: y ee ɛ n a-ɩ y ɔ, ɛ-t aa h ʋ l ʋ m ʋʋ n ɩ-ɩ n ɛ p ɩ k ɩ l ɩ k ʋ ɖ ʋ m f ɛ y ɩ m ɩ n ʋʋ w e y i ɩ k a z aa y ɔ.`  
> **IPA:** `[t] [oː] [v] [e] [n] [i] [m] [l] [ɛ], [m] [e] [h] [e] [j] [ɯ̘ː]-[m] [ɪ] [s] [e]: [j] [eː] [ɛ] [n] [a]-[ɪ] [j] [ɔ], [ɛ]-[t] [aː] [h] [ʊ] [l] [ʊ] [m] [ʊː] [n] [ɪ]-[ɪ] [n] [ɛ] [p] [ɪ] [k] [ɪ] [l] [ɪ] [k] [ʊ] [ʈ] [ʊ] [m] [f] [ɛ] [j] [ɪ] [m] [ɪ] [n] [ʊː] [w] [e] [j] [i] [ɪ] [k] [a] [z] [aː] [j] [ɔ].`

> **Text:** Ma lɛ, mɔnkɔm kedeŋa yɔɔ ɛzɩ ñalɩmɩyɛ yɔ se nɔɔyʋ ɛwɛnɩ lidaʋ mɔ-yɔɔ lɛ, ɛtaawɛɛ cɩkpɛndʋʋ taa.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a l ɛ, m ɔ n k ɔ m k e d e ŋ a y ɔɔ ɛ z ɩ ñ a l ɩ m ɩ y ɛ y ɔ s e n ɔɔ y ʋ ɛ w ɛ n ɩ l i d a ʋ m ɔ-y ɔɔ l ɛ, ɛ t aa w ɛɛ c ɩ kp ɛ n d ʋʋ t aa.`  
> **IPA:** `[m] [a] [l] [ɛ], [m] [ɔ] [n] [k] [ɔ] [m] [k] [e] [d] [e] [ŋ] [a] [j] [ɔː] [ɛ] [z] [ɪ] [n] [a] [l] [ɪ] [m] [ɪ] [j] [ɛ] [j] [ɔ] [s] [e] [n] [ɔː] [j] [ʊ] [ɛ] [w] [ɛ] [n] [ɪ] [l] [i] [d] [a] [ʊ] [m] [ɔ]-[j] [ɔː] [l] [ɛ], [ɛ] [t] [aː] [w] [ɛː] [t͡ʃ] [ɪ] [k͡p] [ɛ] [n] [d] [ʊː] [t] [aː].`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kabyle</b> (<code>kab</code> · DZ)</summary>

> **Text:** mi yenna : Ad ak-fkeɣ tamurt n Kanɛan, d amur n lweṛt ara tkesbem.  
> **Phonemes:** `m i y e n n a : a d a k-f k e ɣ t a m u r t n k a n ɛ a n, d a m u r n l w e ṛ t a r a t k e s b e m.`  
> **IPA:** `m i j ə n n a : a ð a k-f k ə ʁ t a m u r t n k a n ʕ a n, ð a m u r n l w ə r t a r a t k ə s bʷ ə m.`

> **Text:** axaṭer d amdan ur nesɛi ara yiwen n ṛṛay, ur nerkid ara deg iberdan-is meṛṛa.  
> **Phonemes:** `a x a ṭ e r d a m d a n u r n e s ɛ i a r a y i w e n n ṛ ṛ a y, u r n e r k i d a r a d e g i b e r d a n-i s m e ṛ ṛ a.`  
> **IPA:** `a χ a t ə r ð a m ð a n u r n ə s ʕ i a r a j i w ə n n r r a j, u r n ə r k i ð a r a ð ə ɡ i bʷ ə r ð a n-i s m ə r r a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kafa</b> (<code>kbr</code> · ET)</summary>

> **Text:** Hini dachechi heciyechocho, ashoon tiichiibeeti yibbaatoon shunnehine.  
> **Phonemes:** `h i n i d a c h e c h i h e c i y e c h o c h o, a s h oo n t ii c h ii b ee t i y i b b aa t oo n s h u n n e h i n e.`  
> **IPA:** `h i n i d a t͡ʃ h ɛ t͡ʃ h i h ɛ t͡ʃ i j ɛ t͡ʃ h ɔ t͡ʃ h ɔ, a s h ɔː n t iː t͡ʃ h iː b ɛː t i j i b b aː t ɔː n s h u n n ɛ h i n ɛ.`

> **Text:** Doono Yeero, ne giidoona ooge bekkebe! Ne hakkoyich shodde, neen galletemmo noone.  
> **Phonemes:** `d oo n o y ee r o, n e g ii d oo n a oo g e b e k k e b e! n e h a k k o y i c h s h o d d e, n ee n g a l l e t e m m o n oo n e.`  
> **IPA:** `d ɔː n ɔ j ɛː r ɔ, n ɛ ɡ iː d ɔː n a ɔː ɡ ɛ b ɛ k k ɛ b ɛ! n ɛ h a k k ɔ j i t͡ʃ h s h ɔ d d ɛ, n ɛː n ɡ a l l ɛ t ɛ m m ɔ n ɔː n ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kar</b> (<code>xrb</code> · BF)</summary>

> **Text:** Yesu kaplãŋ ngĩĩ naa le yar, gbã mpãy ter syi u n pai ku.  
> **Phonemes:** `y e s u k a p l ã ŋ ng ĩ ĩ n aa l e y a r, gb ã mp ã y t e r s y i u n p a i k u.`  
> **IPA:** `j e s u k a p l ã ŋ ᵑɡ ĩ ĩ n aː l e j a r, ɡ͡b ã m͡p ã j t e r s j i u n p a i k u.`

> **Text:** N pa n n pai dya cɛr u tuhufua na; n n pai pueesya cɛr u nahafua na; n n pai cɔɔ cɛr u nansya na.  
> **Phonemes:** `n p a n n p a i d y a c ɛ r u t u h u f u a n a; n n p a i p u ee s y a c ɛ r u n a h a f u a n a; n n p a i c ɔ ɔ c ɛ r u n a n s y a n a.`  
> **IPA:** `n p a n n p a i d j a c ɛ r u t u h u f u a n a; n n p a i p u eː s j a c ɛ r u n a h a f u a n a; n n p a i c ɔ ɔ c ɛ r u n a n s j a n a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Karamojong</b> (<code>kdj</code> · KE)</summary>

> **Text:** pa abu Ekapolon tocamunos ka atapapaa yok nai ka iwon daadaŋ ŋulu ikiya ne ŋulu ikiyari tokona.  
> **Phonemes:** `p a a b u e k a p o l o n t o c a m u n o s k a a t a p a p a a y o k n a i k a i w o n d a a d a ŋ ŋ u l u i k i y a n e ŋ u l u i k i y a r i t o k o n a.`  
> **IPA:** `p a a b u e k a p o l o n t o t͡ʃ a m u n o s k a a t a p a p a a j o k n a i k a i w o n d a a d a ŋ ŋ u l u i k i j a n e ŋ u l u i k i j a r i t o k o n a.`

> **Text:** Toboŋok esorokit ŋolo apolokinit ŋikalemak tema, Erae iŋes asorokit ŋina arukunete ka Naomi alo Moab.  
> **Phonemes:** `t o b o ŋ o k e s o r o k i t ŋ o l o a p o l o k i n i t ŋ i k a l e m a k t e m a, e r a e i ŋ e s a s o r o k i t ŋ i n a a r u k u n e t e k a n a o m i a l o m o a b.`  
> **IPA:** `t o b o ŋ o k e s o r o k i t ŋ o l o a p o l o k i n i t ŋ i k a l e m a k t e m a, e r a e i ŋ e s a s o r o k i t ŋ i n a a r u k u n e t e k a n a o m i a l o m o a b.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kasem</b> (<code>xsm</code> · BF)</summary>

> **Text:** «Wɛ paarɩ dɩm nyɩ dɩ pɛ wʋdoŋ na lagɩ sɩ o kɩ kadiri candiǝ o pa o bu te tɩn.  
> **Phonemes:** `«w ɛ p a a r ɩ d ɩ m ny ɩ d ɩ p ɛ w ʋ d o ŋ n a l a g ɩ s ɩ o k ɩ k a d i r i c a n d i ǝ o p a o b u t e t ɩ n.`  
> **IPA:** `«w ɛ p a a r ɪ d ɪ m ɲ ɪ d ɪ p ɛ w ʊ d o ŋ n a l a ɡ ɪ s ɪ o k ɪ k a d i r i t̠ʃ a n d i ə o p a o b u t e t ɪ n.`

> **Text:** Yé pa-na dé faŋe Baŋa Wɛ dam dé nii, ne banto badaara déem na ke tei ye basankwe done-ba se go to.  
> **Phonemes:** `y é p a-n a d é f a ŋ e b a ŋ a w ɛ d a m d é n i i, n e b a n t o b a d a a r a d é e m n a k e t e i y e b a s a n k w e d o n e-b a s e g o t o.`  
> **IPA:** `j e p a-n a d e f a ŋ e b a ŋ a w ɛ d a m d e n i i, n e b a n t o b a d a a r a d e e m n a k e t e i j e b a s a n k w e d o n e-b a s e ɡ o t o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Keliko</b> (<code>kbo</code> · CD)</summary>

> **Text:** Ĩsãkárã, mi adri sụ̃ dõngí ũkpõ be laápi ãndẽ vuúpi jũrú ị̃rị̃ rĩ pi ãsámvú gé rĩ tị́nị.  
> **Phonemes:** `ĩ s ã k á r ã, m i a dr i s ụ̃ d õ ng í ũ kp õ b e l a á p i ã nd ẽ v u ú p i j ũ r ú ị̃ r ị̃ r ĩ p i ã s á mv ú g é r ĩ t ị́ n ị.`  
> **IPA:** `ɪ s a k a r a, m ɪ a ɖ ɪ s ʊ d ɔ ᵑɡ ɪ ʊ k͡p ɔ b ɛ l a a p ɪ a ⁿd ɛ v ʊ ʊ p ɪ dz ʊ r ʊ ɪ r ɪ r ɪ p ɪ a s a ᶬv ʊ ɡ ɛ r ɪ t ɪ n ɪ.`

> **Text:** Kộpi nị̃kí ãnyãngã Yẹ́sụ̃ ní sẽé vaápi ꞌdĩri vé ífífí kuyé, ọ̃zụ̃kí kộpivé úmĩ rá, sẽ kộpi nị̃kí tã ꞌdĩri ámá kuyé.  
> **Phonemes:** `k ộ p i n ị̃ k í ã ny ã ng ã y ẹ́ s ụ̃ n í s ẽ é v a á p i ꞌ d ĩ r i v é í f í f í k u y é, ọ̃ z ụ̃ k í k ộ p i v é ú m ĩ r á, s ẽ k ộ p i n ị̃ k í t ã ꞌ d ĩ r i á m á k u y é.`  
> **IPA:** `k ɔ p ɪ n ɪ k ɪ a ɲ a ᵑɡ a j ɛ s ʊ n ɪ s ɛ ɛ v a a p ɪ ꞌ d ɪ r ɪ v ɛ ɪ f ɪ f ɪ k ʊ j ɛ, ɔ z ʊ k ɪ k ɔ p ɪ v ɛ ʊ m ɪ r a, s ɛ k ɔ p ɪ n ɪ k ɪ t a ꞌ d ɪ r ɪ a m a k ʊ j ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kera</b> (<code>ker</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Bədoona ana kəsaananti, mindi mindi mo jəə la bə əsə-isi tiinə Pepeŋa damdi wə a bə əsə-isi ana bə minti wə a məntunuŋ.  
> **Phonemes:** `b ə d oo n a a n a k ə s aa n a n t i, m i n d i m i n d i m o j əə l a b ə ə s ə-i s i t ii n ə p e p e ŋ a d a m d i w ə a b ə ə s ə-i s i a n a b ə m i n t i w ə a m ə n t u n u ŋ.`  
> **IPA:** `b ə d oː n a a n a k ə s aː n a n t i, m i n d i m i n d i m o ɟ əː l a b ə ə s ə-i s i t iː n ə p e p e ŋ a d a m d i w ə a b ə ə s ə-i s i a n a b ə m i n t i w ə a m ə n t u n u ŋ.`

> **Text:** Náŋ ha'aŋ, áŋ gə asə-ase minti áŋ kə Pepeŋ. Səma seŋkaŋ keɗe kasi Nər Sərkəŋa.  
> **Phonemes:** `n á ŋ h a ' a ŋ, á ŋ g ə a s ə-a s e m i n t i á ŋ k ə p e p e ŋ. s ə m a s e ŋ k a ŋ k e ɗ e k a s i n ə r s ə r k ə ŋ a.`  
> **IPA:** `n a ŋ h a ' a ŋ, a ŋ ɡ ə a s ə-a s e m i n t i a ŋ k ə p e p e ŋ. s ə m a s e ŋ k a ŋ k e ɗ e k a s i n ə r s ə r k ə ŋ a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kikuyu</b> (<code>kik</code> · KE)</summary>

> **Text:** na icicio cia kwĩrora, na nguo cia gatani ĩrĩa njega, na itambaya, na macuka ma ciande.  
> **Phonemes:** `n a i c i c i o c i a k w ĩ r o r a, n a ng u o c i a g a t a n i ĩ r ĩ a nj e g a, n a i t a mb a y a, n a m a c u k a m a c i a nd e.`  
> **IPA:** `n a i ʃ i ʃ i ɔ ʃ i a k w i ɾ ɔ ɾ a, n a ᵑɡ u ɔ ʃ i a ɣ a t a n i i ɾ i a ᶮd͡ʒ ɛ ɣ a, n a i t a ᵐb a j a, n a m a ʃ u k a m a ʃ i a ⁿd ɛ.`

> **Text:** matwarwo kũndũ kũrĩ na thaayũ. Andũ arĩa matũũraga ũtũũro wa ũthingu, makua matwaragwo makahuurũke.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a t w a r w o k ũ nd ũ k ũ r ĩ n a th a a y ũ. a nd ũ a r ĩ a m a t ũ ũ r a g a ũ t ũ ũ r o w a ũ th i ng u, m a k u a m a t w a r a g w o m a k a h u u r ũ k e.`  
> **IPA:** `m a t w a ɾ w ɔ k u ⁿd u k u ɾ i n a ð a a j u. a ⁿd u a ɾ i a m a t u u ɾ a ɣ a u t u u ɾ ɔ w a u ð i ᵑɡ u, m a k u a m a t w a ɾ a ɣ w ɔ m a k a h u u ɾ u k ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kili</b> (<code>khy</code> · CD)</summary>

> **Text:** Nda batoi ba bato batotina Inde loonga la baeki bande mbo—.  
> **Phonemes:** `n d a b a t o i b a b a t o b a t o t i n a i n d e l o o n g a l a b a e k i b a n d e m b o—.`  
> **IPA:** `n d a b a t o i b a b a t o b a t o t i n a i n d e l o o n ɡ a l a b a e k i b a n d e m b o—.`

> **Text:** Lamao Yesu loonga la eba bikuke la baeki bande.  
> **Phonemes:** `l a m a o y e s u l o o n g a l a e b a b i k u k e l a b a e k i b a n d e.`  
> **IPA:** `l a m a o u e s u l o o n ɡ a l a e b a b i k u k e l a b a e k i b a n d e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kimbundu</b> (<code>kmb</code> · AO)</summary>

> **Text:** Muthu uala mufundi se ua mu bazela lumoxi ni luiadi, mu ambule.  
> **Phonemes:** `m u t h u u a l a m u f u nd i s e u a m u b a z e l a l u m o x i n i l u i a d i, m u a mb u l e.`  
> **IPA:** `m u~w t h u~w u~w a l a m u~w f u~w ⁿd i~j s e u~w a m u~w b a z e l a l u~w m o ʃ i~j n i~j l u~w i~j a d i~j, m u~w a ᵐb u~w l e.`

> **Text:** Muiuki uinga o muenhu ua ibhaku iê; maji o muẋima ua athu abhumu-phe, uezala usuéia.  
> **Phonemes:** `m u i u k i u i ng a o m u e n h u u a i b h a k u i ê; m a j i o m u ẋ i m a u a a t h u a b h u m u-p h e, u e z a l a u s u é i a.`  
> **IPA:** `m u~w i~j u~w k i~j u~w i~j ᵑɡ a o m u~w e n h u~w u~w a i~j b h a k u~w i~j e; m a ʒ i~j o m u~w ʃ i~j m a u~w a a t h u~w a b h u~w m u~w-p h e, u~w e z a l a u~w s u~w e i~j a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kinyarwanda</b> (<code>kin</code> · BI)</summary>

> **Text:** kuko ushaka kurengera ubugingo bwe azabubura, ariko utīta ku bugingo bwe ku bwanjye ni we uzabukiza.  
> **Phonemes:** `k u k o u s h a k a k u r e n g e r a u b u g i n g o bw e a z a b u b u r a, a r i k o u t ī t a k u b u g i n g o bw e k u bw a n jy e n i w e u z a b u k i z a.`  
> **IPA:** `k u k o u s h a k a k u ɾ e n ɡ e ɾ a u β u ɡ i n ɡ o bɡ e a z a β u β u ɾ a, a ɾ i k o u t i t a k u β u ɡ i n ɡ o bɡ e k u bɡ a n ɟ e n i w e u z a β u k i z a.`

> **Text:** Nyagasani, ibyo nifuza urabizi byose, ntuyobewe uko mpora nsuhuza umutima.  
> **Phonemes:** `ny a g a s a n i, i by o n i f u z a u r a b i z i by o s e, n t u y o b e w e u k o m p o r a n s u h u z a u m u t i m a.`  
> **IPA:** `ɲ a ɡ a s a n i, i bɟ o n i f u z a u ɾ a β i z i bɟ o s e, n t u j o β e w e u k o m p o ɾ a n s u h u z a u m u t i m a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kirundi</b> (<code>run</code> · BI)</summary>

> **Text:** kugira ngo ntibantabagure nkintambwe ngo bampimbaguremwo ibihimba ata wo kuntabara.  
> **Phonemes:** `k u g i r a n g o nt i b a nt a b a g u r e nk i nt a mb w e n g o b a mp i mb a g u r e m w o i b i h i mb a a t a w o k u nt a b a r a.`  
> **IPA:** `[k] [u] [ɡ] [i] [ɾ] [a] [n] [ɡ] [o] [nh] [i] [β] [a] [nh] [a] [β] [a] [ɡ] [u] [ɾ] [e] [ŋx] [i] [nh] [a] [ᵐb] [w] [e] [n] [ɡ] [o] [β] [a] [ᵐp] [i] [ᵐb] [a] [ɡ] [u] [ɾ] [e] [m] [w] [o] [i] [β] [i] [h] [i] [ᵐb] [a] [a] [t] [a] [w] [o] [k] [u] [nh] [a] [β] [a] [ɾ] [a].`

> **Text:** Uzosoneshereze Aroni mukurwawe imyambaro yejejwe, imushariza ikamuhesha icubahiro.  
> **Phonemes:** `u z o s o n e sh e r e z e a r o n i m u k u rw a w e i m y a mb a r o y e j e j w e, i m u sh a r i z a i k a m u h e sh a i c u b a h i r o.`  
> **IPA:** `[u] [ᵈz] [o] [s] [o] [n] [e] [ʃ] [e] [ɾ] [e] [ᵈz] [e] [a] [ɾ] [o] [n] [i] [m] [u] [k] [u] [ɾᵍw] [a] [w] [e] [i] [m] [j] [a] [ᵐb] [a] [ɾ] [o] [j] [e] [d͡ʒ] [e] [d͡ʒ] [w] [e], [i] [m] [u] [ʃ] [a] [ɾ] [i] [ᵈz] [a] [i] [k] [a] [m] [u] [h] [e] [ʃ] [a] [i] [t͡ʃ] [u] [β] [a] [h] [i] [ɾ] [o].`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kissi</b> (<code>kqs</code> · GN)</summary>

> **Text:** Ndu bii yoomu ɲaa yoomaa kpow o ba ni, ndu bii hoeyii yoomuɛi vanndacieea kpow o ba ni.  
> **Phonemes:** `nd u b ii y oo m u ɲ aa y oo m aa kp o w o b a n i, nd u b ii h o e y ii y oo m u ɛ i v a n nd a c i ee a kp o w o b a n i.`  
> **IPA:** `ⁿd u b iː j oː m u ɲ aː j oː m aː k͡p o w o b a n i, ⁿd u b iː h o e j iː j oː m u ɛ i v a n ⁿd a t͡ɕ i eː a k͡p o w o b a n i.`

> **Text:** Mí langbanɔ kon muli aa: «Te lende ni, mɛɛ i tiwnin ba le, yɔŋii kɛ́ nuu laasian. Te nɔm co pɛ baŋa, mɛɛ baŋa.».  
> **Phonemes:** `m í l a n gb a n ɔ k o n m u l i aa: «t e l e nd e n i, m ɛɛ i t i w n i n b a l e, y ɔ ŋ ii k ɛ́ n uu l aa s i a n. t e n ɔ m c o p ɛ b a ŋ a, m ɛɛ b a ŋ a.».`  
> **IPA:** `m i l a n ɡ͡b a n ɔ k o n m u l i aː: «t e l e ⁿd e n i, m ɛː i t i w n i n b a l e, j ɔ ŋ iː k ɛ n uː l aː s i a n. t e n ɔ m t͡ɕ o p ɛ b a ŋ a, m ɛː b a ŋ a.».`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kituba</b> (<code>mkw</code> · CG)</summary>

> **Text:** «Konso kilumbu, nge ke banda na kupesaka na kiyokolo ya minkayulu, bana zole ya mameme ya mvula mosi, bantangu nyonso.  
> **Phonemes:** `«k o ns o k i l u mb u, ng e k e b a nd a n a k u p e s a k a n a k i y o k o l o y a m i nk a y u l u, b a n a z o l e y a m a m e m e y a mv u l a m o s i, b a nt a ng u n y o ns o.`  
> **IPA:** `«k o ⁿs o k i l u ᵐb u, ᵑɡ e k e b a ⁿd a n a k u p e s~ɕ a k a n a k i j o k o l o j a m i ᵑk a j u l u, b a n a z~ʑ o l e j a m a m e m e j a ᶬv u l a m o s~ɕ i, b a ⁿt a ᵑɡ u n j o ⁿs o.`

> **Text:** Yandi yina ke zingaka na mazulu ke na kuseka. Yave ke na kuseka bawu.  
> **Phonemes:** `y a nd i y i n a k e z i ng a k a n a m a z u l u k e n a k u s e k a. y a v e k e n a k u s e k a b a w u.`  
> **IPA:** `j a ⁿd i j i n a k e z~ʑ i ᵑɡ a k a n a m a z~ʑ u l u k e n a k u s~ɕ e k a. j a v e k e n a k u s~ɕ e k a b a w u.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Konkomba</b> (<code>xon</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** aan nimi aawakor moo pɔr kpee, aan ki kan tikunabr, ni salmaa, aan nimi aawan mɔmɔk moo kpee kan.  
> **Phonemes:** `a a n n i m i a a w a k o r m o o p ɔ r kp e e, a a n k i k a n t i k u n a b r, n i s a l m a a, a a n n i m i a a w a n m ɔ m ɔ k m o o kp e e k a n.`  
> **IPA:** `a a n n i m i a a w a k o r m o o p ɔ r k͡p e e, a a n k i k a n t i k u n a b r, n i s a l m a a, a a n n i m i a a w a n m ɔ m ɔ k m o o k͡p e e k a n.`

> **Text:** Uwumbɔr, m wii mpɔɔn pu ki yin si la, ba pu? timoor ni isui mɔmɔk kuur, ke mmii le aah wun ni mɔmɔk na.  
> **Phonemes:** `u w u m b ɔ r, m w i i m p ɔ ɔ n p u k i y i n s i l a, b a p u? t i m o o r n i i s u i m ɔ m ɔ k k u u r, k e m m i i l e a a h w u n n i m ɔ m ɔ k n a.`  
> **IPA:** `u w u m b ɔ r, m w i i m p ɔ ɔ n p u k i j i n s i l a, b a p u? t i m o o r n i i s u i m ɔ m ɔ k k u u r, k e m m i i l e a a h w u n n i m ɔ m ɔ k n a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Krio</b> (<code>kri</code> · Sierra Leone)</summary>

> **Text:** PAPA GƆD go brok pɔsin we de kak in fɛda in os, bɔt i nɔ go ambɔgin uman we in man dɔn day.  
> **Phonemes:** `p a p a g ɔ d g o b r o k p ɔ s i n w e d e k a k i n f ɛ d a i n o s, b ɔ t i n ɔ g o a m b ɔ g i n u m a n w e i n m a n d ɔ n d ay.`  
> **IPA:** `p a p a ɡ ɔ d ɡ o b r o k p ɔ s i n w e d e k a k i n f ɛ d a i n o s, b ɔ t i n ɔ ɡ o a m b ɔ ɡ i n u m a n w e i n m a n d ɔ n d ai.`

> **Text:** PAPA GƆD de wach ɔl udat de du tin tret wan ɛn de yɛri we dɛn de kray;.  
> **Phonemes:** `p a p a g ɔ d d e w a ch ɔ l u d a t d e d u t i n t r e t w a n ɛ n d e y ɛ r i w e d ɛ n d e k r ay;.`  
> **IPA:** `p a p a ɡ ɔ d d e w a tʃ ɔ l u d a t d e d u t i n t r e t w a n ɛ n d e j ɛ r i w e d ɛ n d e k r ai;.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kunda</b> (<code>kdn</code> · MZ)</summary>

> **Text:** ukakhale kumweko kwakanthawe mpaka ukali wankulu wako wamala.  
> **Phonemes:** `u k a kh a l e k u m w e k o k w a k a n th a w e m p a k a u k a l i w a n k u l u w a k o w a m a l a.`  
> **IPA:** `u k a kʰ a l e k u m w e k o k w a k a n tʰ a w e m p a k a u k a l i w a n k u l u w a k o w a m a l a.`

> **Text:** Koma Loti adati kwayiwo, Nee, azimbuye wangu.  
> **Phonemes:** `k o m a l o t i a d a t i k w a y i w o, n ee, a z i mb u y e w a ng u.`  
> **IPA:** `k o m a l o t i a d a t i k w a j i w o, n eː, a z i ᵐb u j e w a ŋ u.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kuria</b> (<code>kuj</code> · KE)</summary>

> **Text:** Ömögambi Sarimanesa wa Asuru naamösoherreeyë; Hosea nawe akabha ömökoramërëmo waae na kömöhakana iriguuti.  
> **Phonemes:** `ö m ö g a mb i s a r i m a n e s a w a a s u r u n aa m ö s o h e rr ee y ë; h o s e a n a w e a k a b h a ö m ö k o r a m ë r ë m o w aa e n a k ö m ö h a k a n a i r i g uu t i.`  
> **IPA:** `o m o ɣ a ᵐb i s a ɾ i m a n e s a w a a s u ɾ u n aː m o s o h e r eː j e; h o s e a n a w e a k a β h a o m o k o ɾ a m e ɾ e m o w aː e n a k o m o h a k a n a i ɾ i ɣ uː t i.`

> **Text:** bhagaiköma hamui kömooyo gumui go guitania Yosua na Abhaisiraeri.  
> **Phonemes:** `b h a g a i k ö m a h a m u i k ö m oo y o g u m u i g o g u i t a n i a y o s u a n a a b h a i s i r a e r i.`  
> **IPA:** `β h a ɣ a i k o m a h a m u i k o m oː j o ɣ u m u i ɣ o ɣ u i t a n i a j o s u a n a a β h a i s i ɾ a e ɾ i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kusaal</b> (<code>kus</code> · BF)</summary>

> **Text:** bɔzugɔ bugusim dim la paŋ na naae, amaa Zugsɔb la na ti'ed pʋpielim dim.  
> **Phonemes:** `b ɔ z u g ɔ b u g u s i m d i m l a p a ŋ n a n a a e, a m a a z u g s ɔ b l a n a t i ' e d p ʋ p i e l i m d i m.`  
> **IPA:** `b ɔ z u ɡ ɔ b u ɡ u s i m d i m l a p a ŋ n a n a a e, a m a a z u ɡ s ɔ b l a n a t i Ɂ e d p ʊ p i e l i m d i m.`

> **Text:** Zugsɔb la yɛl si'eli nwa ye, Kem onɛ ya'abid san'anɛ da' yʋʋr. Ka dɔl nidib la kpɛɛmnam nɛ maanmaannib la sieba keŋ.  
> **Phonemes:** `z u g s ɔ b l a y ɛ l s i ' e l i n w a y e, k e m o n ɛ y a ' a b i d s a n ' a n ɛ d a ' y ʋ ʋ r. k a d ɔ l n i d i b l a kp ɛ ɛ m n a m n ɛ m a a n m a a n n i b l a s i e b a k e ŋ.`  
> **IPA:** `z u ɡ s ɔ b l a j ɛ l s i Ɂ e l i n w a j e, k e m o n ɛ j a Ɂ a b i d s a n Ɂ a n ɛ d a Ɂ j ʊ ʊ r. k a d ɔ l n i d i b l a k͡p ɛ ɛ m n a m n ɛ m a a n m a a n n i b l a s i e b a k e ŋ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Kyode</b> (<code>acd</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** Sakpii belɛ ɔko gi kyaabɔɔ mɔ. Gɛnen so mɔ ɔ dii loo gikolii dɔ ɔ tɛ; asa mɔ, mɔ, mɛ san mɛ yelɛ gigengen so.  
> **Phonemes:** `s a kp i i b e l ɛ ɔ k o g i ky a a b ɔ ɔ m ɔ. g ɛ n e n s o m ɔ ɔ d i i l o o g i k o l i i d ɔ ɔ t ɛ; a s a m ɔ, m ɔ, m ɛ s a n m ɛ y e l ɛ g i g e ng e n s o.`  
> **IPA:** `s a k͡p i i b e l ɛ ɔ k o ɡ i t͡ʃ a a b ɔ ɔ m ɔ. ɡ ɛ n e n s o m ɔ ɔ d i i l o o ɡ i k o l i i d ɔ ɔ t ɛ; a s a m ɔ, m ɔ, m ɛ s a n m ɛ j e l ɛ ɡ i ɡ e ᵑɡ e n s o.`
