> **Text:** Mɛ dabɔlɛ kyo fɛye nnɛ mɔ, mɛ maŋ taalɛ wɔra fɛye sɛi.  
> **Phonemes:** `m ɛ d a b ɔ l ɛ ky o f ɛ y e n n ɛ m ɔ, m ɛ m a ŋ t a a l ɛ w ɔ r a f ɛ y e s ɛ i.`  
> **IPA:** `m ɛ d a b ɔ l ɛ t͡ʃ o f ɛ j e n n ɛ m ɔ, m ɛ m a ŋ t a a l ɛ w ɔ r a f ɛ j e s ɛ i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Lambya</b> (<code>lai</code> · MW)</summary>

> **Text:** Mwee bakamu bane, nanti umuntu yumo mumwinyu apaghukako kubwanaloli, po nanti yumo amunyosekezya kuko.  
> **Phonemes:** `m w ee b a k a m u b a n e, n a n t i u m u n t u y u m o m u m w i ny u a p a g h u k a k o k u b w a n a l o l i, p o n a n t i y u m o a m u ny o s e k e z y a k u k o.`  
> **IPA:** `m w eː b a k a m u b a n e, n a n t i u m u n t u j u m o m u m w i ɲ u a p a ɡ h u k a k o k u b w a n a l o l i, p o n a n t i j u m o a m u ɲ o s e k e z j a k u k o.`

> **Text:** Ivinama vyache vikaba vya chela, zyope inyayo zyache zikaba chela ichakongannya ni tope.  
> **Phonemes:** `i v i n a m a v y a c h e v i k a b a v y a c h e l a, z y o p e i ny a y o z y a c h e z i k a b a c h e l a i c h a k o ng a n ny a n i t o p e.`  
> **IPA:** `i v i n a m a v j a ʧ h e v i k a b a v j a ʧ h e l a, z j o p e i ɲ a j o z j a ʧ h e z i k a b a ʧ h e l a i ʧ h a k o ŋ a n ɲ a n i t o p e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Lango</b> (<code>did</code> · SS)</summary>

> **Text:** Hanneeka occa hatĩ Nyekuc ĩlaallẽ hutuno, hĩnĩngĩtĩ taliinit ꞌcĩ aꞌnyahaakeetu niig ngagĩĩt muumaa cĩ Nyekuc cĩnaac ko?.  
> **Phonemes:** `h a n n e e k a o c c a h a t ĩ ny e k u c ĩ l a a l l ẽ h u t u n o, h ĩ n ĩ n g ĩ t ĩ t a l i i n i t ꞌ c ĩ a ꞌ ny a h a a k e e t u n i i g n g a g ĩ ĩ t m u u m a a c ĩ ny e k u c c ĩ n a a c k o?.`  
> **IPA:** `h a n n e e k a o c c a h a t i ɲ e k u c i l a a l l e h u t u n o, h i n i n ɡ i t i t a l i i n i t ꞌ c i a ꞌ ɲ a h a a k e e t u n i i ɡ n ɡ a ɡ i i t m u u m a a c i ɲ e k u c c i n a a c k o?.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Limbum</b> (<code>lmp</code> · Cameroon)</summary>

> **Text:** Ŋgàŋ à du a ce kar à Mla' ce Rʉ̂ʉ, a ce suŋ buu ce Jisòs à m gèe nè ye. Bèe wɛɛwɛ a bɛɛŋgèr sê.  
> **Phonemes:** `ŋ g à ŋ à d u a c e k a r à m l a ' c e r ʉ̂ ʉ, a c e s u ŋ b u u c e j i s ò s à m g è e n è y e. b è e w ɛ ɛ w ɛ a b ɛ ɛ ŋ g è r s ê.`  
> **IPA:** `ŋ ɡ a˩ ŋ a˩ d u a tʃ e k a r a˩ m l a ʔ tʃ e r ʉ ʉ, a tʃ e s u ŋ b u u tʃ e dʒ i s o˩ s a˩ m ɡ e˩ e n e˩ j e. b e˩ e w ɛ ɛ w ɛ a b ɛ ɛ ŋ ɡ e˩ r s e.`

> **Text:** Wèe à ka' gee yuu ce mɛ̀ làa nè wèe, banɛ wèe yu bkar wa.  
> **Phonemes:** `w è e à k a ' g e e y u u c e m ɛ̀ l à a n è w è e, b a n ɛ w è e y u b k a r w a.`  
> **IPA:** `w e˩ e a˩ k a ʔ ɡ e e j u u tʃ e m ɛ˩ l a˩ a n e˩ w e˩ e, b a n ɛ w e˩ e j u b k a r w a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Lingala</b> (<code>lin</code> · CD)</summary>

> **Text:** Yawe akobunda mpo na bino, mpe bino bovanda kimia.  
> **Phonemes:** `y a w e a k o b u nd a mp o n a b i n o, mp e b i n o b o v a nd a k i m i a.`  
> **IPA:** `j a w e a k o b u ⁿd a ᵐp o n a b i n o, ᵐp e b i n o b o v a ⁿd a k i m i a.`

> **Text:** Oh Yawe, solo, opambolaka bato ya sembo mpe ozingelaka bango na nzela ya ngolu na Yo lokola na nzela ya nguba.  
> **Phonemes:** `o h y a w e, s o l o, o p a mb o l a k a b a t o y a s e mb o mp e o z i ng e l a k a b a ng o n a nz e l a y a ng o l u n a y o l o k o l a n a nz e l a y a ng u b a.`  
> **IPA:** `o h j a w e, s o l o, o p a ᵐb o l a k a b a t o j a s e ᵐb o ᵐp e o z i ᵑɡ e l a k a b a ᵑɡ o n a ⁿz e l a j a ᵑɡ o l u n a j o l o k o l a n a ⁿz e l a j a ᵑɡ u b a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Lotuko</b> (<code>lot</code> · SS)</summary>

> **Text:** ette itoŋo ikosi bwo fe ette igyama ikosi nyo orrijo nya egyama hosi. Arai nya isi daŋ ahehuhumak musyamahyen.  
> **Phonemes:** `e t t e i t o ŋ o i k o s i b w o f e e t t e i g y a m a i k o s i ny o o r r i j o ny a e g y a m a h o s i. a r a i ny a i s i d a ŋ a h e h u h u m a k m u s y a m a h y e n.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ θ θ ɛ i θ ɔ ŋ ɔ i k ɔ s i b w ɔ f ɛ ɛ θ θ ɛ i ɡ j a m a i k ɔ s i ɲ ɔ ɔ r r i ɟ ɔ ɲ a ɛ ɡ j a m a h ɔ s i. a r a i ɲ a i s i d a ŋ a h ɛ h ʊ h ʊ m a k m ʊ s j a m a h j ɛ n.`

> **Text:** ati aliawa iko aŋoruo, hunno osiru ebuhuna toye ette imanya moi, obeŋi adi isi ayamai.  
> **Phonemes:** `a t i a l i a w a i k o a ŋ o r u o, h u n n o o s i r u e b u h u n a t o y e e t t e i m a ny a m o i, o b e ŋ i a d i i s i a y a m a i.`  
> **IPA:** `a θ i a l i a w a i k ɔ a ŋ ɔ r ʊ ɔ, h ʊ n n ɔ ɔ s i r ʊ ɛ b ʊ h ʊ n a θ ɔ j ɛ ɛ θ θ ɛ i m a ɲ a m ɔ i, ɔ b ɛ ŋ i a d i i s i a j a m a i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Luganda</b> (<code>lug</code> · UG)</summary>

> **Text:** Musa bwe yawanikanga emikono gye, Abayisirayiri nga bagoba, naye bwe yagissanga, nga Abamaleki bagoba.  
> **Phonemes:** `m u s a b w e y a w a n i k a n g a e m i k o n o g y e, a b a y i s i r a y i r i n g a b a g o b a, n a y e b w e y a g i s s a n g a, n g a a b a m a l e k i b a g o b a.`  
> **IPA:** `m u s a b w e j a w a n i k a n ɡ a e m i k o n o ɡ j e, a b a j i s i r a j i r i n ɡ a b a ɡ o b a, n a j e b w e j a ɡ i s s a n ɡ a, n ɡ a a b a m a l e k i b a ɡ o b a.`

> **Text:** Kubanga ekiro ekyayise, malayika wa Katonda wange gwe mpeereza, yayimiridde we ndi.  
> **Phonemes:** `k u b a n g a e k i r o e k y a y i s e, m a l a y i k a w a k a t o n d a w a n g e g w e m p ee r e z a, y a y i m i r i d d e w e n d i.`  
> **IPA:** `k u b a n ɡ a e k i r o e k j a j i s e, m a l a j i k a w a k a t o n d a w a n ɡ e ɡ w e m p eː r e z a, j a j i m i r i d d e w e n d i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Lugbara</b> (<code>lgg</code> · CD)</summary>

> **Text:** suri Misimana, towiri Yeremia.  
> **Phonemes:** `s u r i m i s i m a n a, t o w i r i y e r e m i a.`  
> **IPA:** `s u r i m i s i m a n a, t o w i r i j e r e m i a.`

> **Text:** Yi nga ba diyi ba ba ti opele diyi ma drilia, yi zi Mungu tia, yi ti dri yi ma dria.  
> **Phonemes:** `y i ng a ba d i y i ba ba t i o p e l e d i y i m a dr i l i a, y i z i m u ng u t i a, y i t i dr i y i m a dr i a.`  
> **IPA:** `j i ᵑɡ a b d i j i b b t i o p e l e d i j i m a ɖ i l i a, j i z i m u ᵑɡ u t i a, j i t i ɖ i j i m a ɖ i a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Lumun</b> (<code>lmd</code> · SD)</summary>

> **Text:** akka kwaka prïk tokït koIli. Kwakannïkko ngapak ana Kanang ikupupure kaka naak nciki icarak connan.  
> **Phonemes:** `a k k a kw a k a p r ï k t o k ï t k o i l i. kw a k a n n ï k k o ng a p a k a n a k a n a ng i k u p u p u r e k a k a n a a k n c i k i i c a r a k c o n n a n.`  
> **IPA:** `a k k a kʷ a k a p ɾ ɪ k t ɔ k ɪ t k ɔ ɪ l ɪ. kʷ a k a n n ɪ k k ɔ ŋ a p a k a n a k a n a ŋ ɪ k ʊ p ʊ p ʊ ɾ ɛ k a k a n a a k n c ɪ k ɪ ɪ c a ɾ a k c ɔ n n a n.`

> **Text:** Oron thonu itti oron thangarot akka kongotheron cittokït.  
> **Phonemes:** `o r o n th o n u i t t i o r o n th a ng a r o t a k k a k o ng o th e r o n c i t t o k ï t.`  
> **IPA:** `ɔ ɾ ɔ n t̺ ɔ n ʊ ɪ t t ɪ ɔ ɾ ɔ n t̺ a ŋ a ɾ ɔ t a k k a k ɔ ŋ ɔ t̺ ɛ ɾ ɔ n c ɪ t t ɔ k ɪ t.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Lunda</b> (<code>lun</code> · AO)</summary>

> **Text:** nimahina akukudika kuchipaŋu, niyishimi yayu, nimayanda akwimikila, niihina dakukudika muchisu chachipaŋu;.  
> **Phonemes:** `n i m a h i n a a k u k u d i k a k u ch i p a ŋ u, n i y i sh i m i y a y u, n i m a y a n d a a k w i m i k i l a, n i i h i n a d a k u k u d i k a m u ch i s u ch a ch i p a ŋ u;.`  
> **IPA:** `n i m a h i n a a k u k u d i k a k u tʃ i p a ŋ u, n i j i ʃ i m i j a j u, n i m a j a n d a a k w i m i k i l a, n i i h i n a d a k u k u d i k a m u tʃ i s u tʃ a tʃ i p a ŋ u;.`

> **Text:** nikampoŋoji, nikasafu, nichikuŋulu;.  
> **Phonemes:** `n i k a m p o ŋ o j i, n i k a s a f u, n i ch i k u ŋ u l u;.`  
> **IPA:** `n i k a m p o ŋ o dʒ i, n i k a s a f u, n i tʃ i k u ŋ u l u;.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Luvale</b> (<code>lue</code> · AO)</summary>

> **Text:** Ove Kalunga, sokola Isalele Mumalwenyi osena.  
> **Phonemes:** `o v e k a l u ng a, s o k o l a i s a l e l e m u m a lw e ny i o s e n a.`  
> **IPA:** `o ʋ e k a l u ŋ a, s o k o l a i s a l e l e m u m a lʷ e ɲ i o s e n a.`

> **Text:** Mwomwo weshowo mwasaka kulwila mwono wenyi mwaujimbalisa. Oloze weshowo mwajimbalishila mwono wenyi hali Ami, mwauwana.  
> **Phonemes:** `mw o mw o w e sh o w o mw a s a k a k u lw i l a mw o n o w e ny i mw a u j i mb a l i s a. o l o z e w e sh o w o mw a j i mb a l i sh i l a mw o n o w e ny i h a l i a m i, mw a u w a n a.`  
> **IPA:** `mʷ o mʷ o w e ʃ o w o mʷ a s a k a k u lʷ i l a mʷ o n o w e ɲ i mʷ a u ʒ i ᵐb a l i s a. o l o z e w e ʃ o w o mʷ a ʒ i ᵐb a l i ʃ i l a mʷ o n o w e ɲ i h a l i a m i, mʷ a u w a n a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Láá Láá Bwamu</b> (<code>bwj</code> · BF)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɛ́ ɓɛ́lɛ ɛ́ mɔn hɔ̀ bòo lɛ ɛ́ wɔ́ á nùún sɔ ǹ àá ɓɛ́ nɛ̀nbùɛhĩ́ ǹ míkùi ɛ́ bie hɔ̀ ɛ́ lɛ́ ɓɛ́lɛ kɛ́ ǹ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ́ ɓ ɛ́ l ɛ ɛ́ m ɔ n h ɔ̀ b ò o l ɛ ɛ́ w ɔ́ á n ù ú n s ɔ ǹ à á ɓ ɛ́ n ɛ̀ n b ù ɛ h ĩ́ ǹ m í k ù i ɛ́ b i e h ɔ̀ ɛ́ l ɛ́ ɓ ɛ́ l ɛ k ɛ́ ǹ.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ ɓ ɛ l ɛ ɛ m ɔ n h ɔ b o o l ɛ ɛ w ɔ a n u u n s ɔ n a a ɓ ɛ n ɛ n b u ɛ h ĩ n m i k u i ɛ b i e h ɔ ɛ l ɛ ɓ ɛ l ɛ k ɛ n.`

> **Text:** Ɛ́ ɓɛ́ dɔ̀ ɛ́ dũmuasɛ̃̀ hɔ̀ bòo lɛ á khɛ́ ǹ, ɛ̀ ɓɛ̀dɔ̀ ɛ́ ɓiɛ̀ pɛ̃́ ɛ́ yí dɛsĩ̀ wɛ̀.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ́ ɓ ɛ́ d ɔ̀ ɛ́ d ũ m u a s ɛ̃̀ h ɔ̀ b ò o l ɛ á kh ɛ́ ǹ, ɛ̀ ɓ ɛ̀ d ɔ̀ ɛ́ ɓ i ɛ̀ p ɛ̃́ ɛ́ y í d ɛ s ĩ̀ w ɛ̀.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ ɓ ɛ d ɔ ɛ d ũ m u a s ɛ̃ h ɔ b o o l ɛ a ƙ ɛ n, ɛ ɓ ɛ d ɔ ɛ ɓ i ɛ pʰ ɛ̃ ɛ y i d ɛ s ĩ w ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Maasai</b> (<code>mas</code> · KE)</summary>

> **Text:** pee medol ake Olaitoriani nemeshipa, negoro, neiwuang'ie engoro aitung'uaa ninye.  
> **Phonemes:** `p e e m e d o l a k e o l a i t o r i a n i n e m e sh i p a, n e g o r o, n e i wu a n g ' i e e n g o r o a i t u n g ' u a a n i ny e.`  
> **IPA:** `p e e m e ɗ o l a k e o l a i t o ɾ i a n i n e m e ʃ i p a, n e ɠ o ɾ o, n e i wː a n ɠ ʔ i e e n ɠ o ɾ o a i t u n ɠ ʔ u a a n i ɲ e.`

> **Text:** Ore te nkalo e ndoyioroto e nkolong tipika ilkarash onom te nkadoro o ng'apeta tomon o nkitotoneta tomon.  
> **Phonemes:** `o r e t e n k a l o e n d o yi o r o t o e n k o l o n g t i p i k a i l k a r a sh o n o m t e n k a d o r o o n g ' a p e t a t o m o n o n k i t o t o n e t a t o m o n.`  
> **IPA:** `o ɾ e t e n k a l o e n ɗ o jː o ɾ o t o e n k o l o n ɠ t i p i k a i l k a ɾ a ʃ o n o m t e n k a ɗ o ɾ o o n ɠ ʔ a p e t a t o m o n o n k i t o t o n e t a t o m o n.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Makaa</b> (<code>mcp</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Yésus mú shîn bɨ́d búúd, a músə bád mbʉ́ŋʉ́d nyə́mɛ́fwó nə́ a kə́ jəgʉla nə Zɛmbî. Bulú mú zə yínd a njúl cíndú nyəmɛ́.  
> **Phonemes:** `y é s u s m ú sh î n b ɨ́ d b ú ú d, a m ú s ə b á d mb ʉ́ ŋ ʉ́ d ny ə́ m ɛ́ f w ó n ə́ a k ə́ j ə g ʉ l a n ə z ɛ mb î. b u l ú m ú z ə y í nd a n j ú l c í nd ú ny ə m ɛ́.`  
> **IPA:** `j e s u s m u ʃ i n b ɘ d b u u d, a m u s ə b a d ᵐb ʉ ŋ ʉ d ɲ ə m ɛ f w o n ə a k ə ɟ ə ɡ ʉ l a n ə z ɛ ᵐb i. b u l u m u z ə j i ⁿd a n ɟ u l c i ⁿd u ɲ ə m ɛ.`

> **Text:** Wɛɛ jaawʉ́g búúd njɨ sâ í ŋgə́ bwəma nə fwámɛ́ njɨ́ɨ́gʉ́lá yí.  
> **Phonemes:** `w ɛ ɛ j a a w ʉ́ g b ú ú d n j ɨ s â í ŋg ə́ b w ə m a n ə f w á m ɛ́ n j ɨ́ ɨ́ g ʉ́ l á y í.`  
> **IPA:** `w ɛ ɛ ɟ a a w ʉ ɡ b u u d n ɟ ɘ s a i ᵑɡ ə b w ə m a n ə f w a m ɛ n ɟ ɘ ɘ ɡ ʉ l a j i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Makonde</b> (<code>kde</code> · MZ)</summary>

> **Text:** ikava kenga mwiu tùgwete ing'ande ayo, hatwaneka kuva matakudavo.  
> **Phonemes:** `i k a v a k e ng a m w i u t ù g w e t e i ng' a nd e a y o, h a t w a n e k a k u v a m a t a k u d a v o.`  
> **IPA:** `i k a ʋ a k e ᵑɡ a m w i u t u ɡ w e t e i ŋ a ⁿd e a j o, h a t w a n e k a k u ʋ a m a t a k u d a ʋ o.`

> **Text:** Yesu kushidoni, Nangu ndaida namponia.  
> **Phonemes:** `y e s u k u sh i d o n i, n a ng u nd a i d a n a m p o n i a.`  
> **IPA:** `j e s u k u ʃ i d o n i, n a ᵑɡ u ⁿd a i d a n a m p o n i a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mambila</b> (<code>mcu</code> · Cameroon)</summary>

> **Text:** À yeé sɔm gi aá bɔ́, à ŋaá nde ké te tòre mé huún, bèh Càŋ duaà. Cibí yili lɔ bú ké teèn.  
> **Phonemes:** `à y e é s ɔ m g i a á b ɔ́, à ŋ a á nd e k é t e t ò r e m é h u ú n, b è h c à ŋ d u a à. c i b í y i l i l ɔ b ú k é t e è n.`  
> **IPA:** `a˩ j e e˥ s ɔ m ɡ i a a˥ b ɔ˥, a˩ ŋ a a˥ nd e k e˥ t e t o˩ d e m e˥ h u u˥ n, b e˩ h tʃ a˩ ŋ d u a a˩. tʃ i b i˥ j i l i l ɔ b u˥ k e˥ t e e˩ n.`

> **Text:** À yeé ke, nùà koô *gwà sóù déì waà baá, yilí seèn né Jàrûs. À yeé nde ŋene njií Yeésò mân, à cemmé nde ka seèn.  
> **Phonemes:** `à y e é k e, n ù à k o ô *g w à s ó ù d é ì w a à b a á, y i l í s e è n n é j à r û s. à y e é nd e ŋ e n e nj i í y e é s ò m â n, à c e m m é nd e k a s e è n.`  
> **IPA:** `a˩ j e e˥ k e, n u˩ a˩ k o o *ɡ w a˩ s o˥ u˩ d e˥ i˩ w a a˩ b a a˥, j i l i˥ s e e˩ n n e˥ dʒ a˩ d u s. a˩ j e e˥ nd e ŋ e n e ndʒ i i˥ j e e˥ s o˩ m a n, a˩ tʃ e m m e˥ nd e k a s e e˩ n.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mampruli</b> (<code>maw</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** Ŋɔn daa yɛli lala maa, u daa wulisiri ŋɔn ya n‑kpi seem maa.  
> **Phonemes:** `ŋ ɔ n d aa y ɛ l i l a l a m aa, u d aa w u l i s i r i ŋ ɔ n y a n‑kp i s ee m m aa.`  
> **IPA:** `ŋ ɔ n d aː j ɛ l i l a l a m aː, u d aː ʋ u l i s i r i ŋ ɔ n j a n‑k͡p i s eː m m aː.`

> **Text:** Tɔ. N na mari yɛla pam ni n ya n‑yɛli n‑ti i, amaa di zaa yɔri, n bu boori n sɔbi a n‑niŋi gbaŋŋu ni n‑ti i na.  
> **Phonemes:** `t ɔ. n n a m a r i y ɛ l a p a m n i n y a n‑y ɛ l i n‑t i i, a m aa d i z aa y ɔ r i, n b u b oo r i n s ɔ b i a n‑n i ŋ i gb a ŋ ŋ u n i n‑t i i n a.`  
> **IPA:** `t ɔ. n n a m a r i j ɛ l a p a m n i n j a n‑j ɛ l i n‑t i i, a m aː d i z aː j ɔ r i, n b u b oː r i n s ɔ b i a n‑n i ŋ i ɡ͡b a ŋ ŋ u n i n‑t i i n a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mandinka</b> (<code>mnk</code> · GM)</summary>

> **Text:** Ŋa a loŋ ne ko, Misira mansa te soŋ na ali ye taa, fo niŋ sembe baa laata a kaŋ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ŋ a a l o ŋ n e k o, m i s i r a m a n s a t e s o ŋ n a a l i y e t a a, f o n i ŋ s e m b e b a a l a a t a a k a ŋ.`  
> **IPA:** `ŋ a a l o ŋ n e k o, m i s i ɾ a m a n s a t e s o ŋ n a a l i j e t a a, f o n i ŋ s e m b e b a a l a a t a a k a ŋ.`

> **Text:** Ì la kaloolu be fondinkewolu faa la le, ì te balafaa soto la deenaanoolu ye, sako ka hiinoo yitandi dindiŋolu la.  
> **Phonemes:** `ì l a k a l o o l u b e f o n d i n k e w o l u f a a l a l e, ì t e b a l a f a a s o t o l a d e e n a a n o o l u y e, s a k o k a h i i n o o y i t a n d i d i n d i ŋ o l u l a.`  
> **IPA:** `i l a k a l o o l u b e f o n d i n k e w o l u f a a l a l e, i t e b a l a f a a s o t o l a d e e n a a n o o l u j e, s a k o k a h i i n o o j i t a n d i d i n d i ŋ o l u l a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mano</b> (<code>mev</code> · CI)</summary>

> **Text:** Lɛ̀ míáa séĩ́ tóó dɔɔ̀ e kɛá Zízɛ̀ mɔ̀ɔ, a gèe là tòà‐píé‐mìàa lɛ̀ɛ kélɛ̀.  
> **Phonemes:** `l ɛ̀ m í á a s é ĩ́ t ó ó d ɔ ɔ̀ e k ɛ á z í z ɛ̀ m ɔ̀ ɔ, a g è e l à t ò à‐p í é‐m ì à a l ɛ̀ ɛ k é l ɛ̀.`  
> **IPA:** `l ɛ˨ m i˦ a˦ a s e˦ ĩ˦ t o˦ o˦ d ɔ ɔ˨ e k ɛ a˦ z i˦ z ɛ˨ m ɔ˨ ɔ, a g e˨ e l a˨ t o˨ a˨‐p i˦ e˦‐m i˨ a˨ a l ɛ˨ ɛ k e˦ l ɛ˨.`

> **Text:** Yé mɔṹ Dèɓé e à sí e Dàa‐mìa, lɛ́ɛ̀ dɔ kpɛɛ̀ lɛ́ à Dàa‐mì è kɛ̀ à gbe ká.  
> **Phonemes:** `y é m ɔ ṹ d è ɓ é e à s í e d à a‐m ì a, l ɛ́ ɛ̀ d ɔ kp ɛ ɛ̀ l ɛ́ à d à a‐m ì è k ɛ̀ à gb e k á.`  
> **IPA:** `y e˦ m ɔ ũ˦ d e˨ ɓ e˦ e a˨ s i˦ e d a˨ a‐m i˨ a, l ɛ˦ ɛ˨ d ɔ k͡p ɛ ɛ˨ l ɛ˦ a˨ d a˨ a‐m i˨ e˨ k ɛ˨ a˨ ɡ͡b e k a˦.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Marba</b> (<code>mpg</code> · TD)</summary>

> **Text:** Ni kua ba, an mba ni ngaf ki Israel-lâ; yi máma mba mi mbut ni yima a tinim iram vama abo wile man nda wiwilika.  
> **Phonemes:** `n i k u a b a, a n mb a n i ng a f k i i s r a e l-l â; y i m á m a mb a m i mb u t n i y i m a a t i n i m i r a m v a m a a b o w i l e m a n nd a w i w i l i k a.`  
> **IPA:** `n i k u a b a, a n ᵐb a n i ᵑɡ a f k i i s r a e l-l a; j i m a m a ᵐb a m i ᵐb u t n i j i m a a t i n i m i r a m v a m a a b o w i l e m a n ⁿd a w i w i l i k a.`

> **Text:** Ma didina, angî ahina manda, angî ma wurak kana, ar zla da avunanda ki djiber ra kurunda a lang tang djivida.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a d i d i n a, a ng î a h i n a m a nd a, a ng î m a w u r a k k a n a, a r zl a d a a v u n a nd a k i d j i b e r r a k u r u nd a a l a ng t a ng d j i v i d a.`  
> **IPA:** `m a d i d i n a, a ᵑɡ i a h i n a m a ⁿd a, a ᵑɡ i m a w u r a k k a n a, a r ɮ a d a a v u n a ⁿd a k i d dʒ i b e r r a k u r u ⁿd a a l a ᵑɡ t a ᵑɡ d dʒ i v i d a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Masaaba</b> (<code>myx</code> · KE)</summary>

> **Text:** Lwanyuma balibawanayo inywe, mutaambisibwe, lundi mwiriibwe, ni kamanaambo koosi kalibabiyila khubafunisa lisiina lyase.  
> **Phonemes:** `lw a ny u m a b a l i b a w a n a y o i ny w e, m u t aa m b i s i bw e, l u n d i mw i r ii bw e, n i k a m a n aa m b o k oo s i k a l i b a b i y i l a kh u b a f u n i s a l i s ii n a l y a s e.`  
> **IPA:** `lʷ a ɲ u m a b a l i b a w a n a j o i ɲ w e, m u t aː m b i s i bʷ e, l u n d i mʷ i r iː bʷ e, n i k a m a n aː m b o k oː s i k a l i b a b i j i l a kʰ u b a f u n i s a l i s iː n a l j a s e.`

> **Text:** Ise ikhaasiindikhe luufu luubi naabi lutsye lwoonakise ba Hivi, ba Kanani, ba Hiti, nga inywe mushiili khukhwoolayo ta.  
> **Phonemes:** `i s e i kh aa s ii n d i kh e l uu f u l uu b i n aa b i l u ts y e lw oo n a k i s e b a h i v i, b a k a n a n i, b a h i t i, ng a i ny w e m u s h ii l i kh u kh w oo l a y o t a.`  
> **IPA:** `i s e i kʰ aː s iː n d i kʰ e l uː f u l uː b i n aː b i l u ts j e lʷ oː n a k i s e b a h i v i, b a k a n a n i, b a h i t i, ŋ a i ɲ w e m u s h iː l i kʰ u kʰ w oː l a j o t a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mato</b> (<code>mgh</code> · MZ)</summary>

> **Text:** Ala phaari asaana a Rakeli ayanryawe ni Yakopi othene yaari khumi na cese.  
> **Phonemes:** `a l a p h a a r i a s a a n a a r a k e l i a y a n r y a w e n i y a k o p i o t h e n e y a a r i k h u m i n a c e s e.`  
> **IPA:** `ɑ l ɑ p h ɑ ɑ r i ɑ s ɑ ɑ n ɑ ɑ r ɑ k e l i ɑ j ɑ n r j ɑ w e n i j ɑ k o p i o t h e n e j ɑ ɑ r i k h u m i n ɑ c e s e.`

> **Text:** Ukhalhawaya yakaari malhehano o upacha alhe khayaakhuwachiye, attu khaathananiya ukhalhana malhehano o nayelhi.  
> **Phonemes:** `u k h a l h a w a y a y a k a a r i m a l h e h a n o o u p a c h a a l h e k h a y a a k h u w a c h i y e, a t t u k h a a t h a n a n i y a u k h a l h a n a m a l h e h a n o o n a y e l h i.`  
> **IPA:** `u k h ɑ l h ɑ w ɑ j ɑ j ɑ k ɑ ɑ r i m ɑ l h e h ɑ n o o u p ɑ c h ɑ ɑ l h e k h ɑ j ɑ ɑ k h u w ɑ c h i j e, ɑ t t u k h ɑ ɑ t h ɑ n ɑ n i j ɑ u k h ɑ l h ɑ n ɑ m ɑ l h e h ɑ n o o n ɑ j e l h i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mauritian Creole</b> (<code>mfe</code> · MU)</summary>

> **Text:** Zozef ti vann so karo e li ti remet tou larzan la bann apot.  
> **Phonemes:** `z o z e f t i v ann s o k a r o e l i t i r e m e t t ou l a r z an l a b ann a p o t.`  
> **IPA:** `z o z e f t i v an s o k a r o e l i t i r e m e t t u l a r z ɑ̃ l a b an a p o t.`

> **Text:** Tranble, zot bann fezer, zot ki ranpli avek lorgey. Depouy zot, tir zot linz, atas enn sintir otour zot lerin.  
> **Phonemes:** `t r an b l e, z o t b ann f e z er, z o t k i r an p l i a v e k l o r g e y. d e p ou y z o t, t i r z o t l in z, a t a s enn s in t i r o t ou r z o t l er in.`  
> **IPA:** `t r ɑ̃ b l e, z o t b an f e z eːʳ~œːʳ, z o t k i r ɑ̃ p l i a v e k l o r ɡ e j. d e p u j z o t, t i r z o t l ɛ̃ z, a t a s ɛn s ɛ̃ t i r o t u r z o t l eːʳ~œːʳ ɛ̃.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mbembe</b> (<code>mfb</code> · Nigeria)</summary>

> **Text:** Mbɔchù ke mbyangàninju wo nyi éfi yi wo ngre ge lèbe è yá mbyangàninju wo Mpwèmya nyi Yeso Kristò éfi huŋ.  
> **Phonemes:** `m b ɔ ch ù k e m b y a ng à n i n j u w o ny i é f i y i w o ng r e g e l è b e è y á m b y a ng à n i n j u w o m p w è m y a ny i y e s o k r i s t ò é f i h u ŋ.`  
> **IPA:** `m b ɔ tʃ u˩ k e m b j a ŋ a˩ n i n dʒ u w o ɲ i e˥ f͈ i j i w o ŋ r e ɡ e l e˩ b e e˩ j a˥ m b j a ŋ a˩ n i n dʒ u w o m p w e˩ m j a ɲ i j e s͈ o k r i s͈ t o˩ e˥ f͈ i h u ŋ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mbunda</b> (<code>mck</code> · AO)</summary>

> **Text:** Co nona tumamanya tuvali twa kanelya nakutalekao mazina likumi na avali a vana va Yakomba va vanalume.  
> **Phonemes:** `c o n o n a t u m a m a ny a t u v a l i t w a k a n e l y a n a k u t a l e k a o m a z i n a l i k u m i n a a v a l i a v a n a v a y a k o mb a v a v a n a l u m e.`  
> **IPA:** `ʧ ɔ n ɔ n a t u m a m a ɲ a t u v a l i t w a k a n ɛ l j a n a k u t a l ɛ k a ɔ m a z i n a l i k u m i n a a v a l i a v a n a v a j a k ɔ mb a v a v a n a l u m ɛ.`

> **Text:** Vukalu vwa mwene vunafu ngwe kwaana kwa ndumba, ou amushangumuna avulumuna mwonyo wendi.  
> **Phonemes:** `v u k a l u v w a m w e n e v u n a f u ng w e k w a a n a k w a nd u mb a, o u a m u s h a ng u m u n a a v u l u m u n a m w o ny o w e nd i.`  
> **IPA:** `v u k a l u v w a m w ɛ n ɛ v u n a f u ŋɡ w ɛ k w a a n a k w a nd u mb a, ɔ u a m u s h a ŋɡ u m u n a a v u l u m u n a m w ɔ ɲ ɔ w ɛ nd i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Medumba</b> (<code>byv</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Yen mfen nà lo' nkùnkù ntsho nzwit be yi, ngùtni ba tà be ngù bo nke ngua' bonfi, ntshètte yub ndù kà bo yôg.  
> **Phonemes:** `y e n mf e n n à l o ' n k ù n k ù nt sh o n z w i t b e y i, n g ù t n i b a t à b e n g ù b o n k e n g u a ' b o n f i, nt sh è t t e y u b nd ù k à b o y ô g.`  
> **IPA:** `j e n ᵐf e n n a l o ʔ n k u n k u ⁿt ʃ o n z w i t b e j i, n ɡ u t n i b a t a b e n ɡ u b o n k e n ɡ u a ʔ b o n f i, ⁿt ʃ e t t e j u b ⁿd u k a b o j o ɡ.`

> **Text:** Tshùaben bèn ma' ntsho num zin tu, tu wut tsin bin tswe fite.  
> **Phonemes:** `ts h ù a b e n b è n m a ' nt sh o n u m z i n t u, t u w u t ts i n b i n ts w e f i t e.`  
> **IPA:** `ʦ h u a b e n b e n m a ʔ ⁿt ʃ o n u m z i n t u, t u w u t ʦ i n b i n ʦ w e f i t e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Migaama</b> (<code>mmy</code> · TD)</summary>

> **Text:** Kayyeeco ibina ɗa ya maatiki, o maa gu guta beneyya gu leewaa ɗa. Galbigeeco talta o gu kawayya ɗa oko gicco.  
> **Phonemes:** `k a y y ee c o i b i n a ɗ a y a m aa t i k i, o m aa g u g u t a b e n e y y a g u l ee w aa ɗ a. g a l b i g ee c o t a l t a o g u k a w a y y a ɗ a o k o g i c c o.`  
> **IPA:** `k a j j eː c o i b i n a ɗ a j a m aː t i k i, o m aː ɡ u ɡ u t a b e n e j j a ɡ u l eː w aː ɗ a. ɡ a l b i ɡ eː c o t a l t a o ɡ u k a w a j j a ɗ a o k o ɡ i c c o.`

> **Text:** Gimu gasakkaa ma aŋkiya ɗa min oko gimtee ku giiri saa.  
> **Phonemes:** `g i m u g a s a k k aa m a a ŋ k i y a ɗ a m i n o k o g i m t ee k u g ii r i s aa.`  
> **IPA:** `ɡ i m u ɡ a s a k k aː m a a ŋ k i j a ɗ a m i n o k o ɡ i m t eː k u ɡ iː r i s aː.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Minyanka</b> (<code>myk</code> · Mali)</summary>

> **Text:** Kelee dii mu di da já ma ceboro pye: ‹Yere di kagaanra le wolo ma ɲii li ni!› na ta puga tinbara di ɲɛ mu wuu li ni wɛ?.  
> **Phonemes:** `k e l e e d i i m u d i d a j á m a c e b o r o p y e: ‹y e r e d i k a g a a n r a l e w o l o m a ɲ i i l i n i!› n a t a p u g a t i n b a r a d i ɲ ɛ m u w u u l i n i w ɛ?.`  
> **IPA:** `k e l e e d i i m u d i d a d͡ʒ a˥ m a t͡ʃ e b o r o p j e: ‹j e r e d i k a ɡ a a n r a l e w o l o m a ɲ i i l i n i!› n a t a p u ɡ a t i n b a r a d i ɲ ɛ m u w u u l i n i w ɛ?.`

> **Text:** Wee tuun wu ni a Yesu di gbɔn pu ɲìi ki na, na pu pye: «Lemu yʼa zhaa ge, li pye yi mu na saha ni yi wo nʼa daa wu ni.».  
> **Phonemes:** `w e e t u u n w u n i a y e s u d i gb ɔ n p u ɲ ì i k i n a, n a p u p y e: «l e m u y ' a zh a a g e, l i p y e y i m u n a s a h a n i y i w o n ' a d a a w u n i.».`  
> **IPA:** `w e e t u u n w u n i a j e s u d i ɡ͡b ɔ n p u ɲ i˩ i k i n a, n a p u p j e: «l e m u j ' a ʒ a a ɡ e, l i p j e j i m u n a s a h a n i j i w o n ' a d a a w u n i.».`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mmen</b> (<code>bfm</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Zɨnə tè kyi se kyàyn endom kə ghɛ zɛ ə tè nyàʼ ə ghənə alàŋ pəyn səmɔʼ nə, Ə tè pàyn kê Mi sə ndô.  
> **Phonemes:** `z ɨ n ə t è ky i s e ky à yn e nd o m k ə gh ɛ z ɛ ə t è ny à ' ə gh ə n ə a l à ŋ p ə yn s ə m ɔ ' n ə, ə t è p à yn k ê m i s ə nd ô.`  
> **IPA:** `z ɨ n ə t e kʲ i s e kʲ a ɲ e ⁿd o m k ə ɣ ɛ z ɛ ə t e ɲ a ʔ ə ɣ ə n ə a l a ŋ p ə ɲ s ə m ɔ ʔ n ə, ə t e p a ɲ k e m i s ə ⁿd o.`

> **Text:** Wùzhɔyn ə̀təyn ə pə̀ynsə̀ sê Njisə̀s nə, Ò nyi wùə ə Njû, m nyi wùə zhɔyn ə̀ Sàmalyà, Ò kwì zayn sə̂ ndɔnə m̀mo sê mi ə.  
> **Phonemes:** `w ù zh ɔ yn ə̀ t ə yn ə p ə̀ yn s ə̀ s ê nj i s ə̀ s n ə, ò ny i w ù ə ə nj û, m ny i w ù ə zh ɔ yn ə̀ s à m a l y à, ò k w ì z a yn s ə̂ nd ɔ n ə m̀ m o s ê m i ə.`  
> **IPA:** `w u ʒ ɔ ɲ ə t ə ɲ ə p ə ɲ s ə s e ⁿd͡ʒ i s ə s n ə, o ɲ i w u ə ə ⁿd͡ʒ u, m ɲ i w u ə ʒ ɔ ɲ ə s a m a l j a, o k w i z a ɲ s ə ⁿd ɔ n ə m m o s e m i ə.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mo</b> (<code>mfi</code> · Ghana)</summary>

> **Text:** kɛ a ta biɡre bɛlbwa, o den sewɛ, n ɡaaɡe o bɔr, n ta on nae.  
> **Phonemes:** `k ɛ a t a b i g r e b ɛ l b w a, o d e n s e w ɛ, n g a a g e o b ɔ r, n t a o n n a e.`  
> **IPA:** `k ɛ a t a b i ɡ r ɪ b ɛ l b w a, ʊ d ɪ n s ɪ w ɛ, n ɡ a a ɡ ɪ ʊ b ɔ r, n t a ʊ n n a ɪ.`

> **Text:** Weela la taa lɔ o nɛɛ nɔ, ɡɛɛ doŋa ɡe ɛɛ ba o nɛɛ doe. A chɛ kɛ ó le nɛra bini, dɛ́ ɡbo ka so bela baa soɡa la dahara.  
> **Phonemes:** `w e e l a l a t a a l ɔ o n ɛ ɛ n ɔ, g ɛ ɛ d o ŋ a g e ɛ ɛ b a o n ɛ ɛ d o e. a ch ɛ k ɛ ó l e n ɛ r a b i n i, d ɛ́ gb o k a s o b e l a b a a s o g a l a d a h a r a.`  
> **IPA:** `w ɪ ɪ l a l a t a a l ɔ ʊ n ɛ ɛ n ɔ, ɡ ɛ ɛ d ʊ ŋ a ɡ ɪ ɛ ɛ b a ʊ n ɛ ɛ d ʊ ɪ. a tʃ ɛ k ɛ ʊ l ɪ n ɛ r a b i n i, d ɛ ɡ͡b ʊ k a s ʊ b ɪ l a b a a s ʊ ɡ a l a d a h a r a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mongo</b> (<code>lol</code> · CD)</summary>

> **Text:** bondotswa ndomwa botafitakonto; lolo bondola limomwa, boko bofitakonto.  
> **Phonemes:** `b o nd o t s w a nd o m w a b o t a f i t a k o nt o; l o l o b o nd o l a l i m o m w a, b o k o b o f i t a k o nt o.`  
> **IPA:** `[b] [o] [ⁿd] [o] [t] [s] [w] [a] [ⁿd] [o] [m] [w] [a] [b] [o] [t] [a] [f] [i] [t] [a] [k] [o] [ⁿt] [o]; [l] [o] [l] [o] [b] [o] [ⁿd] [o] [l] [a] [l] [i] [m] [o] [m] [w] [a], [b] [o] [k] [o] [b] [o] [f] [i] [t] [a] [k] [o] [ⁿt] [o].`

> **Text:** Ko bayoike banei bakela, O ngoko. Ko bingambi baokwa ndʼase, baomemya imemya.  
> **Phonemes:** `k o b a y o i k e b a n e i b a k e l a, o ng o k o. k o b i ng a mb i b a o k w a nd ' a s e, b a o m e m y a i m e m y a.`  
> **IPA:** `[k] [o] [b] [a] [j] [o] [i] [k] [e] [b] [a] [n] [e] [i] [b] [a] [k] [e] [l] [a], [o] [ᵑɡ] [o] [k] [o]. [k] [o] [b] [i] [ᵑɡ] [a] [ᵐb] [i] [b] [a] [o] [k] [w] [a] [ⁿd] ' [a] [s] [e], [b] [a] [o] [m] [e] [m] [j] [a] [i] [m] [e] [m] [j] [a].`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Morokodo</b> (<code>mgc</code> · SS)</summary>

> **Text:** Ëzë vïdï ma owo kö'du yandi 'bama ne dë koro mo aba, atëdï kaa vo lumë ma o'ba loko ënï da yayi.  
> **Phonemes:** `ë z ë v ï d ï m a o w o k ö 'd u y a nd i 'b a m a n e d ë k o r o m o a b a, a t ë d ï k a a v o l u m ë m a o 'b a l o k o ë n ï d a y a y i.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ z ɛ v ɪ d ɪ m a ʊ w ʊ k ʊ ɗ u j a ⁿd ɪ 'b a m a n ɛ d ɛ k ʊ r ʊ m ʊ a b a, a t ɛ d ɪ k a a v ʊ l u m ɛ m a ʊ ɓ a l ʊ k ʊ ɛ n ɪ d a j a j ɪ.`

> **Text:** Yaanya aba kö'du kise mati dowo ze kazi Wisi ɔmo, ehe yöyö mo ëdï henye, Wïrï bikanyi ra, biköndu ako anza dë mï mo.  
> **Phonemes:** `y a a ny a a b a k ö 'd u k i s e m a t i d o w o z e k a z i w i s i ɔ m o, e h e y ö y ö m o ë d ï h e ny e, w ï r ï b i k a ny i r a, b i k ö nd u a k o a nz a d ë m ï m o.`  
> **IPA:** `j a a ɲ a a b a k ʊ ɗ u k ɪ s ɛ m a t ɪ d ʊ w ʊ z ɛ k a z ɪ w ɪ s ɪ ɔ m ʊ, ɛ h ɛ j ʊ j ʊ m ʊ ɛ d ɪ h ɛ ɲ ɛ, w ɪ r ɪ b ɪ k a ɲ ɪ r a, b ɪ k ʊ ⁿd u a k ʊ a ⁿz a d ɛ m ɪ m ʊ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mossi</b> (<code>mos</code> · BF)</summary>

> **Text:** ne ro-bãooneg nins sẽn tom taab yɩng lalgã zugã sʋka, tɩ b yalem dag n yaa kãntɩɩg pisi n gilgi.  
> **Phonemes:** `n e r o-b ã o o n e g n i n s s ẽ n t o m t a a b y ɩ n g l a l g ã z u g ã s ʋ k a, t ɩ b y a l e m d a g n y a a k ã n t ɩ ɩ g p i s i n g i l g i.`  
> **IPA:** `n e r o-b a o o n e ɡ n i n s s e n t o m t a a b y ɪ n ɡ l a l ɡ a z u ɡ a s ʊ k a, t ɪ b y a l e m d a ɡ n y a a k a n t ɪ ɪ ɡ p i s i n ɡ i l ɡ i.`

> **Text:** Yãmb yĩnga Soaala-Wẽnd sũur tol n puuga ne maam menga, tɩ b yeel-ma: ‹Fo menga kõn kẽ tẽnga ye!.  
> **Phonemes:** `y ã m b y ĩ n g a s o a a l a-w ẽ n d s ũ u r t o l n p u u g a n e m a a m m e n g a, t ɩ b y e e l-m a: ‹f o m e n g a k õ n k ẽ t ẽ n g a y e!.`  
> **IPA:** `y a m b y i n ɡ a s o a a l a-w e n d s u u r t o l n p u u ɡ a n e m a a m m e n ɡ a, t ɪ b y e e l-m a: ‹f o m e n ɡ a k o n k e t e n ɡ a y e!.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Muan</b> (<code>mmo</code> · Côte d'Ivoire)</summary>

> **Text:** «'Ke Davidi 'gbu Krisi 'siila‑ ‹Mɛɛzan›. 'Bhɛɛ‑ ‑e sɔ klɛle‑ ‑a ‑nranɛ 'le 'nale ɛɛ?».  
> **Phonemes:** `«'k e d a v i d i 'gb u k r i s i 's i i l a‑ ‹m ɛ ɛ z a n›. 'bh ɛ ɛ‑ ‑e s ɔ k l ɛ l e‑ ‑a ‑n r a n ɛ 'l e 'n a l e ɛ ɛ?».`  
> **IPA:** `«'k e d a v i d i 'ɡ͡b u k l i s i 's i i l a‑ ‹ɓ ɛ ɛ z a l›. 'ɓ ɛ ɛ‑ ‑e s ɔ k l ɛ l e‑ ‑a ‑l l a l ɛ 'l e 'l a l e ɛ ɛ?».`

> **Text:** 'Bhɛ ‑nɛ 'ɛ nu klɛlɛ 'e zrukpaapɛ ‑gbɛnɛ 'le, 'bhɛɛ‑ ‑a yaza 'ɛ nu mɛɛ ‑bebe zrukpaalɛ.  
> **Phonemes:** `'bh ɛ ‑n ɛ 'ɛ n u k l ɛ l ɛ 'e z r u kp a a p ɛ ‑gb ɛ n ɛ 'l e, 'bh ɛ ɛ‑ ‑a y a z a 'ɛ n u m ɛ ɛ ‑b e b e z r u kp a a l ɛ.`  
> **IPA:** `'ɓ ɛ ‑l ɛ 'ɛ l u k l ɛ l ɛ 'e z l u k͡p a a p ɛ ‑ɡ͡b ɛ l ɛ 'l e, 'ɓ ɛ ɛ‑ ‑a j a z a 'ɛ l u ɓ ɛ ɛ ‑b e b e z l u k͡p a a l ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mundani</b> (<code>mnf</code> · Cameroon)</summary>

> **Text:** Ngule bɔmbù̧ wòt nàà wu bòt lob tò.  
> **Phonemes:** `n g u l e b ɔ m b ù̧ w ò t n à à w u b ò t l o b t ò.`  
> **IPA:** `n ɡ u l e b ɔ m b uˤ w oˤ t n aˤ aˤ w u b oˤ t l o b t oˤ.`

> **Text:** Fɔ̧̀ Dàbid wu tɔ atò ane tǎt vi, tà ghɨ̀ na ka ntsiꞋi waã Dàbid à.  
> **Phonemes:** `f ɔ̧̀ d à b i d w u t ɔ a t ò a n e t ǎ t v i, t à gh ɨ̀ n a k a n ts i ꞌ i w a ã d à b i d à.`  
> **IPA:** `f ɔˤ d aˤ b i d w u t ɔ a t oˤ a n e t a˩˥ t w i, t aˤ ɣ ɯˤ n a k a n ts i ꞌ i w a a~ d aˤ b i d aˤ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Murle</b> (<code>mur</code> · Sudan)</summary>

> **Text:** Ole oke dook akɔm ci abada ŋaatineeŋ kiiya kanyek Jook zany zɛɛ ma bar abada ɛɛti ceen miroi noko doon.  
> **Phonemes:** `o l e o k e d o o k a k ɔ m c i a b a d a ŋ a a t i n e e ŋ k i i y a k a ny e k j o o k z a ny z ɛ ɛ m a b a r a b a d a ɛ ɛ t i c e e n m i r o i n o k o d o o n.`  
> **IPA:** `o l e o k e d o o k a k ɔ m tʃ i a b a d a ŋ a a t i n e e ŋ k i i j a k a ɲ e k dʒ o o k z a ɲ z ɛ ɛ m a b a r a b a d a ɛ ɛ t i tʃ e e n m i r o i n o k o d o o n.`

> **Text:** Maje toonnyawa ween torgɛrɛm ona anyononek torombɛta e olla areyi ka kotobeec.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a j e t o o n ny a w a w e e n t o r g ɛ r ɛ m o n a a ny o n o n e k t o r o m b ɛ t a e o l l a a r e y i k a k o t o b e e c.`  
> **IPA:** `m a dʒ e t o o n ɲ a w a w e e n t o r ɡ ɛ r ɛ m o n a a ɲ o n o n e k t o r o m b ɛ t a e o l l a a r e j i k a k o t o b e e tʃ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Musey</b> (<code>mse</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Yohananni ma a kalavandirana, Elzabad ni ma a dleeŋerana.  
> **Phonemes:** `y o h a n a n n i m a a k a l a v a nd i r a n a, e l z a b a d n i m a a dl e e ŋ e r a n a.`  
> **IPA:** `j o h a n a n n i m a a k a l a v a ⁿd i ɾ a n a, e l z a b a d n i m a a ɮ e e ŋ e ɾ a n a.`

> **Text:** Sa ma ndak a hoŋ va ma Lona tlam ha kina ayna ni gege? Sa ma ndak joɓom ana aŋ li na ni megena ni gege?.  
> **Phonemes:** `s a m a nd a k a h o ŋ v a m a l o n a tl a m h a k i n a a y n a n i g e g e? s a m a nd a k j o ɓ o m a n a a ŋ l i n a n i m e g e n a n i g e g e?.`  
> **IPA:** `s a m a ⁿd a k a h o ŋ v a m a l o n a ɬ a m h a k i n a a j n a n i ɡ e ɡ e? s a m a ⁿd a k d͡ʒ o ɓ o m a n a a ŋ l i n a n i m e ɡ e n a n i ɡ e ɡ e?.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Mwani</b> (<code>wmw</code> · MZ)</summary>

> **Text:** tiwamwarikire wari pakisi, wekala kinviri ca kifo, ilongoze maulu etu njira ya usalama.  
> **Phonemes:** `t i w a m w a r i k i r e w a r i p a k i s i, w e k a l a k i n v i r i c a k i f o, i l o n g o z e m a u l u e t u n j i r a y a u s a l a m a.`  
> **IPA:** `t i w a m w a r i k i r e w a r i p a k i s i, w e k a l a k i n v i r i ʧ a k i f o, i l o n ɡ o z e m a u l u e t u n ʤ i r a j a u s a l a m a.`

> **Text:** kamana kombola uhai isabu yake ulu, aapo maripo akutosha.  
> **Phonemes:** `k a m a n a k o m b o l a u h a i i s a b u y a k e u l u, a a p o m a r i p o a k u t o sh a.`  
> **IPA:** `k a m a n a k o m b o l a u h a i i s a b u j a k e u l u, a a p o m a r i p o a k u t o ʃ a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ménik</b> (<code>tnr</code> · SN)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɗanaŋ konmaŋ ënmëngeyó ɓër gaf ɓawo Ar Gaf ariyé emën. Gungum e Ar Ɗóŋóluko Urün ale.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɗ a n a ŋ k o n m a ŋ ë n m ë ng e y ó ɓ ë r g a f ɓ a w o a r g a f a r i y é e m ë n. g u ng u m e a r ɗ ó ŋ ó l u k o u r ü n a l e.`  
> **IPA:** `ɗ a n a ŋ k ɔ n m a ŋ ɛ n m ɛ ŋɡ ɛ j ɔ ɓ ɛ r ɡ a f ɓ a w ɔ a r ɡ a f a r i j ɛ ɛ m ɛ n. ɡ u ŋɡ u m ɛ a r ɗ ɔ ŋ ɔ l u k ɔ u r u n a l ɛ.`

> **Text:** Ëng ikér gang owocas Urün oɗ gen gipëd gangaŝa eŋ, gipëd gan sakal aŋ agé gacësal. Gon e gocësal oŋ mangolëka okecaye.  
> **Phonemes:** `ë ng i k é r g a ng o w o c a s u r ü n o ɗ g e n g i p ë d g a ng a ŝ a e ŋ, g i p ë d g a n s a k a l a ŋ a g é g a c ë s a l. g o n e g o c ë s a l o ŋ m a ng o l ë k a o k e c a y e.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ ŋɡ i k ɛ r ɡ a ŋɡ ɔ w ɔ c a s u r u n ɔ ɗ ɡ ɛ n ɡ i p ɛ d ɡ a ŋɡ a s a ɛ ŋ, ɡ i p ɛ d ɡ a n s a k a l a ŋ a ɡ ɛ ɡ a c ɛ s a l. ɡ ɔ n ɛ ɡ ɔ c ɛ s a l ɔ ŋ m a ŋɡ ɔ l ɛ k a ɔ k ɛ c a j ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Nambya</b> (<code>nmq</code> · BW)</summary>

> **Text:** Wakati abuja banhu kuti bemwayike, wakatanha mudunhu eli ega kunonamata. Kwakati kwaba madeko, iye wakaba eli ega.  
> **Phonemes:** `w a k a t i a b u j a b a nh u k u t i b e m w a y i k e, w a k a t a nh a m u d u nh u e l i e g a k u n o n a m a t a. k w a k a t i k w a b a m a d e k o, i y e w a k a b a e l i e g a.`  
> **IPA:** `w a ɣ a t i a β u ʤ a β a ⁿh u ɣ u t i β e m w a j i ɣ e, w a ɣ a t a ⁿh a m u d u ⁿh u e l i e ɡ a ɣ u n o n a m a t a. ɣ w a ɣ a t i ɣ w a β a m a d e ɣ o, i j e w a ɣ a β a e l i e ɡ a.`

> **Text:** Tizhani busangu! Lwose lumwe luchinyo lunotiwa nonhu lukwo kuzhe kwombili, asi unhu unsangu unochinyila umbili uwe.  
> **Phonemes:** `t i z h a n i b u s a ng u! l w o s e l u m w e l u c h i ny o l u n o t i w a n o nh u l u k w o k u z h e k w o m b i l i, a s i u nh u u ns a ng u u n o c h i ny i l a u m b i l i u w e.`  
> **IPA:** `t i z ɦ a n i β u s a ᵑɡ u! l w o s e l u m w e l u ʧ ɦ i ɲ o l u n o t i w a n o ⁿh u l u ɣ w o ɣ u z ɦ e ɣ w o m β i l i, a s i u ⁿh u u ⁿs a ᵑɡ u u n o ʧ ɦ i ɲ i l a u m β i l i u w e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Nande</b> (<code>nnb</code> · CD)</summary>

> **Text:** Nenyũ mwasyĩmya omũsondolĩ muguma oko buli kihanda, erigaba ekihugo eribya mwandu.  
> **Phonemes:** `n e ny ũ m w a s y ĩ m y a o m ũ s o nd o l ĩ m u g u m a o k o b u l i k i h a nd a, e r i g a b a e k i h u g o e r i b y a m w a nd u.`  
> **IPA:** `n ɛ ɲ u m w a s j i m j a ɔ m u s ɔ ⁿd ɔ l i m u ɣ u m a ɔ k ɔ β u l i k i h a ⁿd a, ɛ ɾ i ɣ a β a ɛ k i h u ɣ ɔ ɛ ɾ i β j a m w a ⁿd u.`

> **Text:** Kwa abandi bamabya butsira erikirirya kwehi? Eritindikirirya ryabo rika sangula eritindasiganirya ryoMungu kwehi?.  
> **Phonemes:** `k w a a b a nd i b a m a b y a b u t s i r a e r i k i r i r y a k w e h i? e r i t i nd i k i r i r y a r y a b o r i k a s a ng u l a e r i t i nd a s i g a n i r y a r y o m u ng u k w e h i?.`  
> **IPA:** `k w a a β a ⁿd i β a m a β j a β u t s i ɾ a ɛ ɾ i k i ɾ i ɾ j a k w ɛ h i? ɛ ɾ i t i ⁿd i k i ɾ i ɾ j a ɾ j a β ɔ ɾ i k a s a ᵑɡ u l a ɛ ɾ i t i ⁿd a s i ɣ a n i ɾ j a ɾ j ɔ m u ᵑɡ u k w ɛ h i?.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ngbaka</b> (<code>nga</code> · Zaire)</summary>

> **Text:** Zɛ́ dati kɛ na, mbula tɛ hɔ̀ gɔ ni, nɛ nde mi zi do kpasi boe. Kɔ mbula nɛa we hɔ nɛ, nɛ 'dã mɔ kpa ngawi.  
> **Phonemes:** `z ɛ́ d a t i k ɛ n a, m b u l a t ɛ h ɔ̀ g ɔ n i, n ɛ n d e m i z i d o kp a s i b o e. k ɔ m b u l a n ɛ a w e h ɔ n ɛ, n ɛ 'd ã m ɔ kp a ng a w i.`  
> **IPA:** `z ɛ d a t i k ɛ n a, m b u l a t ɛ h ɔ ɡ ɔ n i, n ɛ n d e m i z i d o k͡p a s i b o e. k ɔ m b u l a n ɛ a w e h ɔ n ɛ, n ɛ 'd ã m ɔ k͡p a ŋ a w i.`

> **Text:** Tati a owi wilinɔ kɛ wa a nu fɛlɛ nganga ni, hã wa nyɔngɔ sɛ dɔnggamɛ ni, we kɛ ma santo.  
> **Phonemes:** `t a t i a o w i w i l i n ɔ k ɛ w a a n u f ɛ l ɛ ng a ng a n i, h ã w a ny ɔ ng ɔ s ɛ d ɔ ng g a m ɛ n i, w e k ɛ m a s a n t o.`  
> **IPA:** `t a t i a o w i w i l i n ɔ k ɛ w a a n u f ɛ l ɛ ŋ a ŋ a n i, h ã w a ɲ ɔ ŋ ɔ s ɛ d ɔ ŋ ɡ a m ɛ n i, w e k ɛ m a s a n t o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ngbandi</b> (<code>ngb-zaire</code> · Zaire)</summary>

> **Text:** Ĩ da na kɔzo kɔndɔ ko, Yezo adɔ nɔ ti ndo lɛ ngu, anɔ ka tɛ ava lo.  
> **Phonemes:** `ĩ d a n a k ɔ z o k ɔ n d ɔ k o, y e z o a d ɔ n ɔ t i n d o l ɛ n g u, a n ɔ k a t ɛ a v a l o.`  
> **IPA:** `i~ d a n a k ɔ z o k ɔ n d ɔ k o, j e z o a d ɔ n ɔ t i n d o l ɛ n ɡ u, a n ɔ k a t ɛ a v a l o.`

> **Text:** Ta sanga ma, ta Nzapa ndoha ye gba ma.  
> **Phonemes:** `t a s a n g a m a, t a n z a p a n d o h a y e gb a m a.`  
> **IPA:** `t a s a n ɡ a m a, t a n z a p a n d o h a j e ɡ͡b a m a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Nkonya</b> (<code>nko</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** Yudafɔ bʋtɔfwɩ ɩwɩ amʋ́ amandɩɛ ɔkpasʋ. Mʋ́ sʋ bɔpʋ awʋndɩ akpɔnkpɔɔnkpɔntɩ asie yaɩ woyi amʋtɔ.  
> **Phonemes:** `y u d a f ɔ b ʋ t ɔ fw ɩ ɩ w ɩ a m ʋ́ a m a n d ɩ ɛ ɔ kp a s ʋ. m ʋ́ s ʋ b ɔ p ʋ a w ʋ n d ɩ a kp ɔ n kp ɔɔ n kp ɔ n t ɩ a s i e y a ɩ w o y i a m ʋ t ɔ.`  
> **IPA:** `j u d a f ɔ b ʊ t ɔ fʷ ɪ ɪ w ɪ a m ʊ a m a n d ɪ ɛ ɔ k͡p a s ʊ. m ʊ s ʊ b ɔ p ʊ a w ʊ n d ɪ a k͡p ɔ n k͡p ɔː n k͡p ɔ n t ɪ a s i e j a ɪ w o j i a m ʊ t ɔ.`

> **Text:** Tamɛ mʋ́ o a, ɔlɛlɛ ɔnɔ wa nwʋlʋta, ɔsʋsʋ lefinki.  
> **Phonemes:** `t a m ɛ m ʋ́ o a, ɔ l ɛ l ɛ ɔ n ɔ w a n w ʋ l ʋ t a, ɔ s ʋ s ʋ l e f i n k i.`  
> **IPA:** `t a m ɛ m ʊ o a, ɔ l ɛ l ɛ ɔ n ɔ w a n w ʊ l ʊ t a, ɔ s ʊ s ʊ l e f i n k i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Noni</b> (<code>nhu</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɛ wvú ke shɛɛ gay bó le, mum nɛ ben yî kum e wvú ɛmbeŋ keegɛn ebunle. Ɛ ɛkfuŋ ke jiim ɛ wvú nu kɛ fó wvú ɛmbeŋ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ w v ú k e sh ɛ ɛ g a y b ó l e, m u m n ɛ b e n y î k u m e w v ú ɛ m b e ŋ k e e g ɛ n e b u n l e. ɛ ɛ k f u ŋ k e j i i m ɛ w v ú n u k ɛ f ó w v ú ɛ m b e ŋ.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ w v u k e ʃ ɛ ɛ ɡ a j b o l e, m u m n ɛ b e n j i k u m e w v u ɛ m b e ŋ k e e ɡ ɛ n e b u n l e. ɛ ɛ k f u ŋ k e d͡ʒ i i m ɛ w v u n u k ɛ f o w v u ɛ m b e ŋ.`

> **Text:** Ɛ wee wvù diee nu Waawee to to keeboyse bonyii bò nu ɛ lakɛn wase.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ w e e w v ù d i e e n u w a a w e e t o t o k e e b o y s e b o ny i i b ò n u ɛ l a k ɛ n w a s e.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ w e e w v u d i e e n u w a a w e e t o t o k e e b o j s e b o ɲ i i b o n u ɛ l a k ɛ n w a s e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Northern Sotho</b> (<code>nso</code> · BW)</summary>

> **Text:** Še melawana ya dikabelo tša bupi. Moprista wa Moarone a tlišetše Morena kabelo ya bupi pele ga aletare.  
> **Phonemes:** `š e m e l a w a n a y a d i k a b e l o tš a b u p i. m o p r i s t a w a m o a r o n e a tl i š e tš e m o r e n a k a b e l o y a b u p i p e l e g a a l e t a r e.`  
> **IPA:** `s iː m iː l ɑ w ɑ n ɑ j ɑ d ɪ k ɑ b iː l o ts ɑ b ʊ p ɪ. m o p r ɪ s t ɑ w ɑ m o ɑ r o n iː ɑ tɫ ɪ s iː ts iː m o r iː n ɑ k ɑ b iː l o j ɑ b ʊ p ɪ p iː l iː x ɑ ɑ l iː t ɑ r iː.`

> **Text:** le aletare ya dihlabelo le dibjana tša yona ka moka, le legopo la go hlapela le leoto la lona.  
> **Phonemes:** `l e a l e t a r e y a d i h l a b e l o l e d i b j a n a tš a y o n a k a m o k a, l e l e g o p o l a g o h l a p e l a l e l e o t o l a l o n a.`  
> **IPA:** `l iː ɑ l iː t ɑ r iː j ɑ d ɪ h l ɑ b iː l o l iː d ɪ b ʤ ɑ n ɑ ts ɑ j o n ɑ k ɑ m o k ɑ, l iː l iː x o p o l ɑ x o h l ɑ p iː l ɑ l iː l iː o t o l ɑ l o n ɑ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Nso</b> (<code>lns</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Mo kibáŋ kí lo, kí wàa shùŋrìn, ndzə́ví shuŋrìnè, lo fo bàm, yí yùm.  
> **Phonemes:** `m o k i b á ŋ k í l o, k í w à a sh ù ŋ r ì n, ndz ə́ v í sh u ŋ r ì n è, l o f o b à m, y í y ù m.`  
> **IPA:** `m ɔ k i b a ŋ k i l ɔ, k i w a a ʃ u ŋ ɾ i n, ⁿd͡z ə v i ʃ u ŋ ɾ i n ɛ, l ɔ f ɔ b a m, j i j u m.`

> **Text:** Kùʼùn wìr kù yii diy ji wìr wo kuʼùn wáa kòŋ áwune, à lóo fo fómo, wìr dzə̀m wàa dzə ŋkar yè.  
> **Phonemes:** `k ù ' ù n w ì r k ù y ii d iy j i w ì r w o k u ' ù n w á a k ò ŋ á w u n e, à l ó o f o f ó m o, w ì r dz ə̀ m w à a dz ə ŋk a r y è.`  
> **IPA:** `k u ʔ u n w i ɾ k u j iː d ij d͡ʒ i w i ɾ w ɔ k u ʔ u n w a a k ɔ ŋ a w u n ɛ, a l ɔ ɔ f ɔ f ɔ m ɔ, w i ɾ d͡z ə m w a a d͡z ə ᵑk a ɾ j ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Nuer</b> (<code>nus</code> · ET)</summary>

> **Text:** Ram mi pɛl latdɛ ni pɛl, kä ram mi dɔ̱a̱r latdɛ ni nyin dɔ̱ɔ̱rä.  
> **Phonemes:** `r a m m i p ɛ l l a t d ɛ n i p ɛ l, k ä r a m m i d ɔ̱ a̱ r l a t d ɛ n i ny i n d ɔ̱ ɔ̱ r ä.`  
> **IPA:** `r a m m i p ɛ l l a t d ɛ n i p ɛ l, k aˈ r a m m i d ɔ̤ a̤ r l a t d ɛ n i ɲ i n d ɔ̤ ɔ̤ r aˈ.`

> **Text:** Ni guäth ciɛŋädɛ guicɛ nɛy diaal tin cieŋ wi̱i̱ muɔ̱ɔ̱n.  
> **Phonemes:** `n i g u ä th c i ɛ ŋ ä d ɛ g u i c ɛ n ɛ y d i a a l t i n c i e ŋ w i̱ i̱ m u ɔ̱ ɔ̱ n.`  
> **IPA:** `n i ɡ ṳ aˈ t̪ c i ɛ ŋ aˈ d ɛ ɡ ṳ i c ɛ n ɛ j d i a a l t i n c i e ŋ w i̤ i̤ m ṳ ɔ̤ ɔ̤ n.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Nyamwezi</b> (<code>nym</code> · TZ)</summary>

> **Text:** Na wayombaga, Yesu, unizukile ulu ukwiza mu vutemi wako.  
> **Phonemes:** `n a w a y o mb a g a, y e s u, u n i z u k i l e u l u u k w i z a m u v u t e m i w a k o.`  
> **IPA:** `n a w a j o ᵐb a ɡ a, j ɛ s u, u n i z u k i l ɛ u l u u k w i z a m u v u t ɛ m i w a k o.`

> **Text:** Lelo Agripa wamuwilaga Paulo, Uli na luhusa kwivula. Haho Paulo wagololaga kukono kwakwe, na.  
> **Phonemes:** `l e l o a g r i p a w a m u w i l a g a p a u l o, u l i n a l u h u s a k w i v u l a. h a h o p a u l o w a g o l o l a g a k u k o n o k w a k w e, n a.`  
> **IPA:** `l ɛ l o a ɡ r i p a w a m u w i l a ɡ a p a u l o, u l i n a l u h u s a k w i v u l a. h a h o p a u l o w a ɡ o l o l a ɡ a k u k o n o k w a k w ɛ, n a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Nyole</b> (<code>nuj</code> · UG)</summary>

> **Text:** Aye abaŋeeresa ababbenga, bahayulirenga mu lwijuuye, abandi ko batye.  
> **Phonemes:** `a y e a b a ŋ ee r e s a a b a bb e ng a, b a h a y u l i r e ng a m u lw i j uu y e, a b a nd i k o b a ty e.`  
> **IPA:** `a j e a β a ŋ eː ɾ e s a a β a b e ᵑɡ a, β a x a j u l i ɾ e ᵑɡ a m u lʷ i ɟ uː j e, a β a ⁿd i k o β a tʲ e.`

> **Text:** Yesu ni gabona Nasanayiri ni goleresa aŋa ali, ngʼaloma ati, Bona Omuyisirayiri endiho era abulamo obunafuusi.  
> **Phonemes:** `y e s u ni g a b o n a n a s a n a y i r i ni g o l e r e s a a ŋ a a l i, ng ' a l o m a a t i, b o n a o m u y i s i r a y i r i e nd i h o e r a a b u l a m o o b u n a f uu s i.`  
> **IPA:** `j e s u nʲ ɡ a β o n a n a s a n a j i ɾ i nʲ ɡ o l e ɾ e s a a ŋ a a l i, ᵑɡ ' a l o m a a t i, β o n a o m u j i s i ɾ a j i ɾ i e ⁿd i x o e ɾ a a β u l a m o o β u n a ɸ uː s i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Nzema</b> (<code>nzi</code> · CI)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɔzinle bɛ muala bɛ nwo kpole, na bɛvale sulosulo bɛyele Nyamenle ayɛlɛ bɛzele kɛ, Ɛnɛ yɛnwu ninyɛne ɛzulolɛ kpalɛ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɔ z i n l e b ɛ m u a l a b ɛ n w o k p o l e, n a b ɛ v a l e s u l o s u l o b ɛ y e l e n y a m e n l e a y ɛ l ɛ b ɛ z e l e k ɛ, ɛ n ɛ y ɛ n w u n i n y ɛ n e ɛ z u l o l ɛ k p a l ɛ.`  
> **IPA:** `ɔ z i n l e b ɛ m u a l a b ɛ n w o k p o l e, n a b ɛ v a l e s u l o s u l o b ɛ j e l e n j a m e n l e a j ɛ l ɛ b ɛ z e l e k ɛ, ɛ n ɛ j ɛ n w u n i n j ɛ n e ɛ z u l o l ɛ k p a l ɛ.`

> **Text:** Wɔmɔ die wɔ ahatualɛ ne na kɔ sua nu. Mekola metua amozi amra kakɛ ko ne ala, kɛmɔ menduale wɔ la.  
> **Phonemes:** `w ɔ m ɔ d i e w ɔ a h a t u a l ɛ n e n a k ɔ s u a n u. m e k o l a m e t u a a m o z i a m r a k a k ɛ k o n e a l a, k ɛ m ɔ m e n d u a l e w ɔ l a.`  
> **IPA:** `w ɔ m ɔ d i e w ɔ a h a t u a l ɛ n e n a k ɔ s u a n u. m e k o l a m e t u a a m o z i a m r a k a k ɛ k o n e a l a, k ɛ m ɔ m e n d u a l e w ɔ l a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Obolo</b> (<code>ann</code> · NG)</summary>

> **Text:** Ogwu uga uyok-ejit môbọkọ osusut uyok-ejit kan̄, ire onyan̄a ọmọ isibi, owu òbokpọkpọ ikinyan̄a ọmọ ikeya.  
> **Phonemes:** `o gw u u g a u y o k-e j i t m ô b ọ k ọ o s u s u t u y o k-e j i t k a n̄, i r e o ny a n̄ a ọ m ọ i s i b i, o w u ò b o kp ọ kp ọ i k i ny a n̄ a ọ m ọ i k e y a.`  
> **IPA:** `o ɡ u u ɡ a u j o k-e ɟ i t m o b o k o o s u s u t u j o k-e ɟ i t k a n, i r e o ɲ a n a o m o i s i b i, o w u o b o k͡p o k͡p o i k i ɲ a n a o m o i k e j a.`

> **Text:** Jei Okumugwem, soso, kajit emi ìtele; Awaji n̄a, kada lek ìbọkọ emi me lek!.  
> **Phonemes:** `j e i o k u m u gw e m, s o s o, k a j i t e m i ì t e l e; a w a j i n̄ a, k a d a l e k ì b ọ k ọ e m i m e l e k!.`  
> **IPA:** `ɟ e i o k u m u ɡ e m, s o s o, k a ɟ i t e m i i t e l e; a w a ɟ i n a, k a d a l e k i b o k o e m i m e l e k!.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Oku</b> (<code>oku</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Əb shik dì, è me se chii ghene ghene, fɛle bek ka ghene nè tasen sɛy ndəy sɛ ɛ́ tamen əmntaa so, kɛn ka Bool.  
> **Phonemes:** `ə b s h i k d ì, è m e s e ch ii gh e n e gh e n e, f ɛ l e b e k k a gh e n e n è t a s e n s ɛ y n d ə y s ɛ ɛ́ t a m e n ə m n t aa s o, k ɛ n k a b oo l.`  
> **IPA:** `ə b s h i k d i, e m e s e t͡ʃ iː ɣ e n e ɣ e n e, f ɛ l e b e k k ɑ ɣ e n e n e t ɑ s e n s ɛ j n d ə j s ɛ ɛ t ɑ m e n ə m n t ɑː s o, k ɛ n k ɑ b oː l.`

> **Text:** Ngose wel kaame ɛ wen ɛybee bɛme, əb me lii se kenək è diom se ten nɛ ghel ɛjime teem se əbghune jio.  
> **Phonemes:** `n g o s e w e l k aa m e ɛ w e n ɛ y b ee b ɛ m e, ə b m e l ii s e k e n ə k è d i o m s e t e n n ɛ gh e l ɛ j i m e t ee m s e ə b gh u n e j i o.`  
> **IPA:** `n ɡ o s e w e l k ɑː m e ɛ w e n ɛ j b eː b ɛ m e, ə b m e l iː s e k e n ə k e d i o m s e t e n n ɛ ɣ e l ɛ ʒ i m e t eː m s e ə b ɣ u n e ʒ i o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Oshiwambo</b> (<code>ndo</code> · AO)</summary>

> **Text:** omuyambi na ye ko a ka tale. Ngele oshushi oya taandela, ongulu oyoshidhila.  
> **Phonemes:** `o m u y a m b i n a y e k o a k a t a l e. ng e l e o sh u sh i o y a t aa n d e l a, o ng u l u o y o sh i dh i l a.`  
> **IPA:** `o m u j a m b i n a j e k o a k a t a l e. ŋɡ e l e o ʃ u ʃ i o j a t aː n d e l a, o ŋɡ u l u o j o ʃ i ð i l a.`

> **Text:** Otapa landula omautho gondjondjambo. Oyo ondjapukilela.  
> **Phonemes:** `o t a p a l a n d u l a o m a u th o g o ndj o ndj a m b o. o y o o ndj a p u k i l e l a.`  
> **IPA:** `o t a p a l a n d u l a o m a u θ o ɡ o ɲʤ o ɲʤ a m b o. o j o o ɲʤ a p u k i l e l a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Paasaal</b> (<code>sig</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɛɛ rɛ ʋ mʋ a basɩ ɛɛ tɩɩ tɩya haŋbiye hʋ tɩɩna mɛ, ʋ laa nyʋwa weliŋ anɩɩ ʋ sɩ mʋ baga hʋ aŋ vɩya mʋyɩ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ ɛ r ɛ ʋ m ʋ a b a s ɩ ɛ ɛ t ɩ ɩ t ɩ y a h a ŋ b i y e h ʋ t ɩ ɩ n a m ɛ, ʋ l a a ny ʋ w a w e l i ŋ a n ɩ ɩ ʋ s ɩ m ʋ b a g a h ʋ a ŋ v ɩ y a m ʋ y ɩ.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ ɛ r ɛ ʊ m ʊ a b a s ɪ ɛ ɛ t ɪ ɪ t ɪ j a h a ŋ b i j e h ʊ t ɪ ɪ n a m ɛ, ʊ l a a ɲ ʊ w a w e l i ŋ a n ɪ ɪ ʊ s ɪ m ʊ b a ɡ a h ʊ a ŋ v ɪ j a m ʊ j ɪ.`

> **Text:** Ɛɛ rɛ ʋ baa, Wɩɩsɩ pɩɩsa, ka lagɩlagɩ no ʋ kyiyeli ŋ nyɛ. Ʋ tɔɔ ŋ hɩɩsɩ rɛ dʋnɩya tɩyaŋ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɛ ɛ r ɛ ʋ b a a, w ɩ ɩ s ɩ p ɩ ɩ s a, k a l a g ɩ l a g ɩ n o ʋ ky i y e l i ŋ ny ɛ. ʋ t ɔ ɔ ŋ h ɩ ɩ s ɩ r ɛ d ʋ n ɩ y a t ɩ y a ŋ.`  
> **IPA:** `ɛ ɛ r ɛ ʊ b a a, w ɪ ɪ s ɪ p ɪ ɪ s a, k a l a ɡ ɪ l a ɡ ɪ n o ʊ t͡ʃ i j e l i ŋ ɲ ɛ. ʊ t ɔ ɔ ŋ h ɪ ɪ s ɪ r ɛ d ʊ n ɪ j a t ɪ j a ŋ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Pinyin</b> (<code>pny</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Pobə lə̀ pìʼnə̀ a nɨ puʼù mbô ambo Yěsû lo â myàʼtə̀ a pàa àlaʼà ɨ̀ghobə.  
> **Phonemes:** `p o b ə l ə p ì ' n ə a n ɨ p u ' ù m b ô a m b o y ě s û l o â m y à ' t ə a p à a à l a ' à ɨ̀ gh o b ə.`  
> **IPA:** `p ɔ β ə l ə p i ʔ n ə ɑ n ɨ p u ʔ u m β ɔ ɑ m β ɔ j ɛ s u l ɔ ɑ m j ɑ ʔ t ə ɑ p ɑ ɑ ɑ l ɑ ʔ ɑ ɨ ɣ ɔ β ə.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Punu</b> (<code>puu</code> · CG)</summary>

> **Text:** Moise anolaba yimaga yieni amatsiengila yivala mbana amakewivula rie: Dibandu yi yivala yigenyengili?.  
> **Phonemes:** `m o i s e a n o l a b a y i m a g a y i e n i a m a t s i e ng i l a y i v a l a mb a n a a m a k e w i v u l a r i e: d i b a nd u y i y i v a l a y i g e ny e ng i l i?.`  
> **IPA:** `m o i s e a n o l a b a j i m a ɡ a j i e n i a m a t s i e ŋ i l a j i v a l a ᵐb a n a a m a k e w i v u l a ɾ i e: d i b a ⁿd u j i j i v a l a j i ɡ e ɲ e ŋ i l i?.`

> **Text:** Mua bilumbu binovioga baJuif bamabenguna na bamatunga fundu ubokisa Saul.  
> **Phonemes:** `m u a b i l u mb u b i n o v i o g a b a j u i f b a m a b e ng u n a n a b a m a t u ng a f u nd u u b o k i s a s a u l.`  
> **IPA:** `m u a b i l u ᵐb u b i n o v i o ɡ a b a j u i f b a m a b e ŋ u n a n a b a m a t u ŋ a f u ⁿd u u b o k i s a s a u l.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Rendille</b> (<code>rel</code> · Kenya)</summary>

> **Text:** Nyaakhut walaassaahti Waakh soobahsate weyti a atin soobariitiche.  
> **Phonemes:** `ny a a kh u t w a l a a s s a a h t i w a a kh s o o b a h s a t e w e y t i a a t i n s o o b a r i i t i ch e.`  
> **IPA:** `ɲ a a x u t w a l a a s s a a ħ t i w a a x s ɔ ɔ b a ħ s a t e w e j t i a a t i n s ɔ ɔ b a r i i t i tʃ e.`

> **Text:** Maalinkaas goya, Yeesso minki us kajire kabahe, irte beyti Gaalilaaya afiche orronte.  
> **Phonemes:** `m a a l i n k a a s g o y a, y e e s s o m i n k i u s k a j i r e k a b a h e, i r t e b e y t i g a a l i l a a y a a f i ch e o r r o n t e.`  
> **IPA:** `m a a l i n k a a s ɡ ɔ j a, j e e s s ɔ m i n k i u s k a dʒ i r e k a b a ħ e, i r t e b e j t i ɡ a a l i l a a j a a f i tʃ e ɔ r r ɔ n t e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Ronga</b> (<code>rng</code> · MZ)</summary>

> **Text:** Vahangasungula kumukhongota leswaku asuka atikweni dravu.  
> **Phonemes:** `v a h a n g a s u n g u l a k u m u k h o n g o t a l e sw a k u a s u k a a t i k w e n i d r a v u.`  
> **IPA:** `v a h a n ɡ a s u n ɡ u l a k u m u k h ɔ n ɡ ɔ t a l e sʷ a k u a s u k a a t i k w e n i d r a v u.`

> **Text:** Ntrhaku Yesu aya Nhaveni ya Minhlwari.  
> **Phonemes:** `n t r h a k u y e s u a y a n h a v e n i y a m i n hl w a r i.`  
> **IPA:** `n t r h a k u j e s u a j a n h a v e n i j a m i n ɸ w a r i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Safen</b> (<code>sav</code> · SN)</summary>

> **Text:** Ƴutta, a gapukka an : « Kooh hun na so, a tufkoh ho ŋ ɓaaɓiŋ ngo, a on ndo kañam na ɓekkaah.  
> **Phonemes:** `ƴ u t t a, a g a p u k k a a n : « k o o h h u n n a s o, a t u f k o h h o ŋ ɓ a a ɓ i ŋ ng o, a o n n d o k a ñ a m n a ɓ e k k a a h.`  
> **IPA:** `ʄ u t t a, a ɡ a p u k k a a n : « k ɔ ɔ h h u n n a s ɔ, a t u f k ɔ h h ɔ ŋ ɓ a a ɓ i ŋ ᵑɡ ɔ, a ɔ n n d ɔ k a n a m n a ɓ ɛ k k a a h.`

> **Text:** Ŋ ineem, ɗu teyɗukiɗ Kiristaani Yeesu findi Heɗi, mbaa ɗah, raakaat tumohaaɗ huniɗ na ɗe.  
> **Phonemes:** `ŋ i n e e m, ɗ u t e y ɗ u k i ɗ k i r i s t a a n i y e e s u f i n d i h e ɗ i, mb a a ɗ a h, r a a k a a t t u m o h a a ɗ h u n i ɗ n a ɗ e.`  
> **IPA:** `ŋ i n ɛ ɛ m, ɗ u t ɛ j ɗ u k i ɗ k i r i s t a a n i j ɛ ɛ s u f i n d i h ɛ ɗ i, ᵐb a a ɗ a h, r a a k a a t t u m ɔ h a a ɗ h u n i ɗ n a ɗ ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Sango</b> (<code>sag-central_african_republic</code> · Central African Republic)</summary>

> **Text:** Popo ti azo ti gbata ni akangbi: ambeni amu mbage ti azuife si ambeni amu mbage ti * awatokua.  
> **Phonemes:** `p o p o t i a z o t i gb a t a n i a k a ngb i: a mb e n i a m u mb a g e t i a z u i f e s i a mb e n i a m u mb a g e t i * a w a t o k u a.`  
> **IPA:** `p o p o t i a z o t i ɡ͡b a t a n i a k a ŋɡ͡b i: a ᵐb e n i a m u ᵐb a ɡ e t i a z u i f e s i a ᵐb e n i a m u ᵐb a ɡ e t i * a w a t o k u a.`

> **Text:** Moïse atene na be ti lo: Mbi yeke ngoro ti gue ti baa kota ye ti ndima so. Ngbanga ti nyen si wâ agbi keke ni pepe so.  
> **Phonemes:** `m o ï s e a t e n e n a b e t i l o: mb i y e k e ng o r o t i g u e t i b a a k o t a y e t i nd i m a s o. ngb a ng a t i ny e n s i w â a gb i k e k e n i p e p e s o.`  
> **IPA:** `m o i˧ s e a t e n e n a b e t i l o: ᵐb i j e k e ŋɡ o r o t i ɡ u e t i b a a k o t a j e t i ⁿd i m a s o. ŋɡ͡b a ŋɡ a t i ɲ e n s i w a˥ a ɡ͡b i k e k e n i p e p e s o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Sena</b> (<code>seh</code> · MW)</summary>

> **Text:** mbakhalonga kuti: Ndimo mwene! Mbuya alamukadi pa kufa, pontho abulukira kuna Simau.  
> **Phonemes:** `mb a kh a l o ng a k u t i: nd i m o mw e n e! mb u y a a l a m u k a d i p a k u f a, p o nth o a b u l u k i r a k u n a s i m a u.`  
> **IPA:** `ᵐɓ a kʰ a l ɔ ᵑɡ a k u t i: ⁿɗ i m ɔ mʷ ɛ n ɛ! ᵐɓ u j a a l a m u k a ɗ i p a k u f a, p ɔ ⁿtʰ ɔ a ɓ u l u k i ɾ a k u n a s i m a u.`

> **Text:** Thangwi pinaphedzeranji kuna munthu kuwina dziko yonsene ya pantsi, mbaluza nzimu wace?.  
> **Phonemes:** `th a ng w i p i n a ph e dz e r a nj i k u n a m u nth u k u w i n a dz i k o y o n s e n e y a p a nts i, mb a l u z a n z i m u w a c e?.`  
> **IPA:** `tʰ a ᵑɡ w i p i n a pʰ ɛ dz ɛ ɾ a ⁿʒ i k u n a m u ⁿtʰ u k u w i n a dz i k ɔ j ɔ n s ɛ n ɛ j a p a ⁿts i, ᵐɓ a l u z a n z i m u w a tʃ ɛ?.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Shambala</b> (<code>ksb</code> · TZ)</summary>

> **Text:** kwa kuti viya vya Mulungu vyetailikana, viifumbuke kwawe, hambu Mulungu azaviwafumbwila;.  
> **Phonemes:** `k w a k u t i v i y a v y a m u l u ng u v y e t a i l i k a n a, v i i f u mb u k e k w a w e, h a mb u m u l u ng u a z a v i w a f u mb w i l a;.`  
> **IPA:** `k w a k u t i v i j a v j a m u l u ᵑɡ u v j ɛ t a i l i k a n a, v i i f u ᵐb u k ɛ k w a w ɛ, h a ᵐb u m u l u ᵑɡ u a z a v i w a f u ᵐb w i l a;.`

> **Text:** Usheogohe, mndele Siyoni! Shuyu Zumbe ywako! Aiza, aipandile ṅwana njowe.  
> **Phonemes:** `u sh e o g o h e, m nd e l e s i y o n i! sh u y u z u mb e y w a k o! a i z a, a i p a nd i l e ṅ w a n a nj o w e.`  
> **IPA:** `u ʃ ɛ ɔ ɡ ɔ h ɛ, m ⁿd ɛ l ɛ s i j ɔ n i! ʃ u j u z u ᵐb ɛ j w a k ɔ! a i z a, a i p a ⁿd i l ɛ n w a n a ᶮɟ ɔ w ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Shilluk</b> (<code>shk</code> · SD)</summary>

> **Text:** Nyi lwøg gen cinggi gen geki tyelli gen, kipe ge óthøw. Mïdhi gen ciggɑni, cɑg ɑnɑn gïti ɑtheer.  
> **Phonemes:** `ny i l w ø g g e n c i n g g i g e n g e k i t y e l l i g e n, k i p e g e ó th ø w. m ï dh i g e n c i g g ɑ n i, c ɑ g ɑ n ɑ n g ï t i ɑ th e e r.`  
> **IPA:** `ɲ i l w ø ɡ ɡ e n t͡ʃ i n ɡ ɡ i ɡ e n ɡ e k i t j e l l i ɡ e n, k i p e ɡ e o t̪ ø w. m i d̪ i ɡ e n t͡ʃ i ɡ ɡ ɑ n i, t͡ʃ ɑ ɡ ɑ n ɑ n ɡ i t i ɑ t̪ e e ɾ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Shona</b> (<code>sna</code> · BW)</summary>

> **Text:** Zvibereko zvevhu rako, nezvamabasa ako, zvichadyiwa norudzi rwausingazivi; uchangoremedzwa nokutambudzwa nguva dzose.  
> **Phonemes:** `zv i b e r e k o zv e vh u r a k o, n e zv a m a b a s a a k o, zv i ch a dy i w a n o r u dz i r w a u s i ng a z i v i; u ch a ng o r e m e dz w a n o k u t a mb u dz w a ng u v a dz o s e.`  
> **IPA:** `z̤ᵝ i ɓ e r e k o z̤ᵝ e v̤ u r a k o, n e z̤ᵝ a m a ɓ a s a a k o, z̤ᵝ i t͡ʃ a d̤ʲɡ i w a n o r u d͡z̤ i r w a u s i ŋ a z̤ i ʋ i; u t͡ʃ a ŋ o r e m e d͡z̤ w a n o k u t a ᵐb u d͡z̤ w a ŋ u ʋ a d͡z̤ o s e.`

> **Text:** Vakaverengwa mudzinza raJudha vose vaisvika makumi manomwe ezviuru ane zviuru zvina namazana matanhatu.  
> **Phonemes:** `v a k a v e r e ng w a m u dz i nz a r a j u dh a v o s e v a i sv i k a m a k u m i m a n o m w e e zv i u r u a n e zv i u r u zv i n a n a m a z a n a m a t a nh a t u.`  
> **IPA:** `ʋ a k a ʋ e r e ŋ w a m u d͡z̤ i ⁿz̤ a r a ʤ u d̤ a ʋ o s e ʋ a i sᶲ i k a m a k u m i m a n o m w e e z̤ᵝ i u r u a n e z̤ᵝ i u r u z̤ᵝ i n a n a m a z̤ a n a m a t a n̤ a t u.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Siwu</b> (<code>akp</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɣɛɛ fɔ ne, te ara wa nsia i kafɔkaɖe sɛɛ ɔri iso.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɣ ɛ ɛ f ɔ n e, t e a r a w a n s i a i k a f ɔ k a ɖ e s ɛ ɛ ɔ r i i s o.`  
> **IPA:** `ɣ ɛ ɛ f ɔ n e, t e a r a w a n s i a i k a f ɔ k a ɖ e s ɛ ɛ ɔ r i i s o.`

> **Text:** Ɔɖesɛrã ne, Yesu ɔsɛ i ndu iso ɔba ɔ̃atu ɔ̃ marasuãdze.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɔ ɖ e s ɛ r ã n e, y e s u ɔ s ɛ i n d u i s o ɔ b a ɔ̃ a t u ɔ̃ m a r a s u ã dz e.`  
> **IPA:** `ɔ ɖ e s ɛ r ã n e, j e s u ɔ s ɛ i n d u i s o ɔ b a ɔ̃ a t u ɔ̃ m a r a s u ã ɟ e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Soga</b> (<code>xog</code> · UG)</summary>

> **Text:** Yaakoba mule ati: Kino kyewuunhisa! Omuliro gwagema guti ekisaka, aye kyaloba kusiriira! Ka nseete mbone bukalamu.  
> **Phonemes:** `y aa k o b a m u l e a t i: k i n o ky e w uu nh i s a! o m u l i r o gw a g e m a g u t i e k i s a k a, a y e ky a l o b a k u s i r ii r a! k a n s ee t e mb o n e b u k a l a m u.`  
> **IPA:** `j aː k ɔ β a m u ɺ ɛ a t i: k i n ɔ c ɛ w uː ⁿh i s a! ɔ m u ɺ i ɾ ɔ ɡʷ a ɡ ɛ m a ɡ u t i ɛ k i s a k a, a j ɛ c a ɺ ɔ β a k u s i ɾ iː ɾ a! k a n s ɛː t ɛ ᵐb ɔ n ɛ β u k a ɺ a m u.`

> **Text:** Niiwe badhaadha baife ye baataamu obwesige, baakwesiga era waabawonia.  
> **Phonemes:** `n ii w e b a dh aa dh a b ai f e y e b aa t aa m u o bw e s i g e, b aa kw e s i g a e r a w aa b a w o n i a.`  
> **IPA:** `n iː w ɛ β a d̪ aː d̪ a β aɪ̯ f ɛ j ɛ β aː t aː m u ɔ βʷ ɛ s i ɡ ɛ, β aː kʷ ɛ s i ɡ a ɛ ɾ a w aː β a w ɔ n i a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Songe</b> (<code>sop</code> · CD)</summary>

> **Text:** Paulu mpika Yesu Kidisitu, baitanyinwe bu mutumibwa, mulamunwe ku mukāndu-wibuwa wEfile Mukulu.  
> **Phonemes:** `p a u l u mp i k a y e s u k i d i s i t u, b a i t a ny i n w e b u m u t u m i b w a, m u l a m u n w e k u m u k ā nd u-w i b u w a w e f i l e m u k u l u.`  
> **IPA:** `p a u l u ᵐp i k a j e s u k i d i s i t u, b a i t a ɲ i n w e b u m u t u m i b w a, m u l a m u n w e k u m u k a ⁿɗ u-w i b u w a w e f i l e m u k u l u.`

> **Text:** Kwi kishilatwe kulambula Kesala su?.  
> **Phonemes:** `k w i k i sh i l a t w e k u l a mb u l a k e s a l a s u?.`  
> **IPA:** `k w i k i ʃ i l a t w e k u l a ᵐb u l a k e s a l a s u?.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Sukuma</b> (<code>suk</code> · TZ)</summary>

> **Text:** Ū mu gatī jalīmo shiinū ja būlī mbika, ja magūlū ane, nī ja kwigwesa ha sī, na noni ja kūlala ja būlī mbika.  
> **Phonemes:** `ū m u g a t ī j a l ī m o s h ii n ū j a b ū l ī mb i k a, j a m a g ū l ū a n e, n ī j a kw i gw e s a h a s ī, n a n o n i j a k ū l a l a j a b ū l ī mb i k a.`  
> **IPA:** `[u] [m] [u] [ɡ] [a] [t] [i] [ɟ] [a] [l] [i] [m] [o~ɔ] [s] [h] [iː] [n] [u] [ɟ] [a] [b] [u] [l] [i] [ᵐb] [i] [k] [a], [ɟ] [a] [m] [a] [ɡ] [u] [l] [u] [a] [n] [e~ɛ], [n] [i] [ɟ] [a] [kʷ] [i] [ɡʷ] [e~ɛ] [s] [a] [h] [a] [s] [i], [n] [a] [n] [o~ɔ] [n] [i] [ɟ] [a] [k] [u] [l] [a] [l] [a] [ɟ] [a] [b] [u] [l] [i] [ᵐb] [i] [k] [a].`

> **Text:** Ī numba ī yose īkūbanījiwe chiza mulī wei; nīyo ū mulī wei īkongejaga kūkūla na kūbiza hekalu neela mulī Seeba.  
> **Phonemes:** `ī n u mb a ī y o s e ī k ū b a n ī j i w e c h i z a m u l ī w e i; n ī y o ū m u l ī w e i ī k o ng e j a g a k ū k ū l a n a k ū b i z a h e k a l u n ee l a m u l ī s ee b a.`  
> **IPA:** `[i] [n] [u] [ᵐb] [a] [i] [j] [o~ɔ] [s] [e~ɛ] [i] [k] [u] [b] [a] [n] [i] [ɟ] [i] [w] [e~ɛ] [c] [h] [i] [z] [a] [m] [u] [l] [i] [w] [e~ɛ] [i]; [n] [i] [j] [o~ɔ] [u] [m] [u] [l] [i] [w] [e~ɛ] [i] [i] [k] [o~ɔ] [ŋ] [e~ɛ] [ɟ] [a] [ɡ] [a] [k] [u] [k] [u] [l] [a] [n] [a] [k] [u] [b] [i] [z] [a] [h] [e~ɛ] [k] [a] [l] [u] [n] [eː~ɛː] [l] [a] [m] [u] [l] [i] [s] [eː~ɛː] [b] [a].`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Susu</b> (<code>sus</code> · GN)</summary>

> **Text:** «Marasɛniyɛnyi lɔxɔɛ, fure ɲaaxi kanyi xa siga sɛrɛxɛdubɛ xɔn.  
> **Phonemes:** `«m a r a s ɛn i y ɛn y i l ɔ x ɔ ɛ, f u r e ɲ aa x i k an y i x a s i g a s ɛ r ɛ x ɛ d u b ɛ x ɔn.`  
> **IPA:** `«m a r a s ɛ̃ i j ɛ̃ j i l ɔ ç ɔ ɛ, f u r e ɲ aː ç i k ã j i ç a s i ɡ a s ɛ r ɛ ç ɛ d u b ɛ ç ɔ̃.`

> **Text:** Na xanbi, sɛrɛxɛdubɛ singee nu wuya, barima e to faxa, mixi gbɛtɛe nu tima e ɲɔxɔɛ ra.  
> **Phonemes:** `n a x an b i, s ɛ r ɛ x ɛ d u b ɛ s in g ee n u w u y a, b a r i m a e t o f a x a, m i x i gb ɛ t ɛ e n u t i m a e ɲ ɔ x ɔ ɛ r a.`  
> **IPA:** `n a ç ã b i, s ɛ r ɛ ç ɛ d u b ɛ s ĩ ɡ eː n u w u j a, b a r i m a e t o f a ç a, m i ç i ɡ͡b ɛ t ɛ e n u t i m a e ɲ ɔ ç ɔ ɛ r a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Swahili</b> (<code>swh</code> · BI)</summary>

> **Text:** na safu ya nne ilikuwa ya zabarajadi, ya shohamu na yaspi; yote yalimiminiwa vijalizo vya dhahabu.  
> **Phonemes:** `n a s a f u y a n n e i l i k u w a y a z a b a r a j a d i, y a sh o h a m u n a y a s p i; y o t e y a l i m i m i n i w a v i j a l i z o v y a dh a h a b u.`  
> **IPA:** `n ɑ s ɑ f u j ɑ n n ɛ i l i k u w ɑ j ɑ z ɑ ɓ ɑ r ɑ ʄ ɑ ɗ i, j ɑ ʃ ɔ h ɑ m u n ɑ j ɑ s p i; j ɔ t ɛ j ɑ l i m i m i n i w ɑ v i ʄ ɑ l i z ɔ v j ɑ ð ɑ h ɑ ɓ u.`

> **Text:** na kijiko kimoja cha dhahabu, uzani wake shekeli kumi, kilichojaa uvumba;.  
> **Phonemes:** `n a k i j i k o k i m o j a ch a dh a h a b u, u z a n i w a k e sh e k e l i k u m i, k i l i ch o j a a u v u mb a;.`  
> **IPA:** `n ɑ k i ʄ i k ɔ k i m ɔ ʄ ɑ ʧ ɑ ð ɑ h ɑ ɓ u, u z ɑ n i w ɑ k ɛ ʃ ɛ k ɛ l i k u m i, k i l i ʧ ɔ ʄ ɑ ɑ u v u ᵐb ɑ;.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Swati</b> (<code>ssw</code> · LS)</summary>

> **Text:** Ungibeka emacala lamatima, ungetfwese emacala etono lengatenta ngisemusha.  
> **Phonemes:** `u ng i b e k a e m a c a l a l a m a t i m a, u ng e tf w e s e e m a c a l a e t o n o l e ng a t e n t a ng i s e m u sh a.`  
> **IPA:** `u ŋ i ɓ ɛ kʼ a ɛ m a ᵏǀ a l a l a m a tʼ i m a, u ŋ ɛ tf w ɛ s ɛ ɛ m a ᵏǀ a l a ɛ tʼ ɔ n ɔ l ɛ ŋ a tʼ ɛ n tʼ a ŋ i s ɛ m u ʃ a.`

> **Text:** Umuntfu lolidlabha emsebentini wakhe ufanana nemuntfu lobhubhisako.  
> **Phonemes:** `u m u n tf u l o l i dl a bh a e m s e b e n t i n i w a kh e u f a n a n a n e m u n tf u l o bh u bh i s a k o.`  
> **IPA:** `u m u n tf u l ɔ l i ɮ a bʱ a ɛ m s ɛ ɓ ɛ n tʼ i n i w a kʰ ɛ u f a n a n a n ɛ m u n tf u l ɔ bʱ u bʱ i s a kʼ ɔ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tamahaq</b> (<code>thv</code> · DZ)</summary>

> **Text:** beššan yeɣder ahi édét yebbeddel erreẓamin meraw iméren. Beššan Yaḷḷah wrt yoyyé éd hi yeǧ a yexlan.  
> **Phonemes:** `b e š š a n y e ɣ d e r a h i é d é t y e b b e d d e l e r r e ẓ a m i n m e r a w i m é r e n. b e š š a n y a ḷ ḷ a h w r t y o y y é é d h i y e ǧ a y e x l a n.`  
> **IPA:** `b e s s a n j e ɣ d e r a h i e d e t j e b b e d d e l e r r e zˤ a m i n m e r a x i m e r e n. b e s s a n j a lˤ lˤ a h x r t j o j j e e d h i j e ɡ a j e x l a n.`

> **Text:** beššan wrt osen. Aqqelen Yerušelem egmayen-t.  
> **Phonemes:** `b e š š a n w r t o s e n. a q q e l e n y e r u š e l e m e g m a y e n-t.`  
> **IPA:** `b e s s a n x r t o s e n. a q q e l e n j e r u s e l e m e ɡ m a j e n-t.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tamasheq</b> (<code>taq</code> · BF)</summary>

> **Text:** Izjăr Ɣisa ašăl-wen-dăɣ, ikkʼ asălim n-ejănš, ăqqima.  
> **Phonemes:** `i z j ă r ɣ i s a a š ă l-w e n-d ă ɣ, i k k ' a s ă l i m n-e j ă n š, ă q q i m a.`  
> **IPA:** `[i] [z] [d͡ʒ] [a] [r] [ɣ] [i] [s] [a] [a] [s] [a] [l]-[w] [e] [n]-[d] [a] [ɣ], [i] [k] [k] [ʔ] [a] [s] [a] [l] [i] [m] [n]-[e] [d͡ʒ] [a] [n] [s], [a] [q] [q] [i] [m] [a].`

> **Text:** Ăkfet ăhaləs hănne-s ălxaqq wa fălla-s la, tăkfet daɣ tamăḍt ălxaqq wa fălla-s ila ăhaləs-net.  
> **Phonemes:** `ă k f e t ă h a l ə s h ă n n e-s ă l x a q q w a f ă l l a-s l a, t ă k f e t d a ɣ t a m ă ḍ t ă l x a q q w a f ă l l a-s i l a ă h a l ə s-n e t.`  
> **IPA:** `[a] [k] [f] [e] [t] [a] [h] [a] [l] ə [s] [h] [a] [n] [n] [e]-[s] [a] [l] [x] [a] [q] [q] [w] [a] [f] [a] [l] [l] [a]-[s] [l] [a], [t] [a] [k] [f] [e] [t] [d] [a] [ɣ] [t] [a] [m] [a] [d] [t] [a] [l] [x] [a] [q] [q] [w] [a] [f] [a] [l] [l] [a]-[s] [i] [l] [a] [a] [h] [a] [l] ə [s]-[n] [e] [t].`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tangale</b> (<code>tan</code> · NG)</summary>

> **Text:** nẹ pọni, ka, Ka Yamb lokọ, na shid tọmgu, yan illi Epesho.  
> **Phonemes:** `n ẹ p ọ n i, k a, k a y a mb l o k ọ, n a sh i d t ọ m g u, y a n i l l i e p e sh o.`  
> **IPA:** `n e p o n i, k a, k a j a ᵐb l o k o, n a ç i d t o m ɡ u, j a n i l l i e p e ç o.`

> **Text:** mọnj sẹ mir ki wam dunji pip pọ di sọbọk bota ya som kwad lin Yamba.  
> **Phonemes:** `m ọ nj s ẹ m i r k i w a m d u nj i p i p p ọ d i s ọ b ọ k b o t a y a s o m kw a d l i n y a mb a.`  
> **IPA:** `m o ⁿʤ s e m i r k i w a m d u ⁿʤ i p i p p o d i s o b o k b o t a j a s o m kʷ a d l i n j a ᵐb a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tawallammat Tamajaq</b> (<code>ttq</code> · BF)</summary>

> **Text:** Awedan kul wa iran a di-išɣǝl, ilkǝmet-i, as iga adi edag wa ǝheɣ ǝnta a zʼiggǝz, akf-ay deɣ Abba-nin almaqam.  
> **Phonemes:** `a w e d a n k u l w a i r a n a d i-i š ɣ ǝ l, i l k ǝ m e t-i, a s i g a a d i e d a g w a ǝ h e ɣ ǝ n t a a z ' i g g ǝ z, a k f-a y d e ɣ a b b a-n i n a l m a q a m.`  
> **IPA:** `a w e d a n k u l w a i r a n a d i-i s ɣ ǝ l, i l k ǝ m e t-i, a s i ɡ a a d i e d a ɡ w a ǝ h e ɣ ǝ n t a a z ' i ɡ ɡ ǝ z, a k f-a j d e ɣ a b b a-n i n a l m a q a m.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tchaman</b> (<code>ebr</code> · CI)</summary>

> **Text:** Átɛsalé Lephan Mi bha éswa néphan alia.  
> **Phonemes:** `á t ɛ s a l é l e ph an m i bh a é s w a n é ph an a l i a.`  
> **IPA:** `a t ɛ s a l e l e pʰ ã m i ɓ a e s w a n e pʰ ã a l i a.`

> **Text:** Ánman do Izraɛ khúbhɛ́nɛ́n búdúbúdú sáji bha gɛ wo Hwánthe Yankan.  
> **Phonemes:** `á n m an d o i z r a ɛ kh ú bh ɛ́ n ɛ́ n b ú d ú b ú d ú s á j i bh a g ɛ w o h w á n th e y an k an.`  
> **IPA:** `a n m ã d o i z r a ɛ kʰ u ɓ ɛ n ɛ n b u d u b u d u s a ɟ i ɓ a ɡ ɛ w o x w a n tʰ e j ã k ã.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tem</b> (<code>kdh</code> · BJ)</summary>

> **Text:** Ńna‑ńna gɛ waanʊ́ŋ ɩkɛ́ɖɛɛ Yeésu-jɔ́ ɩtɔ́ yɩ sɩsɩ: «Mɛ́ɛ́zɛ́ɛ nya kʊ́bɔnɩ́.» Ngɛ weegbíríki Yeésu.  
> **Phonemes:** `ń n a‑ń n a g ɛ w a a n ʊ ŋ ɩ k ɛ́ ɖ ɛ ɛ y e é s u-j ɔ́ ɩ t ɔ́ y ɩ s ɩ s ɩ: «m ɛ́ ɛ́ z ɛ́ ɛ ny a k ʊ b ɔ n ɩ́.» n g ɛ w e e gb í r í k i y e é s u.`  
> **IPA:** `n n a‑n n a ɡ ɛ w a a n ʊ ŋ ɪ k ɛ ɖ ɛ ɛ j e e s u-ʤ ɔ ɪ t ɔ j ɪ s ɪ s ɪ: «m ɛ ɛ z ɛ ɛ ɲ a k ʊ b ɔ n ɪ.» n ɡ ɛ w e e ɡ͡b i r i k i j e e s u.`

> **Text:** Wúro Eróodi waanɩ́ɩ bɩlɛ́ nɛ́, ngɛ ɩlaakáarɩ waagʊrʊ́ ɩ́na Yerusalɛ́ɛm zamɔ́ɔ rɩ́ŋa.  
> **Phonemes:** `w ú r o e r ó o d i w a a n ɩ́ ɩ b ɩ l ɛ́ n ɛ́, n g ɛ ɩ l a a k á a r ɩ w a a g ʊ r ʊ ɩ́ n a y e r u s a l ɛ́ ɛ m z a m ɔ́ ɔ r ɩ́ ŋ a.`  
> **IPA:** `w u r o e r o o d i w a a n ɪ ɪ b ɪ l ɛ n ɛ, n ɡ ɛ ɪ l a a k a a r ɪ w a a ɡ ʊ r ʊ ɪ n a j e r u s a l ɛ ɛ m z a m ɔ ɔ r ɪ ŋ a.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tembo</b> (<code>tbt</code> · CD)</summary>

> **Text:** Yuta Isikariyota, muuma mwa banafunzi* ba Yesu ola wábaa ungámurenganya, era kwire áteta mbu:.  
> **Phonemes:** `y u t a i s i k a r i y o t a, m uu m a mw a b a n a f u nz i* b a y e s u o l a w á b aa u ng á m u r e ng a ny a, e r a kw i r e á t e t a mb u:.`  
> **IPA:** `j u t a i s i k a r i j o t a, m uː m a mʷ a b a n a f u ⁿz i* b a j e s u o l a w a b aː u ᵑɡ a m u r e ᵑɡ a ɲ a, e r a kʷ i r e a t e t a ᵐb u:.`

> **Text:** Kutali mundu ola wète nzii inene kurenza ola wêmerera kwâna ekalamo kai bushi na bera bai.  
> **Phonemes:** `k u t a l i m u nd u o l a w è t e nz ii i n e n e k u r e nz a o l a w ê m e r e r a kw â n a e k a l a m o k a i b u sh i n a b e r a b a i.`  
> **IPA:** `k u t a l i m u ⁿd u o l a w e t e ⁿz iː i n e n e k u r e ⁿz a o l a w e m e r e r a kʷ a n a e k a l a m o k a i b u ʃ i n a b e r a b a i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Themne</b> (<code>tem</code> · GN)</summary>

> **Text:** Ɔwa Mosisȧŋ ɔ yamari su ka ȧŋthɔ, kəpa wuni wunɔŋ a ba tə kədif kɔ məsar: munɔŋ-a to mə pa-e?.  
> **Phonemes:** `ɔ w a m o s i s ȧ ŋ ɔ y a m a r i s u k a ȧ ŋ th ɔ, k ə p a w u n i w u n ɔ ŋ a b a t ə k ə d i f k ɔ m ə s a r: m u n ɔ ŋ-a t o m ə p a-e?.`  
> **IPA:** `ɔ w a m o s i s a ŋ ɔ j a m a r i s u k a a ŋ t̪ ɔ, k ə p a w u n i w u n ɔ ŋ a b a t ə k ə d i f k ɔ m ə s a r: m u n ɔ ŋ-a t o m ə p a-e?.`

> **Text:** kȧmȧ wuni o wuni ɔ te gbəli way thalɔm thila thambe kɔn ɔwe ba aŋlomar, thalɔm ȧŋes ŋa ɔsɛm, thalɔm aŋyirka ŋa ȧŋes ŋɔŋ.  
> **Phonemes:** `k ȧ m ȧ w u n i o w u n i ɔ t e gb ə l i w a y th a l ɔ m th i l a th a m b e k ɔ n ɔ w e b a a ŋ l o m a r, th a l ɔ m ȧ ŋ e s ŋ a ɔ s ɛ m, th a l ɔ m a ŋ y i r k a ŋ a ȧ ŋ e s ŋ ɔ ŋ.`  
> **IPA:** `k a m a w u n i o w u n i ɔ t e ɡ͡b ə l i w a j t̪ a l ɔ m t̪ i l a t̪ a m b e k ɔ n ɔ w e b a a ŋ l o m a r, t̪ a l ɔ m a ŋ e s ŋ a ɔ s ɛ m, t̪ a l ɔ m a ŋ j i r k a ŋ a a ŋ e s ŋ ɔ ŋ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tigrinya</b> (<code>tir</code> · DJ)</summary>

> **Text:** ምስላታትኩም ምስላ ሓመድ እዮም፤ ዕርድታትኩምውን ዕርድታት ጭቃ እዮም።.  
> **Phonemes:** `ም ስ ላ ታ ት ኩ ም ም ስ ላ ሓ መ ድ እ ዮ ም፤ ዕ ር ድ ታ ት ኩ ም ው ን ዕ ር ድ ታ ት ጭ ቃ እ ዮ ም።.`  
> **IPA:** `mɨ sɨ la ta tɨ ku mɨ mɨ sɨ la ħa mɐ dɨ ʔɨ jo mɨ፤ ʕɨ rɨ dɨ ta tɨ ku mɨ wɨ nɨ ʕɨ rɨ dɨ ta tɨ tʃʼɨ q’a ʔɨ jo mɨ።.`

> **Text:** ማቲትያን ኤልፍሌሁን ሚቅኔያን ዖቤድኤዶምን ይዒኤልን ዓዛዝያን ድማ ሸሞንተ ኣውታር ብዘለዎ በገና ይቓንዩ ነበሩ።.  
> **Phonemes:** `ማ ቲ ት ያ ን ኤ ል ፍ ሌ ሁ ን ሚ ቅ ኔ ያ ን ዖ ቤ ድ ኤ ዶ ም ን ይ ዒ ኤ ል ን ዓ ዛ ዝ ያ ን ድ ማ ሸ ሞ ን ተ ኣ ው ታ ር ብ ዘ ለ ዎ በ ገ ና ይ ቓ ን ዩ ነ በ ሩ።.`  
> **IPA:** `ma ti tɨ ja nɨ ʔe lɨ fɨ le hu nɨ mi q’ɨ ne ja nɨ ʕo be dɨ ʔe do mɨ nɨ jɨ ʕi ʔe lɨ nɨ ʕa za zɨ ja nɨ dɨ ma ʃɐ mo nɨ tɐ ʔa wɨ ta rɨ bɨ zɐ lɐ wo bɐ ɡɐ na jɨ qʰa nɨ ju nɐ bɐ ru።.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tonga</b> (<code>toh</code> · MZ)</summary>

> **Text:** Wona valala vangu va engedzelanago gutala, va gu nyi venga ngudzu khu tshivba.  
> **Phonemes:** `w o n a v a l a l a v a ng u v a e ng e d z e l a n a g o g u t a l a, v a g u ny i v e ng a ng u d z u k h u t s h i v b a.`  
> **IPA:** `w o n a v a l a l a v a ᵑɡ u v a e ᵑɡ e d z e l a n a ɡ o ɡ u t a l a, v a ɡ u ɲ i v e ᵑɡ a ᵑɡ u d z u k h u t s h i v β a.`

> **Text:** Dzithomba kha dzi gu phasi khu litshigu nya likhulo la Nungungulu, aholu gululama gu ngu guvbulugisa lufuni.  
> **Phonemes:** `d z i t h o mb a k h a d z i g u p h a s i k h u l i t s h i g u ny a l i k h u l o l a n u ng u ng u l u, a h o l u g u l u l a m a g u ng u g u v b u l u g i s a l u f u n i.`  
> **IPA:** `d z i t h o ᵐb a k h a d z i ɡ u p h a s i k h u l i t s h i ɡ u ɲ a l i k h u l o l a n u ᵑɡ u ᵑɡ u l u, a h o l u ɡ u l u l a m a ɡ u ᵑɡ u ɡ u v β u l u ɡ i s a l u f u n i.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tsonga</b> (<code>tso</code> · MZ)</summary>

> **Text:** ni xinkuzana, ni khuna, ni xinyimpfana xa lembe, swi va swa holokosta;.  
> **Phonemes:** `n i x i n k u z a n a, n i kh u n a, n i x i ny i m pf a n a x a l e m b e, sw i v a sw a h o l o k o s t a;.`  
> **IPA:** `n i ʃ i n k u z a n a, n i kʰ u n a, n i ʃ i ɲ i m p̪͡f a n a ʃ a l ɛ m b ɛ, sʷ i β a sʷ a h ɔ l ɔ k ɔ s t a;.`

> **Text:** kambe la hlambanyaka hi tempele u hlambanya ha yona ni loyi a akeke eka yona;.  
> **Phonemes:** `k a m b e l a hl a m b a ny a k a h i t e m p e l e u hl a m b a ny a h a y o n a n i l o y i a a k e k e e k a y o n a;.`  
> **IPA:** `k a m b ɛ l a ɬ a m b a ɲ a k a h i t ɛ m p ɛ l ɛ u ɬ a m b a ɲ a h a j ɔ n a n i l ɔ j i a a k ɛ k ɛ ɛ k a j ɔ n a;.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tswa</b> (<code>tsc</code> · MZ)</summary>

> **Text:** Zinghaho ni tindlela ta vontlhe lava va rivalako Nungungulu; Ni kutsumba ka mukhohlisi ku ta gumelwa.  
> **Phonemes:** `z i n g h a h o n i t i n dl e l a t a v o n tlh e l a v a v a r i v a l a k o n u n g u n g u l u; n i k u ts u m b a k a m u kh o hl i s i k u t a g u m e l w a.`  
> **IPA:** `z i n ɡ h a h ɔ n i t i n ɮ e l a t a v ɔ n t͡ɬʰ e l a v a v a r i v a l a k ɔ n u n ɡ u n ɡ u l u; n i k u t͡sʼ u m b a k a m u kʰ ɔ ɬ i s i k u t a ɡ u m e l w a.`

> **Text:** Ni loku ku mbhelile misisi laha ximombyeni, lihandla la laha ximombyeni; i hlazekile.  
> **Phonemes:** `n i l o k u k u m b h e l i l e m i s i s i l a h a x i m o m b y e n i, l i h a n dl a l a l a h a x i m o m b y e n i; i hl a z e k i l e.`  
> **IPA:** `n i l ɔ k u k u m b h e l i l e m i s i s i l a h a ʃ i m ɔ m b j e n i, l i h a n ɮ a l a l a h a ʃ i m ɔ m b j e n i; i ɬ a z e k i l e.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tumbuka</b> (<code>tum</code> · MW)</summary>

> **Text:** Viteŵetero vyose vya mlimo wa mu Chihema, vikhomo vyakhe, na vya baraza, vyose vikaŵe vya mkuŵa.  
> **Phonemes:** `v i t e ŵ e t e r o vy o s e vy a m l i m o w a m u ch i h e m a, v i kh o m o vy a kh e, n a vy a b a r a z a, vy o s e v i k a ŵ e vy a m k u ŵ a.`  
> **IPA:** `v i t ɛ w ɛ t ɛ ɽ ɔ tʲ ɔ s ɛ tʲ a m ɽ i m ɔ w a m u t͡ʃʰ i h ɛ m a, v i kʰ ɔ m ɔ tʲ a kʰ ɛ, n a tʲ a ɓ a ɽ a z a, tʲ ɔ s ɛ v i k a w ɛ tʲ a m k u w a.`

> **Text:** Ukapereke mwana-mberere yumoza na mulenje ndipo mwana-mberere munyakhe ukapereke na mise.  
> **Phonemes:** `u k a p e r e k e mw a n a-mb e r e r e y u m o z a n a m u l e nj e nd i p o mw a n a-mb e r e r e m u ny a kh e u k a p e r e k e n a m i s e.`  
> **IPA:** `u k a p ɛ ɽ ɛ k ɛ mʷ a n a-ᵐb ɛ ɽ ɛ ɽ ɛ j u m ɔ z a n a m u ɽ ɛ ⁿd͡ʒ ɛ ⁿd i p ɔ mʷ a n a-ᵐb ɛ ɽ ɛ ɽ ɛ m u nʲ a kʰ ɛ u k a p ɛ ɽ ɛ k ɛ n a m i s ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Tupuri</b> (<code>tui</code> · CM)</summary>

> **Text:** Á poɗ wo me ti de el de ɓlagge, Tenen ɓin go de gwaʼge de see ɓe tawa.  
> **Phonemes:** `á p o ɗ w o m e t i d e e l d e ɓ l a g g e, t e n e n ɓ i n g o d e g w a ' g e d e s ee ɓ e t a w a.`  
> **IPA:** `a p o ɗ w o m e t i d e e l d e ɓ l a ɡ ɡ e, t e n e n ɓ i n ɡ o d e ɡ w a ʔ ɡ e d e s eː ɓ e t a w a.`

> **Text:** Mokay diñ ëu weere maga a biñ ra Jerusalem mono: AÀ wo diñ Samuwa, Sobab, Natañ, Salomoñ.  
> **Phonemes:** `m o k a y d i ñ ë u w ee r e m a g a a b i ñ r a j e r u s a l e m m o n o: aà w o d i ñ s a m u w a, s o b a b, n a t a ñ, s a l o m o ñ.`  
> **IPA:** `m o k a j d i ñ e u w eː r e m a ɡ a a b i ñ r a d͡ʒ e r u s a l e m m o n o: aː w o d i ñ s a m u w a, s o b a b, n a t a ñ, s a l o m o ñ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Twi</b> (<code>twi</code> · GH)</summary>

> **Text:** Wo, Awurade na mefrɛɛ wo; wo hɔ na misu mesrɛɛ mmɔborɔhunu se:.  
> **Phonemes:** `w o, a w u r a d e n a m e f r ɛ ɛ w o; w o h ɔ n a m i s u m e s r ɛ ɛ m m ɔ b o r ɔ h u nu s e:.`  
> **IPA:** `w o, æ w u ɾ æ d e n æ m e f ɾ ɛ ɛ w o; w o h ɔ n æ m i s u m e s ɾ ɛ ɛ m m ɔ b o ɾ ɔ h u ɲʷĩ s e:.`

> **Text:** Enti Mose ne Aaron ne Israel mpanyimfo nam Lewifo no mmusua ne wɔn afi so kan wɔn nyinaa.  
> **Phonemes:** `e n ti m o s e n e a a r o n n e i s r a e l m p a nyi m f o n a m l e wi f o n o m m u s u a n e w ɔ n a f i s o k a n w ɔ n nyi n a a.`  
> **IPA:** `e n ti m o s e n e æ æ ɾ o n n e i s ɾ æ e l m pʰ æ ɲːĩ m f o n æ m l e ɥi f o n o m m u s u æ n e w ɔ n æ f i s o kʰ æ n w ɔ n ɲːĩ n æ æ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Uduk</b> (<code>udu</code> · ET)</summary>

> **Text:** Yahuuda/ warkid ki mom piti jin ḵogu mo, dhala Israyiil adi ta mo jin tanu tap̱a ki sule/ sule/ mo.  
> **Phonemes:** `y a h u u d a/ w a r k i d k i m o m p i t i j i n ḵ o g u m o, dh a l a i s r a y i i l a d i t a m o j i n t a n u t a p̱ a k i s u l e/ s u l e/ m o.`  
> **IPA:** `j a h u u d a/ w a r k i d k i m o m p i t i ɟ i n k o ɡ u m o, d̪ a l a i s r a j i i l a d i t a m o ɟ i n t a n u t a p a k i s u l e/ s u l e/ m o.`

> **Text:** Tonṯe/ jin min ma Ashiir ishwa mo tani uni mini ṯawusaṯawus mo dhali adi midi dhoth ton ṯawusga/ ma maliḵ mo.  
> **Phonemes:** `t o n ṯ e/ j i n m i n m a a sh i i r i sh w a m o t a n i u n i m i n i ṯ a w u s a ṯ a w u s m o dh a l i a d i m i d i dh o th t o n ṯ a w u s g a/ m a m a l i ḵ m o.`  
> **IPA:** `t o n t e/ ɟ i n m i n m a a ʃ i i r i ʃ w a m o t a n i u n i m i n i t a w u s a t a w u s m o d̪ a l i a d i m i d i d̪ o t̪ t o n t a w u s ɡ a/ m a m a l i k m o.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Urhobo</b> (<code>urh</code> · NG)</summary>

> **Text:** Ọke rẹ Ananayas vwo nyo eta nana nu, o de she teye otọ o de ghwu. Oshọ rode na de mu ihwo na ejobi ri nyoro.  
> **Phonemes:** `ọ k e r ẹ an an a y a s vw o ny o e t a n an a n u, o d e sh e t e y e o t ọ o d e ghw u. o sh ọ r o d e n a d e m u i hw o n a e j o b i r i ny o r o.`  
> **IPA:** `o k e r e ã ã a j a s β̞̃ o ɲ o e t a n ã a n u, o d e ʃ e t e j e o t o o d e ɣ̞ u. o ʃ o r o d e n a d e m u i x̞ o n a e j o b i r i ɲ o r o.`

> **Text:** Ọ da rhe wan oma rẹ ọtiọye na yovwi nọ emakashe na kidie odẹ ro rere na yovwi nọ ọ rayen.  
> **Phonemes:** `ọ d a rh e w an o m a r ẹ ọ t i ọ y e n a y o vw i n ọ e m a k a sh e n a k i d i e o d ẹ r o r e r e n a y o vw i n ọ ọ r a y en.`  
> **IPA:** `o d a r̥ e w ã o m a r e o t i o j e n a j o β̞̃ i n o e m a k a ʃ e n a k i d i e o d e r o r e r e n a j o β̞̃ i n o o r a j ẽ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Vagala</b> (<code>vag</code> · Ghana)</summary>

> **Text:** Yeesu hiŋra la n du Jɛrusalɛm la nuu guŋ di hum Banabas u la Antiɔk.  
> **Phonemes:** `y e e s u h i ŋ r a l a n d u j ɛ r u s a l ɛ m l a n u u g u ŋ d i h u m b a n a b a s u l a a n t i ɔ k.`  
> **IPA:** `j ɪ ɪ s u h i ŋ r a l a n d u d͡ʒ ɛ r u s a l ɛ m l a n u u ɡ u ŋ d i h u m b a n a b a s u l a a n t i ɔ k.`

> **Text:** N ŋmɛnɛ faa zum biinikpola hiŋ bɛl n waa nɛra ni ra suŋ, ka u sogaa faa du wer lugɔ daa n chigɛ hɛrnanii di lɔɔ nɛnnɛ.  
> **Phonemes:** `n ŋm ɛ n ɛ f a a z u m b i i n i kp o l a h i ŋ b ɛ l n w a a n ɛ r a n i r a s u ŋ, k a u s o g a a f a a d u w e r l u g ɔ d a a n ch i g ɛ h ɛ r n a n i i d i l ɔ ɔ n ɛ n n ɛ.`  
> **IPA:** `n ŋ͡m ɛ n ɛ f a a z u m b i i n i k͡p o l a h i ŋ b ɛ l n w a a n ɛ r a n i r a s u ŋ, k a u s o ɡ a a f a a d u w ɪ r l u ɡ ɔ d a a n t͡ʃ i ɡ ɛ h ɛ r n a n i i d i l ɔ ɔ n ɛ n n ɛ.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Vai</b> (<code>vai</code> · LR)</summary>

> **Text:** ꕮꔧꕪꕌ ꕞꕌ ꖴꗷꗡ ꕞꕌ ꕍꕆ ꗏ ꔇꔀꔒ ꕒ. ꕉꖷ ꕉ ꗪ ꔨ ꖴꘋꗒ ꕃꔤ ꕮꕯ ꖬꕎ ꘃ ꘈꗢ ꖝ ꗏꗡ ꖷ ꔨ ꖏ ꖷ ꕞ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ꕮ ꔧ ꕪ ꕌ ꕞ ꕌ ꖴ ꗷ ꗡ ꕞ ꕌ ꕍ ꕆ ꗏ ꔇ ꔀ ꔒ ꕒ. ꕉ ꖷ ꕉ ꗪ ꔨ ꖴ ꘋ ꗒ ꕃ ꔤ ꕮ ꕯ ꖬ ꕎ ꘃ ꘈ ꗢ ꖝ ꗏ ꗡ ꖷ ꔨ ꖏ ꖷ ꕞ.`  
> **IPA:** `mã ĩ ka ha la ha ku lɛ ɛ la ha hã mĩ lɔ ɓe e le ɓa. a mũ a ɓɛ wi ku ꘋ ⁿɗɔ ki i mã nã su wa kɛ mɛ̃ ɛ̃ ɓu lɔ ɛ mũ wi ko mũ la.`

> **Text:** ꘋ ꗪ ꗋꖺꕒꕌ ꗉ ꕴ ꔝ, ꕴꔤ ꘀꔤ ꔷ ꘃꕩ ꕉꖷ ꕴꔤ ꖴꗷ ꗃ ꕉ ꕮ ꔷ ꘃ ꕞ. ꘃ, ꖦꖕꕰꕊ ꕘꕞ ꕢꔒꔤ ꔳꕩ. ꕴꔤ ꔳꕩ ꕃꕇ ꕃꕇ ꔻꔤ ꗏ. ꘃ, ꕴꕎꕌ ꕃꕇ ꕃꕇ ꔻꔤꘂ ꗏꔤ ꕘꕞꘋꕡ ꕘꕞ ꕢ ꕞ.  
> **Phonemes:** `ꘋ ꗪ ꗋ ꖺ ꕒ ꕌ ꗉ ꕴ ꔝ, ꕴ ꔤ ꘀ ꔤ ꔷ ꘃ ꕩ ꕉ ꖷ ꕴ ꔤ ꖴ ꗷ ꗃ ꕉ ꕮ ꔷ ꘃ ꕞ. ꘃ, ꖦ ꖕ ꕰ ꕊ ꕘ ꕞ ꕢ ꔒ ꔤ ꔳ ꕩ. ꕴ ꔤ ꔳ ꕩ ꕃ ꕇ ꕃ ꕇ ꔻ ꔤ ꗏ. ꘃ, ꕴ ꕎ ꕌ ꕃ ꕇ ꕃ ꕇ ꔻ ꔤ ꘂ ꗏ ꔤ ꕘ ꕞ ꘋ ꕡ ꕘ ꕞ ꕢ ꕞ.`  
> **IPA:** `ꘋ ɓɛ tɔ ɔ ɓa ha fɔ wo je, wo i d͡ʒɛ i li kɛ ja a mũ wo i ku lɛ ɓɔ a mã li kɛ la. kɛ, du u ɲã ã fa la sa le i ti ja. wo i ti ja ki nĩ ki nĩ si i lɔ. kɛ, wo wa ha ki nĩ ki nĩ si i jɛ lɔ i fa la ꘋ ⁿɗa fa la sa la.`

</details>

<details>
<summary><b>Venda</b> (<code>ven</code> · BW)</summary>

> **Text:** U itani fhano? U ḓiita nnyi u tshi vhuya wa ḓikorela tshalo fhano, u tshi kora tshalo nṱha-nṱha luwani wa nga u khosi?.  
> **Phonemes:** `u i t a n i fh a n o? u ḓ i i t a n ny i u tsh i vh u y a w a ḓ i k o r e l a tsh a l o fh a n o, u tsh i k o r a tsh a l o n ṱ h a-n ṱ h a l u w a n i w a ng a u kh o s i?.`  
> **IPA:** `u i tʼ a n i ɸ a n ɔ? u d i i tʼ a n ɲ i u t͡ʃʼ i β u j a w a d i kʼ ɔ ɾ ɛ ɺ a t͡ʃʼ a ɺ ɔ ɸ a n ɔ, u t͡ʃʼ i kʼ ɔ ɾ a t͡ʃʼ a ɺ ɔ n tʼ ɦ a-n tʼ ɦ a ɺ u w a n i w a ŋɡ a u kʰ ɔ s i?.`
